// 社福聯盟進銷存系統_GAS_v116_market_sync_stability_fix
/**
 * 社福聯盟｜進銷存（後台專用）API｜Google Apps Script
 * ---------------------------------------------------------
 * ✅ JSONP callback 支援（前端 callGAS）
 * ✅ 後台：商品 / 供應商 / 進貨 / 銷貨 / 庫存流水
 * ✅ 所有新增/修改/刪除都「必須寫入 Google Sheet」(前端不再做離線本地落地)
 *
 * 需要工作表（建議表頭如下）
 * - Products：id, name, price, image, stock, category, date, cost, unit, safety_stock, reference_price
 * - suppliers：id, name, phone, email, address, note, created_at
 * - purchases：po_id, date, supplier_id, supplier_name, total, items, status, operator, created_at
 * - Orders：order_id, name, phone, address, total, items, date, member_id, status, operator
 * - orderItems（可選）：OrderItemID, OrderID, ProductID, Quantity, UnitPrice, Subtotal
 * - stock_ledger（或 inventoryLog）：ledger_id, product_id, product_name, change, direction, reason, ref_id, operator, date
 * - Members：id, name, username, password, created_at, role
 */

function doGet(e) {
  const p = (e && e.parameter) ? e.parameter : {};
  const type = String(p.type || "").trim();
  try { rememberPrimarySpreadsheet_(SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet()); } catch (e) {}
  if (["syncReferencePrices", "marketPriceBoard", "marketSyncStatus"].indexOf(type) >= 0) {
    try { heartbeatDailyMarketTrigger_(); } catch (e) {}
  }

  const callback = String(p.callback || "").trim();
  const jsonpOk = callback && /^[A-Za-z_$][0-9A-Za-z_$]*(\.[0-9A-Za-z_$]+)*$/.test(callback);

  const respond = (obj) => {
    const json = JSON.stringify(obj);
    const body = jsonpOk ? `${callback}(${json})` : json;
    const out = ContentService.createTextOutput(body);
    return out.setMimeType(jsonpOk ? ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT : ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
  };

  try {
    if (type === "products") return apiProducts_(respond);
    if (type === "manageProduct") return apiManageProduct_(p, respond);

    if (type === "members") return apiMembersLogin_(p, respond);

    // 客戶（電商）登入
    if (type === "customerLogin") return apiCustomerLogin_(p, respond);

    // 客戶（電商）註冊（寫入 customers）
    if (type === "register") return apiCustomerRegister_(p, respond);

    // 後台使用者（Members）管理
    if (type === "manageMember") return apiManageMember_(p, respond);

    // 前台（商城）下單/查單
    if (type === "order") return apiOrderCreate_(p, respond);
    if (type === "orderStatusByToken") return apiOrderStatusByToken_(p, respond);
    if (type === "myOrders") return apiMyOrders_(p, respond);
    if (type === "getOrder") return apiGetOrder_(p, respond);

    if (type === "suppliers") return apiSuppliers_(respond);
    if (type === "manageSupplier") return apiManageSupplier_(p, respond);

    if (type === "customers") return apiCustomers_(respond);
    if (type === "manageCustomer") return apiManageCustomer_(p, respond);

    if (type === "purchases") return apiPurchases_(p, respond);
    if (type === "managePurchase") return apiManagePurchase_(p, respond);
    if (type === "purchaseReceiptTemplate") return respond({ status: "error", message: "purchaseReceiptTemplate 已停用，請改用前端 Excel 模板套印流程" });

    if (type === "pickups") return apiPickups_(respond);
    if (type === "managePickup") return apiManagePickup_(p, respond);

    if (type === "orders") return apiOrders_(respond);
    if (type === "manageOrder") return apiManageOrder_(p, respond);

    if (type === "manageSale") return apiManageSale_(p, respond);

    if (type === "stockLedger") return apiStockLedger_(respond);
    if (type === "productLedger") return apiProductLedger_(p, respond);
    if (type === "productMarketHistory") return apiProductMarketHistory_(p, respond);
    if (type === "stockAdjust") return apiStockAdjust_(p, respond);

    if (type === "syncReferencePrices") return apiSyncReferencePrices_(respond);
    if (type === "marketPriceBoard") return apiMarketPriceBoard_(respond);
    if (type === "marketSyncStatus") return apiMarketSyncStatus_(respond);
    if (type === "weeklyMenu") return apiWeeklyMenu_(p, respond);
    if (type === "menuDashboard") return apiMenuDashboard_(p, respond);
    if (type === "menuMappingConfig") return apiMenuMappingConfig_(p, respond);
    if (type === "manageMenuMapping") return apiManageMenuMapping_(p, respond);

    if (type === "systemSettings") return apiSystemSettings_(respond);
    if (type === "manageSystemSettings") return apiManageSystemSettings_(p, respond);

    if (type === "lastUpdate") return apiLastUpdate_(respond);

    return respond({ status: "error", message: "Invalid Request" });
  } catch (err) {
    return respond({ status: "error", message: String(err && err.message ? err.message : err) });
  }
}

// ----------------- Helpers -----------------
const PRIMARY_SPREADSHEET_ID_PROPERTY_ = 'PRIMARY_SPREADSHEET_ID';
const DAILY_MARKET_TRIGGER_LAST_CHECK_PROPERTY_ = 'DAILY_MARKET_TRIGGER_LAST_CHECK_DATE';
const DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_SUCCESS_PROPERTY_ = 'DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_SUCCESS';
const DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_ERROR_PROPERTY_ = 'DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_ERROR';
const DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_RESULT_PROPERTY_ = 'DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_RESULT';
const DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_DEBUG_PROPERTY_ = 'DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_DEBUG';
const MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_UNTIL_PROPERTY_ = 'DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_UNTIL';
const MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_TOKEN_PROPERTY_ = 'DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_TOKEN';

function rememberPrimarySpreadsheet_(ss) {
  try {
    if (ss && typeof ss.getId === 'function') {
      const id = String(ss.getId());
      const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
      if (String(props.getProperty(PRIMARY_SPREADSHEET_ID_PROPERTY_) || '') !== id) {
        props.setProperty(PRIMARY_SPREADSHEET_ID_PROPERTY_, id);
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return ss;
}

function getPrimarySpreadsheet_() {
  try {
    const active = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    if (active) return rememberPrimarySpreadsheet_(active);
  } catch (e) {}

  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const savedId = String(props.getProperty(PRIMARY_SPREADSHEET_ID_PROPERTY_) || '').trim();
  if (savedId) {
    try {
      return SpreadsheetApp.openById(savedId);
    } catch (e) {
      throw new Error(`自動同步找不到已綁定的試算表（Spreadsheet ID: ${savedId}）。請先從綁定的 Google Sheet 開啟 Apps Script 並手動執行一次，或點一次「同步最新參考行情」重新綁定。`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error('自動同步目前找不到綁定的試算表。請先從此專案綁定的 Google Sheet 開啟 Apps Script，或手動點一次「同步最新參考行情」讓系統記住 Spreadsheet ID。');
}

function heartbeatDailyMarketTrigger_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Taipei', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  if (String(props.getProperty(DAILY_MARKET_TRIGGER_LAST_CHECK_PROPERTY_) || '') === today) return;
  props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_TRIGGER_LAST_CHECK_PROPERTY_, today);
  try { ensureDailyNoonMarketPriceTrigger_(); } catch (e) {}
}

function findSheetByTrimName_(ss, name){
  const target = String(name || "").trim();
  const sheets = ss.getSheets();
  for (let i = 0; i < sheets.length; i++){
    const n = String(sheets[i].getName() || "").trim();
    if (n === target) return sheets[i];
  }
  return null;
}

function getSheetByAnyName_(ss, names) {
  for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(names[i]);
    if (sh) return sh;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(String(names[i]).trim());
    if (sh) return sh;
  }
  return null;
}

function ensureSheet_(ss, name, headers) {
  let sh = ss.getSheetByName(name) || findSheetByTrimName_(ss, name);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(name);
  } else {
    // 若是被空白或大小寫造成的名字差異，嘗試統一名稱
    if (sh.getName() !== name) {
      try { sh.setName(name); } catch(e) {}
    }
  }
  const lastCol = Math.max(sh.getLastColumn(), headers.length);
  const row1 = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0];
  const hasAny = row1.some(v => String(v || "").trim() !== "");
  if (!hasAny) {
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
  } else {
    // Fill missing header cells in order (do not overwrite existing)
    for (let i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
      if (!row1[i] || String(row1[i]).trim() === "") sh.getRange(1, i + 1).setValue(headers[i]);
    }
  }
  return sh;
}

function getHeaders_(sheet) {
  const lastCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn() || 1, 1);
  return sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
}


function normalizeShopEnabled_(v, defaultEnabled) {
  const raw = String(v == null ? "" : v).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!raw) return defaultEnabled === false ? "0" : "1";
  return (["0","false","no","off","n","hide","hidden"].includes(raw)) ? "0" : "1";
}

function headerIndexMap_(headers) {
  const m = {};
  headers.forEach((h, i) => { if (h) m[h] = i; });
  return m;
}

function readObjects_(sheet) {
  const values = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (values.length <= 1) return [];
  const headers = values[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const out = [];
  for (let r = 1; r < values.length; r++) {
    const row = values[r];
    if (!row || row.every(v => v === "" || v === null)) continue;
    const obj = {};
    for (let c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
      const key = headers[c];
      if (!key) continue;
      obj[key] = row[c];
    }
    out.push(obj);
  }
  return out;
}

function readObjectsWithRowNo_(sheet) {
  const values = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (values.length <= 1) return [];
  const headers = values[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const out = [];
  for (let r = 1; r < values.length; r++) {
    const row = values[r];
    if (!row || row.every(v => v === "" || v === null)) continue;
    const obj = { __rowNo: r + 1 };
    for (let c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
      const key = headers[c];
      if (!key) continue;
      obj[key] = row[c];
    }
    out.push(obj);
  }
  return out;
}

function appendObject_(sheet, obj) {
  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const row = new Array(headers.length).fill("");
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    if (!h) return;
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, h)) row[i] = obj[h];
  });
  sheet.appendRow(row);
}

function appendObjectsBatch_(sheet, objs) {
  const rows = Array.isArray(objs) ? objs : [];
  if (!rows.length) return;
  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const values = rows.map(obj => headers.map(h => (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj || {}, h) ? obj[h] : "")));
  sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, values.length, headers.length).setValues(values);
}

function toROCDateString_(d) {
  if (!(d instanceof Date)) return String(d || "").trim();
  const y = d.getFullYear() - 1911;
  const m = ("0" + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2);
  const day = ("0" + d.getDate()).slice(-2);
  return `${y}.${m}.${day}`;
}

function toISODateLocal_(d){
  if (!d) return "";
  try{
    // Date object
    if (Object.prototype.toString.call(d) === "[object Date]" && !isNaN(d.getTime())) {
      return Utilities.formatDate(d, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyy-MM-dd");
    }
    const s = String(d).trim();
    if (!s) return "";
    // ISO with time (Z) -> parse then format local
    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(s)) {
      const dt = new Date(s);
      if (!isNaN(dt.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(dt, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyy-MM-dd");
      return s.slice(0,10);
    }
    // ISO date
    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s)) return s.slice(0,10);
    // ROC YYY.MM.DD / etc
    if (/^\d{2,3}[\.\/\-]\d{1,2}[\.\/\-]\d{1,2}/.test(s) && !/^\d{4}[\.\/\-]/.test(s)) {
      const base = s.split(/[\sT]/)[0];
      const parts = base.split(/[\.\/\-]/);
      const y = (parseInt(parts[0],10) + 1911);
      const m = ("0"+parts[1]).slice(-2);
      const dd = ("0"+parts[2]).slice(-2);
      return `${y}-${m}-${dd}`;
    }
    // Fallback Date parse
    const dt2 = new Date(s);
    if (!isNaN(dt2.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(dt2, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyy-MM-dd");
    return "";
  } catch(e){
    return "";
  }
}


function decimalPlacesText_(v){
  const s = String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/,/g, "").trim();
  const m = s.match(/\.(\d+)$/);
  return m ? m[1].length : 0;
}

function cleanDecimalInput_(v, maxScale){
  maxScale = (maxScale === undefined) ? 6 : Number(maxScale || 0);
  const s = String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/,/g, "").trim();
  if (!s) return 0;
  const n = Number(s);
  if (!isFinite(n)) return 0;
  const scale = Math.min(decimalPlacesText_(s), maxScale);
  return truncateDecimalNumber_(n, scale);
}

function truncateDecimalNumber_(v, digits){
  const n = Number(v);
  if (!isFinite(n)) return 0;
  const scale = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(digits || 0)));
  const factor = Math.pow(10, scale);
  return (n < 0 ? Math.ceil(n * factor) : Math.floor(n * factor)) / factor;
}

function roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(v){
  const s = String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/,/g, "").trim();
  if (!s) return 0;
  const n = Number(s);
  if (!isFinite(n)) return 0;
  return truncateDecimalNumber_(n, 2);
}

function formatPurchaseQtyText_(v){
  const s = String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/,/g, "").trim();
  if (!s) return "";
  const n = Number(s);
  if (!isFinite(n)) return String(v == null ? "" : v).trim();
  // 採購公斤數最多保留 1 位小數；整數不顯示 .0，例如 130.0 -> 130、130.4 -> 130.4
  const truncated = truncateDecimalNumber_(n, 2);
  return Number.isInteger(truncated) ? String(truncated) : truncated.toFixed(2).replace(/0+$/, "").replace(/\.$/, "");
}

function normalizePurchaseMeasurementText_(value, unitText){
  const raw = String(value == null ? "" : value).trim();
  if (!raw) return "";
  const unit = String(unitText || "").trim();
  const re = /^\s*([-+]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(.*)$/;
  const m = raw.match(re);
  if (!m) return raw;
  const suffix = String(m[2] || "").trim();
  if (suffix && unit && suffix.indexOf(unit) < 0 && unit.indexOf(suffix) < 0) return raw;
  const finalUnit = unit || suffix;
  const rounded = formatPurchaseQtyText_(m[1]);
  return finalUnit ? (rounded + " " + finalUnit).trim() : rounded;
}

function sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(v, fallbackValue, maxScale){
  const fallback = (fallbackValue === undefined || fallbackValue === null) ? 0 : Number(fallbackValue || 0);
  const n = cleanDecimalInput_(v, maxScale === undefined ? 6 : maxScale);
  if (!isFinite(n) || n < 0) return isFinite(fallback) && fallback >= 0 ? fallback : 0;
  return n;
}

function sanitizeProductSalePrice_(v, fallbackValue){
  const fallback = (fallbackValue === undefined || fallbackValue === null) ? 0 : Number(fallbackValue || 0);
  const n = cleanDecimalInput_(v, 6);
  if (!isFinite(n) || n < 0) return isFinite(fallback) && fallback >= 0 ? fallback : 0;
  return n;
}

function mulDecimalInput_(a, b, maxScale){
  maxScale = (maxScale === undefined) ? 6 : Number(maxScale || 0);
  const na = Number(String(a == null ? "" : a).replace(/,/g, "").trim() || 0);
  const nb = Number(String(b == null ? "" : b).replace(/,/g, "").trim() || 0);
  if (!isFinite(na) || !isFinite(nb)) return 0;
  const scale = Math.min(decimalPlacesText_(a) + decimalPlacesText_(b), maxScale);
  return truncateDecimalNumber_(na * nb, scale);
}

function addDecimalInput_(a, b, maxScale){
  maxScale = (maxScale === undefined) ? 6 : Number(maxScale || 0);
  const na = Number(a || 0);
  const nb = Number(b || 0);
  if (!isFinite(na) || !isFinite(nb)) return 0;
  const scale = Math.min(Math.max(decimalPlacesText_(a), decimalPlacesText_(b)), maxScale);
  return truncateDecimalNumber_(na + nb, scale);
}

function calcRoundedSalePriceFromCost_(cost, markupPercent){
  const base = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(cost, 0, 6);
  const markup = parsePercentSetting_(markupPercent, DEFAULT_PRODUCT_SALE_MARKUP_PERCENT_);
  if (!isFinite(base) || base <= 0) return "";
  const pct = Math.max(0, isFinite(markup) ? markup : DEFAULT_PRODUCT_SALE_MARKUP_PERCENT_);
  // 與前端「售價計算器」一致：售價取整數並無條件進位；結果永遠不可小於 0，避免設定值或異常輸入讓售價變負數。
  return truncateDecimalNumber_(Math.max(0, base * (1 + pct / 100)), 2);
}

const DEFAULT_PRODUCT_SALE_MARKUP_PERCENT_ = 25;

function parsePercentSetting_(value, fallbackPercent) {
  const fallbackRaw = Number(fallbackPercent);
  const fallback = isFinite(fallbackRaw) && fallbackRaw >= 0 ? fallbackRaw : DEFAULT_PRODUCT_SALE_MARKUP_PERCENT_;
  const raw = String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/％/g, "%").trim();
  if (!raw) return fallback;
  const cleaned = raw.replace(/%/g, "").replace(/,/g, "").trim();
  let n = Number(cleaned);
  if (!isFinite(n)) return fallback;
  // 兼容有人在設定表填 0.25 代表 25%。
  if (n > 0 && n < 1) n = n * 100;
  // 售價加價百分比不得為負數；超大值多半是設定表誤填，回到預設 25%，避免售價暴衝或亂跳。
  if (n < 0 || n > 300) return fallback;
  return n;
}

function getProductSaleMarkupPercent_(ss) {
  let map = {};
  try { map = getSystemSettingsMap_(ss) || {}; } catch (e) { map = {}; }
  const aliases = [
    "product_sale_markup_percent",
    "sale_markup_percent",
    "price_markup_percent",
    "product_price_markup_percent",
    "default_sale_markup_percent",
    "售價加成百分比",
    "售價加價百分比",
    "售價加成",
    "售價加價",
    "加價百分比"
  ];
  for (let i = 0; i < aliases.length; i++) {
    const key = aliases[i];
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(map, key)) {
      return parsePercentSetting_(map[key], DEFAULT_PRODUCT_SALE_MARKUP_PERCENT_);
    }
  }
  return DEFAULT_PRODUCT_SALE_MARKUP_PERCENT_;
}

function repairProductPricingFields_(ss, sheet) {
  if (!sheet) return 0;
  const values = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (!values || values.length <= 1) return 0;
  const headers = values[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  const saleMarkupPercent = getProductSaleMarkupPercent_(ss);
  let changed = 0;

  for (let r = 1; r < values.length; r++) {
    const row = values[r];
    if (!row || row.every(v => v === "" || v === null)) continue;

    if (idx.cost !== undefined) {
      const costText = String(row[idx.cost] == null ? "" : row[idx.cost]).trim();
      if (costText && parseLooseNumber_(row[idx.cost]) < 0) {
        row[idx.cost] = 0;
        changed++;
      }
    }

    if (idx.price !== undefined) {
      const priceText = String(row[idx.price] == null ? "" : row[idx.price]).trim();
      const priceNum = parseLooseNumber_(row[idx.price]);
      if (priceText && (priceNum === "" || !isFinite(Number(priceNum)) || Number(priceNum) < 0)) {
        const costNum = idx.cost !== undefined ? sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(row[idx.cost], 0, 6) : 0;
        row[idx.price] = costNum > 0 ? calcRoundedSalePriceFromCost_(costNum, saleMarkupPercent) : 0;
        changed++;
      }
    }

    if (idx.reference_price !== undefined) {
      const refText = String(row[idx.reference_price] == null ? "" : row[idx.reference_price]).trim();
      if (refText && parseLooseNumber_(row[idx.reference_price]) < 0) {
        row[idx.reference_price] = 0;
        changed++;
      }
    }

    if (idx.safety_stock !== undefined) {
      const safetyText = String(row[idx.safety_stock] == null ? "" : row[idx.safety_stock]).trim();
      if (safetyText && parseLooseNumber_(row[idx.safety_stock]) < 0) {
        row[idx.safety_stock] = 0;
        changed++;
      }
    }
  }

  if (changed > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, values.length, values[0].length).setValues(values);
  }
  return changed;
}

function normalizeDate_(d) {
  if (d instanceof Date) return toROCDateString_(d);
  return String(d || "").trim();
}

function compareDate_(d1, d2) {
  d1 = normalizeDate_(d1);
  d2 = normalizeDate_(d2);
  if (!d1 && !d2) return 0;
  if (!d1) return -1;
  if (!d2) return 1;
  const a = d1.split(".").map(Number);
  const b = d2.split(".").map(Number);
  if (a.length < 3 || b.length < 3 || a.some(isNaN) || b.some(isNaN)) {
    return d1 === d2 ? 0 : (d1 > d2 ? 1 : -1);
  }
  if (a[0] !== b[0]) return a[0] - b[0];
  if (a[1] !== b[1]) return a[1] - b[1];
  return a[2] - b[2];
}

function getLedgerSheet_(ss) {
  return getSheetByAnyName_(ss, ["stock_ledger", "stockLedger", "StockLedger", "inventoryLog", "InventoryLog", "lnventoryLog"]);
}

function ensureLedgerSheet_(ss) {
  // 以既有欄位為主（不重排、不覆蓋），必要時才補齊缺漏欄位
  const baseHeaders = ["ledger_id","product_id","product_name","change","direction","reason","ref_id","operator","date","target"];
  const sh = getLedgerSheet_(ss) || ensureSheet_(ss, "stock_ledger", baseHeaders);

  // 若 sheet 已有表頭，確保 baseHeaders 都存在（缺的就補在最後，不改動既有順序）
  const headers = getHeaders_(sh);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  let changed = false;
  let lastCol = sh.getLastColumn();
  baseHeaders.forEach(h => {
    if (idx[h] === undefined) {
      lastCol += 1;
      sh.getRange(1, lastCol).setValue(h);
      changed = true;
    }
  });
  if (changed) {
    // No-op, just makes sure header row is committed
  }

// 若需要顯示/追蹤歷史成本，追加欄位（只加在最後，不重排）
const extraHeaders = ["cost","sku","unit"];
extraHeaders.forEach(h => {
  if (idx[h] === undefined) {
    lastCol += 1;
    sh.getRange(1, lastCol).setValue(h);
    changed = true;
  }
});
  return sh;
}

function writeLedger_(ss, obj) {
  const ledger = ensureLedgerSheet_(ss);
  appendObject_(ledger, obj);
}

function generateId_(prefix) {
  return prefix + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Taipei", "yyyyMMddHHmmss");
}

/**
 * 供應商代碼規則：yyyyMMdd + 3位流水（例：20260317001）
 * - 每日序號從 001 起算
 * - 只掃描 suppliers.id 欄位中符合當日前綴的純數字代碼
 * - 建議搭配 LockService 使用，避免併發重號
 */
function generateSupplierDailyId_(sheet, dateObj) {
  const d = dateObj instanceof Date ? dateObj : new Date();
  const prefix = Utilities.formatDate(d, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyyMMdd");
  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  const col = idx.id;
  if (col === undefined) throw new Error('找不到欄位：id');

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  let maxSeq = 0;
  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    const vals = sheet.getRange(2, col + 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (let i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
      const s = String(vals[i][0] || '').trim();
      if (!s) continue;
      if (/^\d+$/.test(s) && s.indexOf(prefix) === 0 && s.length >= 11) {
        const suf = parseInt(s.slice(8), 10);
        if (!isNaN(suf)) maxSeq = Math.max(maxSeq, suf);
      }
    }
  }

  const next = maxSeq + 1;
  const suf3 = ('000' + next).slice(-3);
  return prefix + suf3;
}

/**
 * 單號規則：yyyyMMdd + 4位流水（例：202602090001）
 * - 每日序號從 0001 起算
 * - 以指定 sheet + 欄位掃描當日最大值後 +1
 * - 建議在 LockService 區塊內呼叫，避免併發重號
 */
function generateDailyDocNo_(sheet, idField, dateObj) {
  const d = dateObj instanceof Date ? dateObj : new Date();
  const prefix = Utilities.formatDate(d, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyyMMdd");

  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  const col = idx[idField];
  if (col === undefined) throw new Error(`找不到欄位：${idField}`);

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  let maxSeq = 0;

  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    const vals = sheet.getRange(2, col + 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (let i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
      const s = String(vals[i][0] || "").trim();
      if (!s) continue;
      // 只看符合 prefix 的純數字單號（舊格式會被忽略）
      if (s.length >= 12 && s.indexOf(prefix) === 0) {
        const suf = parseInt(s.slice(8), 10);
        if (!isNaN(suf)) maxSeq = Math.max(maxSeq, suf);
      }
    }
  }

  const next = maxSeq + 1;
  const suf4 = ("0000" + next).slice(-4);
  return prefix + suf4;
}

function nextDailySequence_(sheet, idField, dateObj) {
  const d = dateObj instanceof Date ? dateObj : new Date();
  const prefix = Utilities.formatDate(d, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyyMMdd");
  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  const col = idx[idField];
  if (col === undefined) throw new Error(`找不到欄位：${idField}`);
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  let maxSeq = 0;
  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    const vals = sheet.getRange(2, col + 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (let i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
      const s = String(vals[i][0] || "").trim();
      if (!s) continue;
      if (s.length >= 12 && s.indexOf(prefix) === 0) {
        const suf = parseInt(s.slice(8), 10);
        if (!isNaN(suf)) maxSeq = Math.max(maxSeq, suf);
      }
    }
  }
  return maxSeq + 1;
}

function dailyDocNoFromSeq_(dateObj, seq) {
  const d = dateObj instanceof Date ? dateObj : new Date();
  const prefix = Utilities.formatDate(d, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyyMMdd");
  const suf4 = ("0000" + Number(seq || 1)).slice(-4);
  return prefix + suf4;
}

function toISODateOnly_(s){
  // Accept YYYY-MM-DD or ROC like 115.02.03 or 115/02/03
  s = String(s || "").trim();
  if (!s) return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd");
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s;

  // ROC format
  const m = s.match(/^(\d{2,3})[\.\/\-](\d{1,2})[\.\/\-](\d{1,2})/);
  if (m){
    const y = Number(m[1]) + 1911;
    const mo = String(m[2]).padStart(2,"0");
    const d = String(m[3]).padStart(2,"0");
    return `${y}-${mo}-${d}`;
  }

  // Fallback parse
  const dt = new Date(s);
  if (!isNaN(dt.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(dt, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd");
  return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd");
}

function toLocalDateTime_(d){
  const dt = (d instanceof Date) ? d : new Date(d);
  return Utilities.formatDate(dt, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
}


function mustJson_(s) {
  const raw = String(s == null ? "" : s);
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (e1) {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(raw));
    } catch (e2) {
      throw e2;
    }
  }
}

];
const PINGTUNG_RAW_HEADERS_ = ["sync_time","source_file","market_date","item_name","spec","market","upper_price","middle_price","lower_price","raw_json"];
const MARKET_PRICE_HISTORY_HEADERS_ = ["history_id","batch_id","sync_time","sync_source","product_id","product_name","product_spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","market","market_label","market_date","raw_name","raw_spec","upper_price","middle_price","lower_price","matched"];
const MARKET_SYNC_LOG_HEADERS_ = ["log_time","status","source","message","latest_market_date","raw_count","fetched_count","preserved_count","updated_count","mapped_count","unmatched_mapping_count","used_fallback","fetch_url","fetch_debug","batch_id"];

function apiMarketSyncStatus_(respond) {

  const logs = readObjects_(logSh);
  const lastLog = logs.length ? logs[logs.length - 1] : null;
  let lastResult = null;
  try {
    lastResult = JSON.parse(String(props.getProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_RESULT_PROPERTY_) || ''));
  } catch (e) {
    lastResult = null;
  }

    status: 'ok',

    last_error: String(props.getProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_ERROR_PROPERTY_) || ''),
    last_debug: String(props.getProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_DEBUG_PROPERTY_) || ''),
    last_result: lastResult,
    last_log: lastLog
  });
}

function getLastMarketSyncResultFromProps_(props) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(String((props || PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()).getProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_RESULT_PROPERTY_) || ''));
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function acquireMarketSyncRunGuard_(props, ttlMs) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const until = Number(String((props || PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()).getProperty(MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_UNTIL_PROPERTY_) || '0')) || 0;
  if (until > now) return { acquired: false, until: until, token: String((props || PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()).getProperty(MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_TOKEN_PROPERTY_) || '') };
  const token = `${now}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
  (props || PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()).setProperty(MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_TOKEN_PROPERTY_, token);
  (props || PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()).setProperty(MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_UNTIL_PROPERTY_, String(now + Math.max(15000, Number(ttlMs || 0) || 60000)));
  return { acquired: true, until: now + Math.max(15000, Number(ttlMs || 0) || 60000), token: token };
}

function releaseMarketSyncRunGuard_(props, token) {
  const p = props || PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const current = String(p.getProperty(MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_TOKEN_PROPERTY_) || '');
  if (token && current && token !== current) return;
  p.deleteProperty(MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_TOKEN_PROPERTY_);
  p.deleteProperty(MARKET_SYNC_RUNNING_UNTIL_PROPERTY_);
}

function syncPingtungRawAndReferencePrices() {
  return syncPingtungRawAndReferencePrices_({ source: 'manual_editor', ensure_trigger: true });
}

  return ensureDailyNoonMarketPriceTrigger_();
}

function dailyNoonMarketPriceSync() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const now = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
  try {
    const result = syncPingtungRawAndReferencePrices_({ source: 'daily_trigger', ensure_trigger: false, allow_fallback_on_fetch_error: true });
    props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_SUCCESS_PROPERTY_, `${now}｜${String(result && result.message || 'ok')}`);
    props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_RESULT_PROPERTY_, JSON.stringify({ status: 'ok', at: now, result: result || {} }));
    props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_DEBUG_PROPERTY_, String(result && result.fetch_debug || ''));
    props.deleteProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_ERROR_PROPERTY_);
    return result;
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = String(err && err.message || err || '');
    props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_ERROR_PROPERTY_, `${now}｜${msg}`);
    props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_RESULT_PROPERTY_, JSON.stringify({ status: 'error', at: now, message: msg }));
    props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_DEBUG_PROPERTY_, msg);
    throw err;
  }
}

function syncPingtungRawAndReferencePrices_(options) {
  options = options || {};
  const ss = getPrimarySpreadsheet_();
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const syncTime = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
  const syncSource = String(options.source || '').trim() || 'manual';
  const batchId = generateId_('MPH');
  let triggerInfo = { installed: false, trigger_count: 0, handler: DAILY_MARKET_PRICE_TRIGGER_HANDLER_ };
  if (options.ensure_trigger !== false) {
    try {
      triggerInfo = ensureDailyNoonMarketPriceTrigger_();
    } catch (triggerErr) {
      triggerInfo = { installed: false, trigger_count: 0, handler: DAILY_MARKET_PRICE_TRIGGER_HANDLER_, warning: String(triggerErr && triggerErr.message || triggerErr || '') };
    }
  }

  const guardTtlMs = Number(options.guard_ttl_ms || (syncSource === 'manual_api' ? 90000 : 180000)) || 90000;
  const guard = acquireMarketSyncRunGuard_(props, guardTtlMs);
  if (!guard.acquired) {
    const lastResult = getLastMarketSyncResultFromProps_(props);
    return {
      message: '市場價格同步仍在執行中，先回傳目前已知狀態，請稍後重新整理。',
      busy: true,
      in_progress_until: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(guard.until), 'Asia/Taipei', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
      last_result: lastResult,
      trigger_installed: !!triggerInfo.installed,
      trigger_count: Number(triggerInfo.trigger_count || 0),
      trigger_handler: String(triggerInfo.handler || DAILY_MARKET_PRICE_TRIGGER_HANDLER_),
      trigger_warning: String(triggerInfo.warning || ''),
      synced_at: syncTime
    };
  }

  try {
    const rawSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "PingtungRaw", PINGTUNG_RAW_HEADERS_);
    const existingRows = readNormalizedPingtungRawRows_(rawSh);

    let fetched = null;
    let usedFallback = false;
    let fallbackReason = '';
    let fetchUrl = '';
    let fetchDebug = '';

    try {
      fetched = fetchVegetableRawRowsRobust_(options);
      fetchUrl = String(fetched && fetched.fetch_url || '');
      fetchDebug = String(fetched && fetched.fetch_debug || '');
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = summarizeMarketSyncError_(err);
      const allowFallback = options.allow_fallback_on_fetch_error !== false;
      const canFallback = !!existingRows.length && allowFallback && isRecoverableMarketSyncError_(err);
      if (!canFallback) {
        appendMarketSyncLog_(ss, {
          log_time: syncTime,
          status: 'error',
          source: syncSource,
          message: msg,
          latest_market_date: existingRows.reduce((acc, r) => String(r.market_date || '') > acc ? String(r.market_date || '') : acc, ''),
          raw_count: existingRows.length,
          fetched_count: 0,
          preserved_count: existingRows.length,
          updated_count: 0,
          mapped_count: 0,
          unmatched_mapping_count: 0,
          used_fallback: 0,
          fetch_url: '',
          fetch_debug: msg,
          batch_id: batchId
        });
        throw err;
      }
      fetched = { rows: [], fetch_url: '', fetch_debug: msg };
      usedFallback = true;
      fallbackReason = msg;
      fetchDebug = msg;
    }

    const historyRows = mergeRawHistoryRows_(existingRows, fetched.rows || []);
    const latestRows = selectLatestRawRows_(historyRows);
    if (!historyRows.length || !latestRows.length) {
      appendMarketSyncLog_(ss, {
        log_time: syncTime,
        status: 'error',
        source: syncSource,
        message: '目前沒有可保留的果菜市場價格資料',
        latest_market_date: '',
        raw_count: 0,
        fetched_count: 0,
        preserved_count: 0,
        updated_count: 0,
        mapped_count: 0,
        unmatched_mapping_count: 0,
        used_fallback: usedFallback ? 1 : 0,
        fetch_url: fetchUrl,
        fetch_debug: fetchDebug,
        batch_id: batchId
      });
      throw new Error('目前沒有可保留的果菜市場價格資料');
    }

    rawSh.clearContents();
    rawSh.getRange(1, 1, 1, PINGTUNG_RAW_HEADERS_.length).setValues([PINGTUNG_RAW_HEADERS_]);
    rawSh.getRange(2, 1, historyRows.length, PINGTUNG_RAW_HEADERS_.length).setValues(historyRows.map(r => [
      r.sync_time,
      r.source_file,
      r.market_date,
      r.item_name,
      r.spec,
      r.market,
      r.upper_price,
      r.middle_price,
      r.lower_price,
      r.raw_json
    ]));

    const applied = applyReferencePricesFromRaw_(ss, latestRows, { batch_id: batchId, sync_time: syncTime, sync_source: syncSource });
    const latestMarketDate = historyRows.reduce((acc, r) => String(r.market_date || '') > acc ? String(r.market_date || '') : acc, '');
    const fetchedCount = (fetched && Array.isArray(fetched.rows)) ? fetched.rows.length : 0;
    const newCount = countNewRawHistoryRows_(existingRows, fetched.rows || []);
    const suffix = usedFallback
      ? `（本次未取得可用新行情，已保留最後一次更新價格，最新行情日期 ${latestMarketDate || '—'}；原因：${fallbackReason || '沿用既有資料'}）`
      : '';

    const result = {
      message: `同步完成：PingtungRaw 歷史保留 ${historyRows.length} 筆，最新快照 ${latestRows.length} 筆，商品主檔參考價格更新 ${applied.updated_count} 筆${suffix}`,
      raw_count: historyRows.length,
      latest_snapshot_count: latestRows.length,
      fetched_count: fetchedCount,
      preserved_count: Math.max(0, historyRows.length - newCount),
      appended_count: newCount,
      updated_count: applied.updated_count,
      mapped_count: applied.mapped_count,
      unmatched_mapping_count: applied.unmatched_mapping_count,
      latest_market_date: latestMarketDate,
      used_fallback: usedFallback,
      fallback_reason: fallbackReason,
      batch_id: batchId,
      history_logged_count: applied.history_logged_count || 0,
      trigger_installed: !!triggerInfo.installed,
      trigger_count: Number(triggerInfo.trigger_count || 0),
      trigger_handler: String(triggerInfo.handler || DAILY_MARKET_PRICE_TRIGGER_HANDLER_),
      trigger_warning: String(triggerInfo.warning || ''),
      fetch_url: fetchUrl,
      fetch_debug: fetchDebug,
      synced_at: syncTime
    };

    appendMarketSyncLog_(ss, {
      log_time: syncTime,
      status: usedFallback ? 'fallback' : 'ok',
      source: syncSource,
      message: result.message,
      latest_market_date: latestMarketDate,
      raw_count: result.raw_count,
      fetched_count: result.fetched_count,
      preserved_count: result.preserved_count,
      updated_count: result.updated_count,
      mapped_count: result.mapped_count,
      unmatched_mapping_count: result.unmatched_mapping_count,
      used_fallback: usedFallback ? 1 : 0,
      fetch_url: fetchUrl,
      fetch_debug: fetchDebug,
      batch_id: batchId
    });

    props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_LAST_RESULT_PROPERTY_, JSON.stringify({ status: 'ok', at: syncTime, result: result }));
    props.setProperty(DAILY_MARKET_SYNC_DEBUG_PROPERTY_, String(fetchDebug || ''));
    return result;
  } finally {
    releaseMarketSyncRunGuard_(props, guard.token);
  }
}

function appendMarketSyncLog_(ss, obj) {
  try {
    const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, 'MarketSyncLog', MARKET_SYNC_LOG_HEADERS_);
    appendObject_(sh, obj || {});
  } catch (e) {}
}

function summarizeMarketSyncError_(err) {
  return String(err && err.message || err || '').trim();
}

function isRecoverableMarketSyncError_(err) {
  const msg = summarizeMarketSyncError_(err).toLowerCase();
  if (!msg) return false;
  if (msg.indexOf('沒有可辨識的蔬菜行情資料') >= 0 || msg.indexOf('沒有可辨識的行情資料') >= 0) return true;
  return [
    'http 403', 'http 404', 'http 408', 'http 409', 'http 423', 'http 425', 'http 429',
    'http 500', 'http 502', 'http 503', 'http 504',
    'timed out', 'time out', 'timeout', 'exception:', 'service invoked too many times',
    'address unavailable', 'dns', 'socket', 'network', 'fetch failed', 'rate limit', 'abuse detection',
    'json 解析失敗', 'unexpected token', 'syntaxerror', 'empty response', '沒有任何可用的市場價格來源'
  ].some(function(token){ return msg.indexOf(token) >= 0; });
}

function getPingtungGistRawUrlCandidates_() {
  return [
    'https://gist.githubusercontent.com/surfo25888052/f1df7ae631d4eff3790b75c3536cd7ef/raw/daily-report.json',
    'https://gist.github.com/surfo25888052/f1df7ae631d4eff3790b75c3536cd7ef/raw/daily-report.json'
  ];
}

function fetchUrlTextWithRetries_(url, options) {
  options = options || {};
  const attempts = Math.max(1, Number(options.attempts || 2));
  const headers = options.headers || {};
  let lastErr = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < attempts; i++) {
    try {
      const res = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
        muteHttpExceptions: true,
        followRedirects: true,
        validateHttpsCertificates: true,
        headers: headers
      });
      const code = Number(res.getResponseCode() || 0);
      const body = String(res.getContentText() || '');
      if (code >= 200 && code < 300) {
        return { ok: true, url: url, code: code, text: body, content_type: String(res.getHeaders()['Content-Type'] || '') };
      }
      const bodyHint = body ? body.slice(0, 200) : '';
      throw new Error(`抓取 ${url} 失敗（HTTP ${code}）${bodyHint ? '｜' + bodyHint : ''}`);
    } catch (err) {
      lastErr = err;
      if (i < attempts - 1) Utilities.sleep(800 * (i + 1));
    }
  }
  throw lastErr || new Error(`抓取 ${url} 失敗`);
}

function parseGistApiResponseText_(text) {
  const gist = JSON.parse(String(text || '{}'));
  const files = gist.files || {};
  const syncTime = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  const out = [];
  const rawCandidates = [];

  Object.keys(files).forEach(function(k) {
    const f = files[k] || {};
    const filename = String(f.filename || k || '').trim() || k;
    let content = String(f.content || '');
    const mimeType = String(f.type || '');
    const rawUrl = String(f.raw_url || '').trim();
    if (rawUrl) rawCandidates.push(rawUrl);
    if ((!content || f.truncated) && rawUrl) {
      const rawRes = fetchUrlTextWithRetries_(rawUrl, { attempts: 2, headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Google-Apps-Script', 'Accept': 'text/plain,application/json;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' } });
      content = String(rawRes.text || '');
    }
    const parsedRows = parsePingtungMarketFile_(content, filename, mimeType);
    parsedRows.forEach(function(row) {
      row.sync_time = syncTime;
      row.source_file = filename;
      out.push(row);
    });
  });

  if (!out.length) throw new Error('Gist 內沒有可辨識的蔬菜行情資料');
  return {
    rows: out,
    fetch_url: PINGTUNG_GIST_API_URL_,
    fetch_debug: `api_files=${Object.keys(files).length}; raw_candidates=${rawCandidates.length}`
  };
}

function toROCQueryDate_(dateObj) {
  const d = (dateObj instanceof Date) ? dateObj : new Date(dateObj);
  return toROCDateString_(d);
}

function mapTargetVegetableMarketKey_(marketCode, marketName) {
  const code = String(marketCode || '').trim();
  if (code && TARGET_VEGETABLE_MARKET_CODE_MAP_[code]) return TARGET_VEGETABLE_MARKET_CODE_MAP_[code];
  const normalized = normalizeMarketLabel_(marketName || code);
  return (normalized === 'FirstMarket' || normalized === 'SecondMarket' || normalized === 'Pingtung') ? normalized : '';
}

function extractRowsFromApiPayload_(payload) {
  if (Array.isArray(payload)) return payload;
  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') return [];
  if (Array.isArray(payload.Data)) return payload.Data;
  if (Array.isArray(payload.data)) return payload.data;
  if (Array.isArray(payload.DATA)) return payload.DATA;
  if (payload.result && Array.isArray(payload.result)) return payload.result;
  if (payload.response && Array.isArray(payload.response)) return payload.response;
  return [];
}

function makeApiPageSignature_(rows) {
  const list = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
  if (!list.length) return 'empty';
  const first = list[0] || {};
  const last = list[list.length - 1] || {};
  return JSON.stringify({
    count: list.length,
    first_date: String(first.TransDate || first['交易日期'] || ''),
    first_code: String(first.CropCode || first['作物代號'] || ''),
    first_market: String(first.MarketCode || first['市場代號'] || ''),
    last_date: String(last.TransDate || last['交易日期'] || ''),
    last_code: String(last.CropCode || last['作物代號'] || ''),
    last_market: String(last.MarketCode || last['市場代號'] || '')
  });
}

function normalizeMoaAgriProductsRow_(row, syncTime, sourceLabel) {
  const tcType = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['TcType','種類代碼']) || '').trim();
  if (tcType && tcType !== MOA_VEGETABLE_TC_TYPE_) return null;

  const marketCode = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['MarketCode','市場代號']) || '').trim();
  const marketName = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['MarketName','市場名稱']) || '').trim();
  const marketKey = mapTargetVegetableMarketKey_(marketCode, marketName);
  if (!marketKey) return null;

  const cropName = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['CropName','作物名稱','name','item_name']) || '').trim();
  if (!cropName) return null;
  const split = splitMarketItemAndSpec_(cropName);
  const marketDate = normalizeLooseDate_(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['TransDate','交易日期']) || '');
  const upper = parseLooseNumber_(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['Upper_Price','上價','upper_price']));
  const middle = parseLooseNumber_(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['Middle_Price','中價','middle_price']));
  const lower = parseLooseNumber_(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['Lower_Price','下價','lower_price']));
  if (!marketDate || (upper === '' && middle === '' && lower === '')) return null;

  return {
    sync_time: String(syncTime || '').trim(),
    source_file: String(sourceLabel || 'AgriProductsTransType').trim(),
    market_date: marketDate,
    item_name: split.item_name,
    spec: split.spec,
    market: marketKey,
    upper_price: upper,
    middle_price: middle,
    lower_price: lower,
    raw_json: JSON.stringify(row)
  };
}

function buildMarketSyncFetchConfig_(options) {
  options = options || {};
  const fastMode = !!options.fast_mode;
  return {
    fast_mode: fastMode,
    day_scan_limit: Math.max(1, Number(options.day_scan_limit || (fastMode ? 5 : MOA_MARKET_SYNC_LOOKBACK_DAYS_)) || (fastMode ? 5 : MOA_MARKET_SYNC_LOOKBACK_DAYS_)),
    probe_page_limit: Math.max(1, Number(options.probe_page_limit || (fastMode ? 2 : 3)) || (fastMode ? 2 : 3)),
    fetch_page_limit: Math.max(2, Number(options.page_limit || (fastMode ? 6 : MOA_MARKET_SYNC_MAX_PAGES_)) || (fastMode ? 6 : MOA_MARKET_SYNC_MAX_PAGES_)),
    attempts: Math.max(1, Number(options.fetch_attempts || (fastMode ? 2 : 3)) || (fastMode ? 2 : 3)),
    sleep_ms: Math.max(0, Number(options.page_sleep_ms || (fastMode ? 20 : 80)) || (fastMode ? 20 : 80))
  };
}

function buildMoaMarketApiUrl_(dateText, page, marketCode) {
  const parts = [
    `TcType=${encodeURIComponent(MOA_VEGETABLE_TC_TYPE_)}`,
    `Start_time=${encodeURIComponent(dateText)}`,
    `End_time=${encodeURIComponent(dateText)}`,
    `Page=${page}`
  ];
  if (marketCode) parts.unshift(`MarketCode=${encodeURIComponent(String(marketCode).trim())}`);
  return `${MOA_AGRI_PRODUCTS_API_URL_}?${parts.join('&')}`;
}

function fetchMoaApiRowsForDate_(dateText, syncTime, config, marketCode) {
  const headers = {
    Accept: 'application/json',
    'User-Agent': 'Google-Apps-Script',
    'Cache-Control': 'no-cache'
  };
  const expectedMarketCode = String(marketCode || '').trim();
  const expectedMarketKey = expectedMarketCode ? String(TARGET_VEGETABLE_MARKET_CODE_MAP_[expectedMarketCode] || '').trim() : '';
  const allRows = [];
  const pageSigns = {};
  const pageSummaries = [];
  let lastUrl = '';

  for (let page = 1; page <= Number(config.fetch_page_limit || MOA_MARKET_SYNC_MAX_PAGES_); page++) {
    const url = buildMoaMarketApiUrl_(dateText, page, expectedMarketCode);
    lastUrl = url;
    const res = fetchUrlTextWithRetries_(url, { attempts: Number(config.attempts || 2), headers: headers });
    let payload = null;
    try {
      payload = JSON.parse(String(res.text || '[]'));
    } catch (err) {
      throw new Error(`AgriProductsTransType ${dateText} ${expectedMarketCode || 'ALL'} 第 ${page} 頁 JSON 解析失敗：${err && err.message ? err.message : err}`);
    }
    const pageRows = extractRowsFromApiPayload_(payload);
    if (!pageRows.length) {
      pageSummaries.push(`p${page}=0`);
      break;
    }

    const signature = makeApiPageSignature_(pageRows);
    if (pageSigns[signature]) {
      pageSummaries.push(`p${page}=repeat`);
      break;
    }
    pageSigns[signature] = true;

    let accepted = 0;
    pageRows.forEach(function(row) {
      const normalized = normalizeMoaAgriProductsRow_(row, syncTime, `AgriProductsTransType#${expectedMarketCode || 'ALL'}#${dateText}#page${page}`);
      if (!normalized) return;
      if (expectedMarketKey && normalizeMarketLabel_(normalized.market || '') !== expectedMarketKey) return;
      allRows.push(normalized);
      accepted += 1;
    });
    pageSummaries.push(`p${page}=${pageRows.length}/${accepted}`);
    if (page >= Number(config.probe_page_limit || 2) && accepted === 0 && !allRows.length) break;
    if (Number(config.sleep_ms || 0) > 0) Utilities.sleep(Number(config.sleep_ms || 0));
  }

  return {
    rows: allRows,
    fetch_url: lastUrl || buildMoaMarketApiUrl_(dateText, 1, expectedMarketCode),
    fetch_debug: `source=moa_api_v1; market=${expectedMarketCode || 'ALL'}; date=${dateText}; ${pageSummaries.join(', ')}; rows=${allRows.length}`
  };
}

function fetchVegetableRawRowsFromMoaApiV1_(options) {
  const syncTime = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  const config = buildMarketSyncFetchConfig_(options);
  const today = new Date();
  const targetMarkets = TARGET_VEGETABLE_MARKET_TARGETS_.slice();
  const foundByMarket = {};
  const foundDateByMarket = {};
  const foundUrlByMarket = {};
  const probeMap = {};
  const lastDebugByMarket = {};

  targetMarkets.forEach(function(target) {
    const marketKey = String(target.market_key || '').trim();
    const marketCode = String(target.market_code || '').trim();
    probeMap[marketKey] = [];

    for (let dayOffset = 0; dayOffset <= Number(config.day_scan_limit || MOA_MARKET_SYNC_LOOKBACK_DAYS_); dayOffset++) {
      const dateObj = new Date(today.getTime() - (dayOffset * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
      const dateText = toROCQueryDate_(dateObj);
      const probe = fetchMoaApiRowsForDate_(dateText, syncTime, config, marketCode);
      lastDebugByMarket[marketKey] = probe.fetch_debug || '';
      const probeCount = Array.isArray(probe.rows) ? probe.rows.length : 0;
      probeMap[marketKey].push(`${dateText}:${probeCount}`);

      if (!probeCount) continue;

      foundByMarket[marketKey] = probe.rows.filter(function(row) {
        return normalizeMarketLabel_(row && row.market || '') === marketKey;
      });
      if (!foundByMarket[marketKey].length) continue;

      foundDateByMarket[marketKey] = dateText;
      foundUrlByMarket[marketKey] = probe.fetch_url || '';
      break;
    }
  });

  const mergedRows = [];
  targetMarkets.forEach(function(target) {
    const marketKey = String(target.market_key || '').trim();
    if (!foundByMarket[marketKey]) return;
    foundByMarket[marketKey].forEach(function(row) { mergedRows.push(row); });
  });

  if (!mergedRows.length) {
    throw new Error(`AgriProductsTransType 在最近 ${Number(config.day_scan_limit || MOA_MARKET_SYNC_LOOKBACK_DAYS_) + 1} 天沒有可用的 N04 目標市場資料`);
  }

  const marketSummary = targetMarkets.map(function(target) {
    const marketKey = String(target.market_key || '').trim();
    const marketCode = String(target.market_code || '').trim();
    return `${marketCode}/${marketKey}=${foundDateByMarket[marketKey] || 'missing'}`;
  }).join(', ');

  const debugSummary = targetMarkets.map(function(target) {
    const marketKey = String(target.market_key || '').trim();
    const marketCode = String(target.market_code || '').trim();
    return `${marketCode}:${(probeMap[marketKey] || []).join('>')}`;
  }).join(' | ');

  const fetchUrlSummary = targetMarkets.map(function(target) {
    const marketKey = String(target.market_key || '').trim();
    const marketCode = String(target.market_code || '').trim();
    return `${marketCode}=${foundUrlByMarket[marketKey] || buildMoaMarketApiUrl_(toROCQueryDate_(today), 1, marketCode)}`;
  }).join(' || ');

  const lastDebugSummary = targetMarkets.map(function(target) {
    const marketKey = String(target.market_key || '').trim();
    const marketCode = String(target.market_code || '').trim();
    return `${marketCode}:${String(lastDebugByMarket[marketKey] || '').trim()}`;
  }).join(' || ');

  return {
    rows: mergedRows,
    fetch_url: fetchUrlSummary,
    fetch_debug: `source=moa_api_v1_split_market; ${marketSummary}; probe=${debugSummary}; last=${lastDebugSummary}; rows=${mergedRows.length}`
  };
}

function fetchVegetableRawRowsFromLegacyFarmTrans_() {
  const syncTime = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  const endDate = new Date();
  const startDate = new Date(endDate.getTime() - (MOA_MARKET_SYNC_LOOKBACK_DAYS_ * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
  const startText = toROCQueryDate_(startDate);
  const endText = toROCQueryDate_(endDate);
  const url = `${MOA_FARM_TRANS_API_URL_}?StartDate=${encodeURIComponent(startText)}&EndDate=${encodeURIComponent(endText)}&TcType=${encodeURIComponent(MOA_VEGETABLE_TC_TYPE_)}`;
  const res = fetchUrlTextWithRetries_(url, {
    attempts: 2,
    headers: {
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'User-Agent': 'Google-Apps-Script',
      'Cache-Control': 'no-cache'
    }
  });

  let payload = null;
  try {
    payload = JSON.parse(String(res.text || '[]'));
  } catch (err) {
    throw new Error(`FarmTransData JSON 解析失敗：${err && err.message ? err.message : err}`);
  }

  const rows = extractRowsFromApiPayload_(payload)
    .map(function(row) { return normalizeLegacyFarmTransRow_(row, syncTime); })
    .filter(function(row) { return !!row; });

  if (!rows.length) {
    throw new Error(`FarmTransData 在 ${startText} ~ ${endText} 期間沒有可用的 N04 目標市場資料`);
  }

  return {
    rows: rows,
    fetch_url: url,
    fetch_debug: `source=farm_trans_legacy; window=${startText}~${endText}; rows=${rows.length}`
  };
}

function fetchVegetableRawRowsRobust_(options) {
  options = options || {};
  const errors = [];

  try {
    return fetchVegetableRawRowsFromMoaApiV1_(options);
  } catch (err) {
    errors.push(`moa_api_v1=${summarizeMarketSyncError_(err)}`);
  }

  try {
    return fetchVegetableRawRowsFromLegacyFarmTrans_();
  } catch (err) {
    errors.push(`farm_trans_legacy=${summarizeMarketSyncError_(err)}`);
  }

  try {
    const gistResult = fetchPingtungRawFromGist_();
    gistResult.fetch_debug = `source=gist_fallback; ${String(gistResult.fetch_debug || '')}`.trim();
    return gistResult;
  } catch (err) {
    errors.push(`gist=${summarizeMarketSyncError_(err)}`);
  }

  throw new Error(errors.join(' || ') || '沒有任何可用的市場價格來源');
}

function ensureDailyNoonMarketPriceTrigger_() {
  const all = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  const hits = all.filter(t => String(t.getHandlerFunction && t.getHandlerFunction() || '') === DAILY_MARKET_PRICE_TRIGGER_HANDLER_);
  for (let i = 1; i < hits.length; i++) {
    ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(hits[i]);
  }
  let installed = false;
  if (!hits.length) {
    ScriptApp.newTrigger(DAILY_MARKET_PRICE_TRIGGER_HANDLER_)
      .timeBased()
      .everyDays(1)
      .atHour(12)
      .nearMinute(0)
      .inTimezone('Asia/Taipei')
      .create();
    installed = true;
  }
  const finalCount = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().filter(t => String(t.getHandlerFunction && t.getHandlerFunction() || '') === DAILY_MARKET_PRICE_TRIGGER_HANDLER_).length;
  return { installed: installed, trigger_count: finalCount, handler: DAILY_MARKET_PRICE_TRIGGER_HANDLER_ };
}

function readNormalizedPingtungRawRows_(sh) {
  if (!sh) return [];
  return readObjects_(sh).map(r => ({
    sync_time: String(r.sync_time || '').trim(),
    source_file: String(r.source_file || '').trim(),
    market_date: normalizeLooseDate_(r.market_date || ''),
    item_name: String(r.item_name || '').trim(),
    spec: String(r.spec || '').trim(),
    market: normalizeMarketLabel_(r.market || ''),
    upper_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.upper_price),
    middle_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.middle_price),
    lower_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.lower_price),
    raw_json: String(r.raw_json || '')
  })).filter(r => r.item_name && r.market);
}

function normalizeRawHistoryRow_(row) {
  if (!row) return null;
  const market = normalizeMarketLabel_(row.market || '');
  const itemName = String(row.item_name || '').trim();
  const spec = String(row.spec || '').trim();
  const marketDate = normalizeLooseDate_(row.market_date || '');
  if (!market || !itemName || !marketDate) return null;
  return {
    sync_time: String(row.sync_time || '').trim(),
    source_file: String(row.source_file || '').trim(),
    market_date: marketDate,
    item_name: itemName,
    spec: spec,
    market: market,
    upper_price: parseLooseNumber_(row.upper_price),
    middle_price: parseLooseNumber_(row.middle_price),
    lower_price: parseLooseNumber_(row.lower_price),
    raw_json: String(row.raw_json || '')
  };
}

function rawHistoryIdentityKey_(row) {
  const normalized = normalizeRawHistoryRow_(row);
  if (!normalized) return '';
  return [
    normalized.market,
    normalized.market_date,
    normalizeLookupKey_(normalized.item_name),
    normalizeLookupKey_(normalized.spec)
  ].join('||');
}

function compareRawHistoryRowQuality_(current, next, preferNew) {
  const currentScore = [current.upper_price, current.middle_price, current.lower_price].filter(v => v !== '' && v !== null && v !== undefined).length;
  const nextScore = [next.upper_price, next.middle_price, next.lower_price].filter(v => v !== '' && v !== null && v !== undefined).length;
  if (nextScore > currentScore) return 1;
  if (nextScore < currentScore) return -1;

  const currentSync = String(current.sync_time || '');
  const nextSync = String(next.sync_time || '');
  if (nextSync > currentSync) return 1;
  if (nextSync < currentSync) return -1;
  return preferNew ? 1 : 0;
}

function sortRawHistoryRows_(rows) {
  return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).slice().sort((a, b) => {
    const dateCmp = String(b.market_date || '').localeCompare(String(a.market_date || ''));
    if (dateCmp !== 0) return dateCmp;
    const marketCmp = marketSortPriority_(b.market) - marketSortPriority_(a.market);
    if (marketCmp !== 0) return marketCmp;
    const nameCmp = String(a.item_name || '').localeCompare(String(b.item_name || ''), 'zh-Hant');
    if (nameCmp !== 0) return nameCmp;
    const specCmp = String(a.spec || '').localeCompare(String(b.spec || ''), 'zh-Hant');
    if (specCmp !== 0) return specCmp;
    return String(b.sync_time || '').localeCompare(String(a.sync_time || ''));
  });
}

function mergeRawHistoryRows_(existingRows, fetchedRows) {
  const bucket = {};
  const put_ = (row, preferNew) => {
    const normalized = normalizeRawHistoryRow_(row);
    if (!normalized) return;
    const key = rawHistoryIdentityKey_(normalized);
    if (!key) return;
    const current = bucket[key];
    if (!current) {
      bucket[key] = normalized;
      return;
    }
    if (compareRawHistoryRowQuality_(current, normalized, preferNew) > 0) {
      bucket[key] = normalized;
    }
  };

  (Array.isArray(existingRows) ? existingRows : []).forEach(row => put_(row, false));
  (Array.isArray(fetchedRows) ? fetchedRows : []).forEach(row => put_(row, true));
  return sortRawHistoryRows_(Object.keys(bucket).map(k => bucket[k]));
}

function selectLatestRawRows_(rows) {
  const bucket = {};
  const put_ = (row, preferNew) => {
    const normalized = normalizeRawHistoryRow_(row);
    if (!normalized) return;
    const key = [normalized.market, normalizeLookupKey_(normalized.item_name), normalizeLookupKey_(normalized.spec)].join('||');
    const current = bucket[key];
    if (!current) {
      bucket[key] = normalized;
      return;
    }

    const currentDate = String(current.market_date || '');
    const nextDate = String(normalized.market_date || '');
    if (nextDate > currentDate) {
      bucket[key] = normalized;
      return;
    }
    if (nextDate < currentDate) return;

    if (compareRawHistoryRowQuality_(current, normalized, preferNew) > 0) {
      bucket[key] = normalized;
    }
  };

  (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).forEach(row => put_(row, true));
  return sortRawHistoryRows_(Object.keys(bucket).map(k => bucket[k]));
}

function countNewRawHistoryRows_(existingRows, fetchedRows) {
  const existingKeys = {};
  (Array.isArray(existingRows) ? existingRows : []).forEach(function(row) {
    const key = rawHistoryIdentityKey_(row);
    if (key) existingKeys[key] = true;
  });
  let count = 0;
  const seenFetched = {};
  (Array.isArray(fetchedRows) ? fetchedRows : []).forEach(function(row) {
    const key = rawHistoryIdentityKey_(row);
    if (!key || existingKeys[key] || seenFetched[key]) return;
    seenFetched[key] = true;
    count += 1;
  });
  return count;
}

function fetchPingtungRawFromGist_() {
  const headers = {
    Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json',
    'User-Agent': 'Google-Apps-Script',
    'Cache-Control': 'no-cache'
  };

  const fetchErrors = [];
  try {
    const apiRes = fetchUrlTextWithRetries_(PINGTUNG_GIST_API_URL_, { attempts: 2, headers: headers });
    return parseGistApiResponseText_(apiRes.text || '');
  } catch (err) {
    fetchErrors.push(`api=${summarizeMarketSyncError_(err)}`);
  }

  const rawCandidates = getPingtungGistRawUrlCandidates_();
  for (let i = 0; i < rawCandidates.length; i++) {
    const url = rawCandidates[i];
    try {
      const rawRes = fetchUrlTextWithRetries_(url, { attempts: 2, headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Google-Apps-Script', 'Accept': 'text/plain,application/json;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' } });
      const rows = parsePingtungMarketFile_(String(rawRes.text || ''), 'daily-report.json', String(rawRes.content_type || 'application/json'));
      if (rows.length) {
        const syncTime = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
        rows.forEach(function(row) {
          row.sync_time = syncTime;
          row.source_file = 'daily-report.json';
        });
        return {
          rows: rows,
          fetch_url: url,
          fetch_debug: `raw_rows=${rows.length}`
        };
      }
      fetchErrors.push(`raw=${url}｜沒有可辨識的蔬菜行情資料`);
    } catch (err) {
      fetchErrors.push(`raw=${url}｜${summarizeMarketSyncError_(err)}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error(fetchErrors.join(' || ') || 'Gist 內沒有可辨識的蔬菜行情資料');
}

function parsePingtungMarketFile_(content, filename, mimeType) {
  const text = String(content || "").replace(/^﻿/, "").trim();
  if (!text) return [];

  const lowerName = String(filename || "").toLowerCase();
  let candidates = [];

  const tryJson = () => {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(text);
      candidates = extractRowsFromAnyJson_(data);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const tryDelimited = (delimiter) => {
    try {
      const rows = Utilities.parseCsv(text, delimiter);
      if (!rows || rows.length < 2) return false;
      const headers = rows[0].map(v => String(v || "").trim());
      candidates = rows.slice(1).map(r => {
        const obj = {};
        headers.forEach((h, idx) => { if (h) obj[h] = r[idx]; });
        return obj;
      });
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  };

  if (lowerName.endsWith('.json') || /json/i.test(mimeType)) {
    if (!tryJson()) { tryDelimited(',') || tryDelimited('\t'); }
  } else if (lowerName.endsWith('.csv')) {
    if (!tryDelimited(',')) tryJson();
  } else if (lowerName.endsWith('.tsv') || lowerName.endsWith('.txt')) {
    if (!tryDelimited('\t')) { if (!tryDelimited(',')) tryJson(); }
  } else {
    if (!tryJson()) { if (!tryDelimited(',')) tryDelimited('\t'); }
  }

  if (!candidates.length && looksLikeMarketTextBlock_(text)) {
    candidates = parseMarketTextBlock_(text, filename, '');
  }

  const out = [];
  candidates.forEach(obj => {
    const norm = normalizeMarketRow_(obj);
    if (!norm) return;
    out.push({
      market_date: norm.market_date,
      item_name: norm.item_name,
      spec: norm.spec,
      market: norm.market,
      upper_price: norm.upper_price,
      middle_price: norm.middle_price,
      lower_price: norm.lower_price,
      raw_json: JSON.stringify(obj)
    });
  });
  return out;
}

function extractRowsFromAnyJson_(node) {
  const out = [];
  const seenTextBlocks = {};

  function pushTextBlock_(text, marketHint, dateHint) {
    const s = String(text || '').trim();
    if (!s) return;
    const key = `${String(marketHint || '')}__${String(dateHint || '')}__${s.slice(0, 120)}`;
    if (seenTextBlocks[key]) return;
    seenTextBlocks[key] = true;
    parseMarketTextBlock_(s, marketHint, dateHint).forEach(row => out.push(row));
  }

  function walk(v, keyHint, inheritedDateHint) {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) {
      v.forEach(item => walk(item, keyHint, inheritedDateHint));
      return;
    }
    if (typeof v === 'string') {
      if (looksLikeMarketTextBlock_(v)) pushTextBlock_(v, keyHint, inheritedDateHint);
      return;
    }
    if (!v || typeof v !== 'object') return;

    if (looksLikeMarketRowObject_(v)) out.push(v);

    const localDateHint = pickValueByAliases_(v, ['updatedAt','updated_at','date','market_date','trade_date','日期','交易日期']) || inheritedDateHint || '';
    Object.keys(v).forEach(k => {
      const child = v[k];
      if (typeof child === 'string') {
        if (looksLikeMarketTextBlock_(child)) pushTextBlock_(child, k, localDateHint);
        return;
      }
      if (Array.isArray(child) || (child && typeof child === 'object')) walk(child, k, localDateHint);
    });
  }

  walk(node, '', '');
  return out;
}


function looksLikeMarketTextBlock_(text) {
  const s = String(text || '').trim();
  if (!s) return false;
  const hasName = /[一-鿿A-Za-z]/.test(s);
  const hasPrice = /(上價|最高價)\s*[:：]?\s*[\d\-]|(中價|平均價)\s*[:：]?\s*[\d\-]|(下價|最低價)\s*[:：]?\s*[\d\-]/.test(s);
  const hasDate = /(交易日期|更新日期|查詢日期|日期)\s*[:：]?\s*[0-9\.\/-]{5,}/.test(s);
  return hasName && hasPrice && hasDate;
}

function parseMarketTextBlock_(text, marketHint, dateHint) {
  const out = [];
  const lines = String(text || '').split(/\r?\n+/).map(v => String(v || '').trim()).filter(Boolean);
  let currentMarket = normalizeMarketLabel_(marketHint);
  let currentDate = normalizeLooseDate_(dateHint || '');

  const headerDateMatcher = /(交易日期|更新日期|查詢日期|日期)\s*[:：]?\s*([0-9\.\/-]+)/;
  lines.forEach(function(line) {
    const cleaned = line.replace(/^[•‧・●○◦▪■□◆◇★☆※◎◉\-]+\s*/, '').trim();
    if (!cleaned) return;

    const marketHintInLine = normalizeMarketLabel_(cleaned);
    if (marketHintInLine === 'Pingtung' || marketHintInLine === 'FirstMarket' || marketHintInLine === 'SecondMarket') {
      currentMarket = marketHintInLine;
    }

    const headerDateMatch = cleaned.match(headerDateMatcher);
    if (headerDateMatch) currentDate = normalizeLooseDate_(headerDateMatch[2] || currentDate);

    const itemPart = String((cleaned.split(/[：:]/)[0] || '')).trim();
    const upperM = cleaned.match(/(?:上價|最高價)\s*[:：]?\s*([^，,；;\s]+)/);
    const middleM = cleaned.match(/(?:中價|平均價)\s*[:：]?\s*([^，,；;\s]+)/);
    const lowerM = cleaned.match(/(?:下價|最低價)\s*[:：]?\s*([^，,；;\s]+)/);
    const dateM = cleaned.match(headerDateMatcher);

    const upper = parseLooseNumber_(upperM ? upperM[1] : '');
    const middle = parseLooseNumber_(middleM ? middleM[1] : '');
    const lower = parseLooseNumber_(lowerM ? lowerM[1] : '');
    const marketDate = normalizeLooseDate_(dateM ? dateM[2] : currentDate);

    if (!itemPart || (upper === '' && middle === '' && lower === '')) return;

    const split = splitMarketItemAndSpec_(itemPart);
    out.push({
      item_name: split.item_name,
      spec: split.spec,
      market: currentMarket || normalizeMarketLabel_(marketHint),
      market_date: marketDate,
      upper_price: upper,
      middle_price: middle,
      lower_price: lower,
      raw_line: cleaned
    });
  });
  return out;
}

function splitMarketItemAndSpec_(label) {
  const s = String(label || '').trim();
  if (!s) return { item_name: '', spec: '' };
  const parts = s.split(/\s*[\-－–—]\s*/);
  if (parts.length <= 1) return { item_name: s, spec: '' };
  return {
    item_name: String(parts.shift() || '').trim(),
    spec: String(parts.join('-') || '').trim()
  };
}

function normalizeMarketLabel_(marketHint) {
  const raw = String(marketHint || '').trim();
  const nk = normalizeLookupKey_(raw);
  if (!nk) return '';
  if (raw === '900' || nk === '900' || nk.indexOf('pingtung') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('屏東') >= 0 || nk.indexOf('pt') === 0) return 'Pingtung';
  if (raw === '109' || nk === '109' || nk.indexOf('firstmarket') >= 0 || nk.indexOf('taipei1') >= 0 || nk.indexOf('market1') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('第一市場') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('第一果菜市場') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('台北一市') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('台北第一') >= 0) return 'FirstMarket';
  if (raw === '104' || nk === '104' || nk.indexOf('secondmarket') >= 0 || nk.indexOf('taipei2') >= 0 || nk.indexOf('market2') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('第二市場') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('第二果菜市場') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('台北二市') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('台北第二') >= 0) return 'SecondMarket';
  if (/(果菜市場|市場|market)/i.test(raw)) return raw;
  return raw;
}

function marketSortPriority_(market) {
  const raw = String(market || '').trim();
  const nk = normalizeLookupKey_(raw);
  if (nk.indexOf('pingtung') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('屏東') >= 0) return 30;
  if (nk.indexOf('secondmarket') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('第二市場') >= 0) return 20;
  if (nk.indexOf('firstmarket') >= 0 || raw.indexOf('第一市場') >= 0) return 10;
  return 0;
}


function looksLikeMarketRowObject_(obj) {
  const name = pickValueByAliases_(obj, ["item_name","name","item","product","crop","commodity","菜名","品名","名稱","作物名稱","作物","蔬菜名稱"]);
  const upper = pickValueByAliases_(obj, ["upper_price","high_price","high","upper","max_price","最高價","上價"]);
  const middle = pickValueByAliases_(obj, ["middle_price","mid_price","mid","middle","avg_price","中價","平均價"]);
  const lower = pickValueByAliases_(obj, ["lower_price","low_price","low","lower","min_price","最低價","下價"]);
  return !!String(name || "").trim() && [upper, middle, lower].some(v => String(v || "").trim() !== "");
}

function normalizeMarketRow_(obj) {
  const combinedName = String(pickValueByAliases_(obj, ['item_name','name','item','product','crop','commodity','菜名','品名','名稱','作物名稱','作物','蔬菜名稱']) || '').trim();
  if (!combinedName) return null;

  let spec = String(pickValueByAliases_(obj, ['spec','grade','size','規格','規格別','品級','品規']) || '').trim();
  let itemName = combinedName;
  if (!spec) {
    const split = splitMarketItemAndSpec_(combinedName);
    itemName = split.item_name;
    spec = split.spec;
  }

  const market = normalizeMarketLabel_(pickValueByAliases_(obj, ['market','market_name','市場','交易市場','marketLabel','market_label']) || '');
  const marketDate = normalizeLooseDate_(pickValueByAliases_(obj, ['date','market_date','trade_date','updatedAt','updated_at','日期','交易日期','更新日期','查詢日期']) || '');
  const upper = parseLooseNumber_(pickValueByAliases_(obj, ['upper_price','high_price','high','upper','max_price','最高價','上價']));
  const middle = parseLooseNumber_(pickValueByAliases_(obj, ['middle_price','mid_price','mid','middle','avg_price','中價','平均價']));
  const lower = parseLooseNumber_(pickValueByAliases_(obj, ['lower_price','low_price','low','lower','min_price','最低價','下價']));
  if (upper === '' && middle === '' && lower === '') return null;
  return { item_name: itemName, spec: spec, market: market, market_date: marketDate, upper_price: upper, middle_price: middle, lower_price: lower };
}

function pickValueByAliases_(obj, aliases) {
  const map = {};
  Object.keys(obj || {}).forEach(k => { map[normalizeLookupKey_(k)] = obj[k]; });
  for (let i = 0; i < aliases.length; i++) {
    const nk = normalizeLookupKey_(aliases[i]);
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(map, nk) && map[nk] !== "" && map[nk] !== null && map[nk] !== undefined) return map[nk];
  }
  const normKeys = Object.keys(map);
  for (let i = 0; i < aliases.length; i++) {
    const ak = normalizeLookupKey_(aliases[i]);
    const hit = normKeys.find(k => k.indexOf(ak) >= 0 || ak.indexOf(k) >= 0);
    if (hit && map[hit] !== "" && map[hit] !== null && map[hit] !== undefined) return map[hit];
  }
  return "";
}

function normalizeLookupKey_(s) {
  return String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_\-\/\\(\)\[\]\{\}\.。:：]/g, "").trim();
}

function parseLooseNumber_(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return "";
  if (typeof v === 'number') return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : "";
  const s = String(v).trim();
  if (!s || s === '-' || s === '--') return "";
  const m = s.replace(/[,，\s]/g, '').match(/-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/);
  if (!m) return "";
  const n = Number(m[0]);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : "";
}

function normalizeLooseDate_(v) {
  if (!v) return '';
  if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === '[object Date]' && !isNaN(v.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(v, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  const s = String(v).trim();
  if (!s) return '';
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s)) return s.slice(0,10);
  if (/^\d{4}[\.\/]\d{1,2}[\.\/]\d{1,2}/.test(s)) {
    const parts = s.split(/[\sT]/)[0].split(/[\.\/]/);
    return `${parts[0]}-${('0'+parts[1]).slice(-2)}-${('0'+parts[2]).slice(-2)}`;
  }
  if (/^\d{2,3}[\.\/-]\d{1,2}[\.\/-]\d{1,2}/.test(s)) {
    const parts = s.split(/[\sT]/)[0].split(/[\.\/-]/);
    const rocYear = Number(parts[0]);
    if (Number.isFinite(rocYear)) {
      const year = rocYear < 1911 ? (rocYear + 1911) : rocYear;
      return `${year}-${('0'+parts[1]).slice(-2)}-${('0'+parts[2]).slice(-2)}`;
    }
  }
  const dt = new Date(s);
  if (!isNaN(dt.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(dt, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  return s;
}

function applyReferencePricesFromRaw_(ss, rawRows, options) {
  options = options || {};
  const synSh = ensureSheet_(ss, 'Synonyms', ['raw_name','product_name','spec','product_id','enabled','note']);
  const synRows = readObjects_(synSh);
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, 'Products', ['id','name','sku','price','image','stock','category','date','cost','unit','safety_stock','supplier_ids','expiry_date','last_purchase_date','spec','reference_price','reference_price_date','reference_price_name','shop_enabled']);
  const historySh = ensureSheet_(ss, 'MarketPriceHistory', MARKET_PRICE_HISTORY_HEADERS_);
  const values = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (values.length <= 1) return { updated_count: 0, mapped_count: 0, unmatched_mapping_count: 0, history_logged_count: 0, batch_id: String(options.batch_id || '') };
  const headers = values[0].map(h => String(h || '').trim());
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  const syncTime = String(options.sync_time || Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'));
  const syncSource = String(options.sync_source || 'manual').trim() || 'manual';
  const batchId = String(options.batch_id || generateId_('MPH')).trim();

  const rawLookup = {};
  const addRawLookup_ = (key, row) => {
    const nk = normalizeLookupKey_(key);
    if (!nk) return;
    if (!rawLookup[nk]) rawLookup[nk] = [];
    rawLookup[nk].push(row);
  };

  rawRows.forEach(r => {
    const itemName = String(r.item_name || '').trim();
    const spec = String(r.spec || '').trim();
    if (!itemName) return;
    addRawLookup_(itemName, r);
    if (spec) addRawLookup_(`${itemName}-${spec}`, r);
  });

  Object.keys(rawLookup).forEach(k => rawLookup[k].sort((a, b) => {
    const dateCmp = String(b.market_date || '').localeCompare(String(a.market_date || ''));
    if (dateCmp !== 0) return dateCmp;
    return marketSortPriority_(b.market) - marketSortPriority_(a.market);
  }));

  let updatedCount = 0, mappedCount = 0, unmatchedMappingCount = 0;
  const historyByProduct = {};

  const normalizeMarketDateOnly_ = (v) => {
    const raw = String(v || '').trim();
    if (!raw) return '';
    const normalized = normalizeLooseDate_(raw);
    if (normalized) return normalized;
    const match = raw.match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
    if (match) return match[1];
    const dt = new Date(raw);
    if (!isNaN(dt.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(dt, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    return raw.slice(0, 10);
  };

  const makeHistoryDedupKey_ = (row) => {
    if (!row) return '';
    const productId = String(row.product_id || '').trim();
    const productName = String(row.product_name || '').trim();
    const productSpec = String(row.product_spec || '').trim();
    const productKey = productId || `${productName}||${productSpec}`;
    const marketDate = normalizeMarketDateOnly_(row.market_date || '');
    if (!productKey || !marketDate) return '';
    return [productKey, marketDate].join('||');
  };

  const chooseReferencePrice_ = (hit) => {
    if (hit && hit.upper_price !== '' && hit.upper_price !== null && hit.upper_price !== undefined) return hit.upper_price;
    if (hit && hit.middle_price !== '' && hit.middle_price !== null && hit.middle_price !== undefined) return hit.middle_price;
    if (hit && hit.lower_price !== '' && hit.lower_price !== null && hit.lower_price !== undefined) return hit.lower_price;
    return '';
  };

  const productHistoryKey_ = (rowNo) => {
    const pid = idx.id !== undefined ? String(values[rowNo][idx.id] || '').trim() : '';
    const pname = idx.name !== undefined ? String(values[rowNo][idx.name] || '').trim() : '';
    const pspec = idx.spec !== undefined ? String(values[rowNo][idx.spec] || '').trim() : '';
    return pid ? `id:${pid}` : `row:${rowNo}:${normalizeLookupKey_(pname)}:${normalizeLookupKey_(pspec)}`;
  };

  const buildHistoryRow_ = (rowNo, matched, hit, hitName) => {
    const productId = idx.id !== undefined ? String(values[rowNo][idx.id] || '').trim() : '';
    const productName = idx.name !== undefined ? String(values[rowNo][idx.name] || '').trim() : '';
    const productSpec = idx.spec !== undefined ? String(values[rowNo][idx.spec] || '').trim() : '';
    const referencePrice = idx.reference_price !== undefined ? parseLooseNumber_(values[rowNo][idx.reference_price]) : '';
    const referencePriceDate = idx.reference_price_date !== undefined ? String(values[rowNo][idx.reference_price_date] || '').trim() : '';
    const referencePriceName = idx.reference_price_name !== undefined ? String(values[rowNo][idx.reference_price_name] || '').trim() : '';
    const marketKey = matched && hit ? normalizeMarketLabel_(hit.market || '') : '';
    return {
      history_id: '',
      batch_id: batchId,
      sync_time: syncTime,
      sync_source: syncSource,
      product_id: productId,
      product_name: productName,
      product_spec: productSpec,
      reference_price: referencePrice,
      reference_price_date: referencePriceDate,
      reference_price_name: referencePriceName || String(hitName || ''),
      market: marketKey,
      market_label: marketKey ? marketDisplayLabel_(marketKey) : '',
      market_date: matched && hit ? String(hit.market_date || '').trim() : '',
      raw_name: matched && hit ? String(hit.item_name || '').trim() : '',
      raw_spec: matched && hit ? String(hit.spec || '').trim() : '',
      upper_price: matched && hit ? hit.upper_price : '',
      middle_price: matched && hit ? hit.middle_price : '',
      lower_price: matched && hit ? hit.lower_price : '',
      matched: matched ? '1' : '0'
    };
  };

  const setRowPrice_ = (rowNo, hit, hitName, shouldLogHistory) => {
    const pickedPrice = chooseReferencePrice_(hit);
    if (idx.reference_price !== undefined) values[rowNo][idx.reference_price] = pickedPrice;
    if (idx.reference_price_date !== undefined) values[rowNo][idx.reference_price_date] = String(hit && hit.market_date || '');
    if (idx.reference_price_name !== undefined) values[rowNo][idx.reference_price_name] = String(hitName || (hit && hit.item_name) || '');
    updatedCount++;
    if (shouldLogHistory) {
      historyByProduct[productHistoryKey_(rowNo)] = buildHistoryRow_(rowNo, true, hit, hitName);
    }
  };

  const findProductRow_ = (productId, productName, spec) => {
    const pid = String(productId || '').trim();
    const pname = String(productName || '').trim();
    const pspec = String(spec || '').trim();
    for (let r = 1; r < values.length; r++) {
      if (pid && idx.id !== undefined && String(values[r][idx.id] || '').trim() === pid) return r;
