      const rowName = idx.name !== undefined ? String(values[r][idx.name] || '').trim() : '';
      const rowSpec = idx.spec !== undefined ? String(values[r][idx.spec] || '').trim() : '';
      if (pname && rowName === pname && (!pspec || rowSpec === pspec)) return r;
    }
    return -1;
  };

  const enabledValue_ = (rowObj) => {
    const v = pickValueByAliases_(rowObj, ['enabled','enable','active','啟用','是否啟用']);
    if (v === '' || v === null || v === undefined) return true;
    const s = String(v).trim().toLowerCase();
    return !(s === '0' || s === 'false' || s === 'no' || s === 'n' || s === '停用' || s === '否');
  };

  if (synRows.length) {
    synRows.forEach(row => {
      if (!enabledValue_(row)) return;
      const rawName = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['raw_name','market_name','raw','alias','synonym','keyword','原始名稱','外部名稱','行情名稱','對照名稱','比對名稱']) || '').trim();
      const productName = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['product_name','product','商品名稱','商品','系統商品','對應商品','name']) || '').trim();
      const spec = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['spec','規格','對應規格']) || '').trim();
      const productId = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['product_id','id','商品id','商品ID']) || '').trim();
      const lookupName = rawName || productName;
      if (!lookupName) return;
      const hits = (rawLookup[normalizeLookupKey_(lookupName)] || []).filter(hit => !spec || String(hit.spec || '').trim() === spec);
      if (!hits.length) { unmatchedMappingCount++; return; }
      const rowNo = findProductRow_(productId, productName || lookupName, spec);
      if (rowNo < 0) { unmatchedMappingCount++; return; }
      mappedCount++;
      setRowPrice_(rowNo, hits[0], lookupName, true);
    });
  } else {
    for (let r = 1; r < values.length; r++) {
      const rowName = idx.name !== undefined ? String(values[r][idx.name] || '').trim() : '';
      const rowSpec = idx.spec !== undefined ? String(values[r][idx.spec] || '').trim() : '';
      if (!rowName) continue;
      const baseHits = (rawLookup[normalizeLookupKey_(rowName)] || []).filter(hit => !rowSpec || String(hit.spec || '').trim() === rowSpec);
      const combinedHits = rowSpec ? (rawLookup[normalizeLookupKey_(`${rowName}-${rowSpec}`)] || []) : [];
      const hits = baseHits.length ? baseHits : combinedHits;
      if (!hits.length) continue;
      mappedCount++;
      setRowPrice_(r, hits[0], rowName, false);
    }
  }

  prodSh.getRange(1, 1, values.length, values[0].length).setValues(values);

  const existingHistorySignatures = {};
  readObjects_(historySh).forEach(row => {
    const sig = makeHistoryDedupKey_(row);
    if (sig) existingHistorySignatures[sig] = true;
  });

  const pendingHistorySignatures = {};
  const historyRows = synRows.length ? Object.keys(historyByProduct).sort().map(key => historyByProduct[key]).filter(row => {
    const sig = makeHistoryDedupKey_(row);
    if (!sig) return false;
    if (existingHistorySignatures[sig] || pendingHistorySignatures[sig]) return false;
    pendingHistorySignatures[sig] = true;
    return true;
  }) : [];
  historyRows.forEach((row, idxRow) => {
    row.history_id = `${batchId}-${('0000' + (idxRow + 1)).slice(-4)}`;
  });
  if (historyRows.length) appendObjectsBatch_(historySh, historyRows);

  return { updated_count: updatedCount, mapped_count: mappedCount, unmatched_mapping_count: unmatchedMappingCount, history_logged_count: historyRows.length, batch_id: batchId };
}


function buildMarketPriceBoard_(ss) {
  const rawSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "PingtungRaw", PINGTUNG_RAW_HEADERS_);
  const synSh = ensureSheet_(ss, 'Synonyms', ['raw_name','product_name','spec','product_id','enabled','note']);
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, 'Products', ['id','name','sku','price','image','stock','category','date','cost','unit','safety_stock','supplier_ids','expiry_date','last_purchase_date','spec','reference_price','reference_price_date','reference_price_name','shop_enabled']);

  const rawRows = readObjects_(rawSh).map(r => ({
    sync_time: String(r.sync_time || '').trim(),
    source_file: String(r.source_file || '').trim(),
    market_date: normalizeLooseDate_(r.market_date || ''),
    item_name: String(r.item_name || '').trim(),
    spec: String(r.spec || '').trim(),
    market: normalizeMarketLabel_(r.market || ''),
    upper_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.upper_price),
    middle_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.middle_price),
    lower_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.lower_price),
    raw_json: String(r.raw_json || '')
  })).filter(r => r.item_name);
  const synRows = readObjects_(synSh);
  const products = readObjects_(prodSh).map(p => ({
    id: String(p.id || '').trim(),
    name: String(p.name || '').trim(),
    sku: String(p.sku || p.part_no || p.code || '').trim(),
    spec: String(p.spec || '').trim(),
    unit: String(p.unit || '').trim(),
    category: String(p.category || '').trim(),
    cost: parseLooseNumber_(p.cost),
    price: parseLooseNumber_(p.price),
    last_purchase_date: normalizeLooseDate_(p.last_purchase_date || '')
  })).filter(p => p.name);

  const rawByMarket = buildRawLookupByMarket_(rawRows);
  const groups = { FirstMarket: [], SecondMarket: [], Pingtung: [] };

  products.forEach(product => {
    const aliases = getProductMarketAliases_(product, synRows);
    ['FirstMarket', 'SecondMarket', 'Pingtung'].forEach(marketKey => {
      const hit = chooseLatestHitByMarket_(rawByMarket[marketKey] || {}, aliases, product.name, product.spec);
      if (!hit) return;
      groups[marketKey].push({
        product_id: product.id,
        sku: product.sku,
        product_name: product.name,
        product_spec: product.spec,
        unit: product.unit,
        category: product.category,
        cost: product.cost,
        price: product.price,
        last_purchase_date: String(product.last_purchase_date || ''),
        market: marketKey,
        market_label: marketDisplayLabel_(marketKey),
        market_date: String(hit.market_date || ''),
        raw_name: String(hit.item_name || ''),
        raw_spec: String(hit.spec || ''),
        upper_price: hit.upper_price,
        middle_price: hit.middle_price,
        lower_price: hit.lower_price
      });
    });
  });

  Object.keys(groups).forEach(k => groups[k].sort((a, b) => {
    const catCmp = String(a.category || '').localeCompare(String(b.category || ''), 'zh-Hant');
    if (catCmp !== 0) return catCmp;
    const nameCmp = String(a.product_name || '').localeCompare(String(b.product_name || ''), 'zh-Hant');
    if (nameCmp !== 0) return nameCmp;
    return String(a.product_spec || '').localeCompare(String(b.product_spec || ''), 'zh-Hant');
  }));

  const latestSync = rawRows.reduce((acc, r) => String(r.sync_time || '') > acc ? String(r.sync_time || '') : acc, '');
  const latestMarketDate = rawRows.reduce((acc, r) => String(r.market_date || '') > acc ? String(r.market_date || '') : acc, '');
  const matchedCount = Object.keys(groups).reduce((sum, k) => sum + groups[k].length, 0);

  return {
    synced_at: latestSync,
    latest_market_date: latestMarketDate,
    raw_count: rawRows.length,
    product_count: products.length,
    matched_count: matchedCount,
    groups: groups
  };
}

function buildRawLookupByMarket_(rawRows) {
  const byMarket = { FirstMarket: {}, SecondMarket: {}, Pingtung: {} };
  const addHit_ = (marketKey, key, row) => {
    if (!byMarket[marketKey]) byMarket[marketKey] = {};
    const nk = normalizeLookupKey_(key);
    if (!nk) return;
    if (!byMarket[marketKey][nk]) byMarket[marketKey][nk] = [];
    byMarket[marketKey][nk].push(row);
  };

  rawRows.forEach(row => {
    const marketKey = normalizeMarketLabel_(row.market || '');
    if (!byMarket[marketKey]) return;
    const itemName = String(row.item_name || '').trim();
    const spec = String(row.spec || '').trim();
    if (!itemName) return;
    addHit_(marketKey, itemName, row);
    if (spec) addHit_(marketKey, `${itemName}-${spec}`, row);
  });

  Object.keys(byMarket).forEach(marketKey => {
    Object.keys(byMarket[marketKey]).forEach(key => {
      byMarket[marketKey][key].sort((a, b) => compareMarketHitDesc_(a, b));
    });
  });
  return byMarket;
}

function compareMarketHitDesc_(a, b) {
  const ad = String(a && a.market_date || '');
  const bd = String(b && b.market_date || '');
  const dateCmp = bd.localeCompare(ad);
  if (dateCmp !== 0) return dateCmp;
  const as = String(a && a.spec || '');
  const bs = String(b && b.spec || '');
  if (!!bs !== !!as) return bs ? 1 : -1;
  return String(b && b.item_name || '').localeCompare(String(a && a.item_name || ''), 'zh-Hant');
}

function marketDisplayLabel_(market) {
  const key = normalizeMarketLabel_(market);
  if (key === 'FirstMarket') return '台北第一果菜市場';
  if (key === 'SecondMarket') return '台北第二果菜市場';
  if (key === 'Pingtung') return '屏東果菜市場';
  return String(market || '');
}

function getProductMarketAliases_(product, synRows) {
  const pid = String(product && product.id || '').trim();
  const pname = String(product && product.name || '').trim();
  const pspec = String(product && product.spec || '').trim();
  const out = [];
  const seen = {};
  const pushAlias_ = (rawName, spec) => {
    const rn = String(rawName || '').trim();
    const sp = String(spec || '').trim();
    if (!rn) return;
    const key = `${normalizeLookupKey_(rn)}__${normalizeLookupKey_(sp)}`;
    if (seen[key]) return;
    seen[key] = true;
    out.push({ raw_name: rn, spec: sp });
  };

  synRows.forEach(row => {
    if (!enabledSynonymRow_(row)) return;
    const targetId = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['product_id','id','商品id','商品ID']) || '').trim();
    const targetName = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['product_name','product','商品名稱','商品','系統商品','對應商品','name']) || '').trim();
    const targetSpec = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['spec','規格','對應規格']) || '').trim();
    const rawName = String(pickValueByAliases_(row, ['raw_name','market_name','raw','alias','synonym','keyword','原始名稱','外部名稱','行情名稱','對照名稱','比對名稱']) || '').trim();

    let matched = false;
    if (pid && targetId && pid === targetId) matched = true;
    if (!matched && targetName && pname === targetName) {
      matched = (!targetSpec || !pspec || targetSpec === pspec);
    }
    if (!matched) return;
    pushAlias_(rawName || targetName || pname, targetSpec || pspec);
  });

  pushAlias_(pname, pspec);
  return out;
}

function enabledSynonymRow_(rowObj) {
  const v = pickValueByAliases_(rowObj, ['enabled','enable','active','啟用','是否啟用']);
  if (v === '' || v === null || v === undefined) return true;
  const s = String(v).trim().toLowerCase();
  return !(s === '0' || s === 'false' || s === 'no' || s === 'n' || s === '停用' || s === '否');
}

function chooseLatestHitByMarket_(marketLookup, aliases, productName, productSpec) {
  let best = null;
  const candidateAliases = Array.isArray(aliases) && aliases.length ? aliases : [{ raw_name: productName, spec: productSpec }];
  candidateAliases.forEach(alias => {
    const rawName = String(alias && alias.raw_name || productName || '').trim();
    const targetSpec = String(alias && alias.spec || productSpec || '').trim();
    if (!rawName) return;

    let hits = [];
    const byName = marketLookup[normalizeLookupKey_(rawName)] || [];
    const byCombo = targetSpec ? (marketLookup[normalizeLookupKey_(`${rawName}-${targetSpec}`)] || []) : [];
    if (targetSpec) {
      const exact = byName.filter(hit => String(hit.spec || '').trim() === targetSpec);
      hits = exact.length ? exact.concat(byCombo) : byCombo.concat(byName);
    } else {
      hits = byName.concat(byCombo);
    }

    hits.forEach(hit => {
      if (!best || compareMarketHitDesc_(hit, best) < 0) {
        best = hit;
      }
    });
  });
  return best;
}

// ----------------- Members (login) -----------------
function apiMembersLogin_(p, respond) {
  const username = String(p.username || "").trim();
  const password = String(p.password || "").trim();
  if (!username || !password) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少帳號或密碼" });

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Members", ["id","name","username","password","created_at","role"]);
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const headers = rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const u = String(rows[i][idx.username] || "").trim();
    const pw = String(rows[i][idx.password] || "").trim();
    if (u === username && pw === password) {
      return respond({
        status: "ok",
        id: rows[i][idx.id],
        name: rows[i][idx.name],
        role: rows[i][idx.role] || "user"
      });
    }
  }
  return respond({ status: "error", message: "帳號或密碼錯誤" });
}


/**
 * 客戶（電商）登入：只讀取 customers（帳號密碼在 customers）
 * 注意：customers / Members 已分流，前台不可再回查 Members。
 */
function apiCustomerLogin_(p, respond) {
  const username = String(p.username || "").trim();
  const password = String(p.password || "").trim();
  if (!username || !password) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少帳號或密碼" });

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "customers", ["id","name","phone","email","address","note","created_at","username","password","role"]);
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const headers = rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  if (idx.username === undefined || idx.password === undefined) {
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "customers 工作表缺少登入欄位 username / password" });
  }

  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const u = String(rows[i][idx.username] || "").trim();
    const pw = String(rows[i][idx.password] || "").trim();
    if (u === username && pw === password) {
      return respond({
        status: "ok",
        id: rows[i][idx.id],
        name: rows[i][idx.name],
        phone: idx.phone !== undefined ? String(rows[i][idx.phone] || "").trim() : "",
        email: idx.email !== undefined ? String(rows[i][idx.email] || "").trim() : "",
        address: idx.address !== undefined ? String(rows[i][idx.address] || "").trim() : "",
        role: rows[i][idx.role] || "customer"
      });
    }
  }

  return respond({ status: "error", message: "帳號或密碼錯誤" });
}

/**
 * 客戶（電商）註冊：寫入 customers（帳號密碼在 customers）
 * 允許：電商平台註冊、後台新增 customers
 */
function apiCustomerRegister_(p, respond) {
  const name = String(p.name || "").trim();
  const username = String(p.username || "").trim();
  const password = String(p.password || "").trim();

  if (!name || !username || !password) {
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少註冊資料" });
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "customers", ["id","name","phone","email","address","note","created_at","username","password","role"]);
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const headers = rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  // 帳號重複檢查（customers.username）
  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const u = String(rows[i][idx.username] || "").trim();
    if (u === username) {
      return respond({ status: "error", message: "帳號已存在" });
    }
  }


  // 也避免與後台 Members 帳號重複（避免同帳號造成混淆）
  const memSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Members", ["id","name","username","password","created_at","role"]);
  const memRows = memSh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const memHeaders = memRows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const midx = headerIndexMap_(memHeaders);
  for (let i = 1; i < memRows.length; i++) {
    const u2 = String(memRows[i][midx.username] || "").trim();
    if (u2 === username) {
      return respond({ status: "error", message: "此帳號已被後台使用者使用，請更換" });
    }
  }

  const id = generateId_("C");
  appendObject_(sh, {
    id: id,
    customer_id: id,
    name: name,
    username: username,
    password: password,
    role: "customer",
    phone: String(p.phone || "").trim(),
    email: String(p.email || "").trim(),
    address: String(p.address || "").trim(),
    note: String(p.note || "").trim(),
    created_at: new Date()
  });

  return respond({ status: "ok", message: "註冊成功", id: id });
}

/**
 * 後台使用者（Members）管理：後台列出/新增/修改/刪除
 * - action=list | add | update | delete
 */
function apiManageMember_(p, respond){
  const action = String(p.action || "").trim();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Members", ["id","name","username","password","created_at","role"]);
  const headers = getHeaders_(sh);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  if (action === "list") {
    const list = readObjects_(sh);
    return respond({ status: "ok", data: list });
  }

  if (action === "add") {
    const name = String(p.name || "").trim();
    const username = String(p.username || "").trim();
    const password = String(p.password || "").trim();
    const role = String(p.role || "user").trim() || "user";
    if (!name || !username || !password) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少使用者資料" });

    // username 重複檢查
    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
      const u = String(rows[i][idx.username] || "").trim();
      if (u === username) return respond({ status: "error", message: "帳號已存在" });
    }

    const id = String(p.id || "").trim() || generateId_("M");
    appendObject_(sh, {
      id: id,
      name: name,
      username: username,
      password: password,
      created_at: new Date(),
      role: role
    });
    return respond({ status: "ok", message: "使用者新增成功", id: id });
  }

  if (action === "update") {
    const id = String(p.id || "").trim();
    if (!id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少使用者 id" });

    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.id]).trim() === id) {
        if (p.name !== undefined && idx.name !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.name + 1).setValue(p.name);
        if (p.username !== undefined && idx.username !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.username + 1).setValue(p.username);
        if (p.password !== undefined && idx.password !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.password + 1).setValue(p.password);
        if (p.role !== undefined && idx.role !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.role + 1).setValue(p.role);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "使用者更新成功" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到使用者" });
  }


  if (action === "delete") {
    const id = String(p.id || "").trim();
    if (!id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少使用者 id" });

    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = rows.length - 1; r >= 1; r--) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.id]).trim() === id) {
        sh.deleteRow(r + 1);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "使用者刪除成功" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到使用者" });
  }

  return respond({ status: "error", message: "Invalid action" });
}


// ----------------- Products -----------------
function apiProducts_(respond) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);
  try { repairProductPricingFields_(ss, sh); } catch (e) {}
  const list = readObjects_(sh).map(o => {
    // 單一來源：只回傳 supplier_ids（供應商代碼，多選用逗號分隔）
    const sid = String(o.supplier_ids || o.supplier_id || "").trim();
    o.supplier_ids = sid;
    delete o.supplier_id;
    delete o.supplier_name;
    delete o.supplier_names;
    o.price = sanitizeProductSalePrice_(o.price, 0);
    o.cost = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(o.cost, 0, 6);
    o.reference_price = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(o.reference_price, 0, 6);
    o.safety_stock = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(o.safety_stock, 0, 6);
    o.shop_enabled = normalizeShopEnabled_(o.shop_enabled, true);
    return o;
  });
  return respond(list);
}

function apiManageProduct_(p, respond) {
  const action = String(p.action || "").trim(); // add/update/delete
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);
  const headers = getHeaders_(sh);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  if (action === "add") {
    const name = String(p.name || "").trim();
    if (!name) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 name" });

    const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
    let newId = 1;
    if (lastRow > 1) {
      const idCol = idx.id + 1;
      const vals = sh.getRange(2, idCol, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
      const nums = vals.map(v => Number(String(v).trim())).filter(v => !isNaN(v));
      newId = (nums.length ? Math.max.apply(null, nums) : 0) + 1;
    }
    const now = new Date();
    
const costIn = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.cost, 0, 6);
const stockIn = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.stock, 0, 6);
const priceIn = sanitizeProductSalePrice_(p.price, 0);
const priceRaw = (p.price === undefined || p.price === null) ? "" : String(p.price).trim();
let finalPrice = priceIn;
// 售價預設帶入成本（避免被誤帶成庫存數量），並保證售價不會寫入負數。
if ((!priceRaw || priceRaw === "0" || finalPrice === 0) && costIn > 0) finalPrice = costIn;
if (priceRaw && Number(priceRaw) < 0) finalPrice = costIn > 0 ? costIn : 0;
if (finalPrice === stockIn && costIn > 0 && finalPrice !== costIn) finalPrice = costIn;
finalPrice = sanitizeProductSalePrice_(finalPrice, costIn > 0 ? costIn : 0);

const obj = {
  id: newId,
  name: String(p.name || "").trim(),
  sku: String(p.sku || "").trim(),
  price: finalPrice,
  image: String(p.image || "").trim(),
  stock: stockIn,
  category: String(p.category || "").trim(),
  date: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"),
  cost: costIn,
  unit: String(p.unit || "").trim(),
  safety_stock: sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.safety_stock, 0, 6),
  supplier_ids: String(p.supplier_ids || p.supplier_id || "").trim(),
  spec: String(p.spec || "").trim(),
  shop_enabled: normalizeShopEnabled_(p.shop_enabled, true)
};
    appendObject_(sh, obj);
    return respond({ status: "ok", message: "商品新增成功", id: newId });
  }

  if (action === "update") {
    const id = String(p.id || "").trim();
    if (!id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少商品 id" });

    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.id]).trim() === id) {
        const row = rows[r].slice();
        if (p.name !== undefined && idx.name !== undefined) row[idx.name] = p.name;
        if (p.sku !== undefined && idx.sku !== undefined) row[idx.sku] = p.sku;
        if (p["料號"] !== undefined && idx.sku !== undefined) row[idx.sku] = p["料號"];
        if (p.price !== undefined && idx.price !== undefined) {
          const currentStock = (idx.stock !== undefined) ? sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(rows[r][idx.stock] || 0, 0, 6) : 0;
          const currentCost  = (idx.cost  !== undefined) ? sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(rows[r][idx.cost]  || 0, 0, 6) : 0;
          const nextStock = (p.stock !== undefined) ? sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.stock, currentStock, 6) : currentStock;
          const nextCost  = (p.cost  !== undefined) ? sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.cost, currentCost, 6) : currentCost;
          const priceRaw = (p.price === undefined || p.price === null) ? "" : String(p.price).trim();
          let nextPrice = sanitizeProductSalePrice_(p.price, 0);
          if ((!priceRaw || priceRaw === "0" || nextPrice === 0) && nextCost > 0) nextPrice = nextCost;
          if (priceRaw && Number(priceRaw) < 0) nextPrice = nextCost > 0 ? nextCost : 0;
          if (nextPrice === nextStock && nextCost > 0 && nextPrice !== nextCost) nextPrice = nextCost;
          row[idx.price] = sanitizeProductSalePrice_(nextPrice, nextCost > 0 ? nextCost : 0);
        }
        if (p.cost !== undefined && idx.cost !== undefined) row[idx.cost] = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.cost, 0, 6);
        if (p.stock !== undefined && idx.stock !== undefined) row[idx.stock] = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.stock, 0, 6);
        if (p.reference_price !== undefined && idx.reference_price !== undefined) row[idx.reference_price] = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.reference_price, 0, 6);
        if (p.reference_price_date !== undefined && idx.reference_price_date !== undefined) row[idx.reference_price_date] = String(p.reference_price_date || "").trim();
        if (p.last_purchase_date !== undefined && idx.last_purchase_date !== undefined) row[idx.last_purchase_date] = String(p.last_purchase_date || "").trim();
        if (p.category !== undefined && idx.category !== undefined) row[idx.category] = p.category;
        if (p.unit !== undefined && idx.unit !== undefined) row[idx.unit] = p.unit;
        if (p.spec !== undefined && idx.spec !== undefined) row[idx.spec] = String(p.spec || "").trim();
        if (p.shop_enabled !== undefined && idx.shop_enabled !== undefined) row[idx.shop_enabled] = normalizeShopEnabled_(p.shop_enabled, true);
        if (p.safety_stock !== undefined && idx.safety_stock !== undefined) row[idx.safety_stock] = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.safety_stock, 0, 6);
        if (p.safety !== undefined && idx.safety_stock !== undefined) row[idx.safety_stock] = sanitizeNonNegativeNumber_(p.safety, 0, 6);
        // 供應商：只寫 supplier_ids（代碼、可多個用逗號）
        if (p.supplier_ids !== undefined) {
          const v = String(p.supplier_ids || "").trim();
          if (!v) return respond({ status: "error", message: "supplier_ids 不可為空" });
          if (idx.supplier_ids !== undefined) row[idx.supplier_ids] = v;
          // 若表內仍有舊欄位，順便清空避免混淆
          if (idx.supplier_id !== undefined) row[idx.supplier_id] = "";
          if (idx.supplier_name !== undefined) row[idx.supplier_name] = "";
          if (idx.supplier_names !== undefined) row[idx.supplier_names] = "";
        }
        if (p.expiry_date !== undefined && idx.expiry_date !== undefined) row[idx.expiry_date] = String(p.expiry_date || "").trim();
        sh.getRange(r + 1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([row.slice(0, headers.length)]);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "商品已更新" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到該商品" });
  }

  if (action === "delete") {
    const id = String(p.id || "").trim();
    if (!id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少商品 id" });
    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.id]).trim() === id) {
        sh.deleteRow(r + 1);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "商品已刪除" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到該商品" });
  }

  return respond({ status: "error", message: "未知 action" });
}

// ----------------- Suppliers -----------------
function apiSuppliers_(respond) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  // 兼容既有表名可能帶空白
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "suppliers", ["id","name","phone","email","address","note","created_at"]);
  const raw = readObjects_(sh);
  const data = raw.map(x => ({
    id: x.id || x.supplier_id || x.supplierId || x["supplier id"] || "",
    name: x.name || "",
    phone: x.phone || "",
    email: x.email || "",
    address: x.address || "",
    note: x.note || "",
    created_at: x.created_at || x.createdAt || ""
  })).filter(x => String(x.name || "").trim() !== "");
  return respond({ status: "ok", data });
}

function apiManageSupplier_(p, respond) {
  const action = String(p.action || "").trim();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "suppliers", ["id","name","phone","email","address","note","created_at"]);
  const headers = getHeaders_(sh);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  if (action === "add") {
    const supplier = p.supplier ? mustJson_(p.supplier) : null;
    const name = String((supplier && supplier.name) || p.name || "").trim();
    if (!name) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少供應商名稱" });

    const lock = LockService.getDocumentLock();
    lock.waitLock(30000);
    try {
      const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
        if (String(rows[r][idx.name] || "").trim() === name) {
          return respond({ status: "error", message: "供應商名稱已存在" });
        }
      }

      // 新規則：供應商 ID 固定由後端產生為 yyyyMMdd + 3 位流水，例如 20260317001
      const id = generateSupplierDailyId_(sh, new Date());
      const obj = {
        id: id,
        supplier_id: id,
        name: name,
        phone: (supplier && supplier.phone) || p.phone || "",
        email: (supplier && supplier.email) || p.email || "",
        address: (supplier && supplier.address) || p.address || "",
        note: (supplier && supplier.note) || p.note || "",
        created_at: new Date()
      };
      appendObject_(sh, obj);
      return respond({ status: "ok", message: "供應商新增成功", id: id });
    } finally {
      try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }

  if (action === "update") {
    const id = String(p.id || "").trim();
    if (!id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少供應商 id" });

    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.id]).trim() === id) {
        if (p.name !== undefined && idx.name !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.name + 1).setValue(p.name);
        if (p.sku !== undefined && idx.sku !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.sku + 1).setValue(p.sku);
        if (p["料號"] !== undefined && idx.sku !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.sku + 1).setValue(p["料號"]);
        if (p.phone !== undefined && idx.phone !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.phone + 1).setValue(p.phone);
        if (p.email !== undefined && idx.email !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.email + 1).setValue(p.email);
        if (p.address !== undefined && idx.address !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.address + 1).setValue(p.address);
        if (p.note !== undefined && idx.note !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.note + 1).setValue(p.note);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "供應商已更新" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到供應商" });
  }

  if (action === "delete") {
    const id = String(p.id || "").trim();
    if (!id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少供應商 id" });

    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.id]).trim() === id) {
        sh.deleteRow(r + 1);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "供應商已刪除" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到供應商" });
  }

  return respond({ status: "error", message: "未知 action" });
}

// ----------------- Purchases (進貨) -----------------
// ----------------- Customers -----------------
function apiCustomers_(respond){
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "customers", ["id","name","phone","email","address","note","created_at","username","password","role"]);
  const list = readObjects_(sh);
  return respond({ status: "ok", data: list });
}

function apiManageCustomer_(p, respond){
  const action = String(p.action || "").trim();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "customers", ["id","name","phone","email","address","note","created_at","username","password","role"]);
  const headers = getHeaders_(sh);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  if (action === "add"){
    const customer = p.customer ? mustJson_(p.customer) : null;
    const name = String((customer && customer.name) || p.name || "").trim();
    if (!name) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少客戶名稱" });

    const id = String((customer && customer.id) || p.id || generateId_("C")).trim();
    const obj = {
      id: id,
      customer_id: id,
      name: name,
      username: (customer && customer.username) || p.username || "",
      password: (customer && customer.password) || p.password || "",
      role: (customer && customer.role) || p.role || "customer",
      phone: (customer && customer.phone) || p.phone || "",
      email: (customer && customer.email) || p.email || "",
      address: (customer && customer.address) || p.address || "",
      note: (customer && customer.note) || p.note || "",
      created_at: new Date()
    };
    appendObject_(sh, obj);
    return respond({ status: "ok", message: "客戶新增成功", id: id });
  }

  if (action === "update"){
    const id = String(p.id || "").trim();
    if (!id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少客戶 id" });

    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++){
      if (String(rows[r][idx.id]).trim() === id){
        if (p.name !== undefined && idx.name !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.name + 1).setValue(p.name);
        if (p.username !== undefined && idx.username !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.username + 1).setValue(p.username);
        if (p.password !== undefined && idx.password !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.password + 1).setValue(p.password);
        if (p.role !== undefined && idx.role !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.role + 1).setValue(p.role);
        if (p.phone !== undefined && idx.phone !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.phone + 1).setValue(p.phone);
        if (p.address !== undefined && idx.address !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.address + 1).setValue(p.address);
        if (p.note !== undefined && idx.note !== undefined) sh.getRange(r + 1, idx.note + 1).setValue(p.note);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "客戶更新成功" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到客戶" });
  }

  if (action === "delete"){
    const id = String(p.id || "").trim();
    if (!id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少客戶 id" });
    const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = rows.length - 1; r >= 1; r--){
      if (String(rows[r][idx.id]).trim() === id){
        sh.deleteRow(r + 1);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "客戶刪除成功" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到客戶" });
  }

  return respond({ status: "error", message: "Invalid action" });
}


function purchaseHeaders_(){
  return ["po_id","date","arrival_date","form_no","form_name","supplier_id","supplier_name","total","items","expiry_date","status","operator","created_at","source_order_id","source_order_ids","stock_applied","completed_at","auto_generated"];
}

function buildOrderLookupMaps_(ss) {
  const sh = ensureOrdersSheet_(ss);
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const out = {
    linkedPurchaseToOrderId: {},
    orderIdToDate: {},
    orderIdToCreatedDate: {},
    orderIdToShippingDate: {},
    orderIdToMeta: {}
  };
  if (rows.length <= 1) return out;
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim()));
  const orderIdIdx = idx.order_id;
  const linkedPurchaseIdx = idx.linked_purchase_id;
  const dateIdx = idx.date;
  const shippingDateIdx = idx.shipping_date;
  for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const row = rows[r] || [];
    const orderId = String(orderIdIdx !== undefined ? (row[orderIdIdx] || "") : "").trim();
    if (!orderId) continue;
    const shippingDate = shippingDateIdx !== undefined ? toISODateLocal_(row[shippingDateIdx] || "") : "";
    const orderDate = dateIdx !== undefined ? toISODateLocal_(row[dateIdx] || "") : "";
    out.orderIdToDate[orderId] = shippingDate || orderDate || "";
    out.orderIdToCreatedDate[orderId] = orderDate || "";
    out.orderIdToShippingDate[orderId] = shippingDate || "";
    out.orderIdToMeta[orderId] = {
      order_id: orderId,
      created_date: orderDate || "",
      shipping_date: shippingDate || ""
    };
    if (linkedPurchaseIdx !== undefined) {
      const linkedPurchaseIds = splitCsvIds_(row[linkedPurchaseIdx]);
      linkedPurchaseIds.forEach(function(linkedPurchaseId){
        if (linkedPurchaseId && !out.linkedPurchaseToOrderId[linkedPurchaseId]) {
          out.linkedPurchaseToOrderId[linkedPurchaseId] = orderId;
        }
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function findOrderIdByLinkedPurchaseId_(ss, poId, lookup){
  const target = String(poId || "").trim();
  if (!target) return "";
  if (lookup && lookup.linkedPurchaseToOrderId) {
    return String(lookup.linkedPurchaseToOrderId[target] || "").trim();
  }
  const built = buildOrderLookupMaps_(ss);
  return String(built.linkedPurchaseToOrderId[target] || "").trim();
}

function splitCsvIds_(value) {
  const seen = {};
  return String(value == null ? "" : value)
    .split(",")
    .map(function(part){ return String(part || "").trim(); })
    .filter(function(part){
      if (!part) return false;
      if (seen[part]) return false;
      seen[part] = true;
      return true;
    });
}

function joinCsvIds_(ids) {
  return splitCsvIds_((Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : [ids]).join(",")).join(",");
}

function ensurePurchasesSheet_(ss){
  const expected = purchaseHeaders_();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "purchases", expected);
  const current = getHeaders_(sh);
  const normalizedCurrent = current.map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const hasDup = normalizedCurrent.some((h, i) => !!h && normalizedCurrent.indexOf(h) !== i);
  const sameOrder = expected.length === normalizedCurrent.length && expected.every((h, i) => normalizedCurrent[i] === h);
  if (sameOrder && !hasDup) return sh;

  const vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const currentHeaders = (vals[0] || []).map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const firstIdx = {};
  currentHeaders.forEach((h, i) => {
    if (h && firstIdx[h] === undefined) firstIdx[h] = i;
  });
  const aliases = {
    po_id: ["po_id", "purchase_id", "purchaseId", "id", "ref_id"],
    date: ["date"],
    arrival_date: ["arrival_date", "receive_date"],
    form_no: ["form_no", "form_code"],
    form_name: ["form_name", "form_title"],
    supplier_id: ["supplier_id", "supplierId", "supplier"],
    supplier_name: ["supplier_name", "supplierName"],
    total: ["total"],
    items: ["items"],
    expiry_date: ["expiry_date"],
    status: ["status"],
    operator: ["operator"],
    created_at: ["created_at"],
    source_order_id: ["source_order_id"],
    source_order_ids: ["source_order_ids","source_orders","source_order_list"],
    stock_applied: ["stock_applied"],
    completed_at: ["completed_at"],
    auto_generated: ["auto_generated"]
  };

  const orderLookup = buildOrderLookupMaps_(ss);
  const out = [expected.slice()];
  for (let r = 1; r < vals.length; r++) {
    const row = vals[r] || [];
    if (row.every(v => v === "" || v === null)) continue;
    const obj = {};
    expected.forEach((h) => {
      const names = aliases[h] || [h];
      let hitIdx = -1;
      for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
        const idx = firstIdx[names[i]];
        if (idx !== undefined) { hitIdx = idx; break; }
      }
      obj[h] = hitIdx >= 0 ? row[hitIdx] : "";
    });

    if (!String(obj.form_no || "").trim()) {
      const info = getPurchaseFormInfoByCategory_(String(obj.form_name || "").trim());
      obj.form_no = info.form_no;
    }
    if (!String(obj.form_name || "").trim()) {
      const info = getPurchaseFormInfoByCategory_(String(obj.form_name || "").trim());
      obj.form_name = info.form_name;
    }
    obj.source_order_ids = String(obj.source_order_ids || "").trim();
    if (!obj.source_order_ids && String(obj.source_order_id || "").trim()) {
      obj.source_order_ids = String(obj.source_order_id || "").trim();
    }
    if (!String(obj.source_order_id || "").trim() && obj.source_order_ids) {
      obj.source_order_id = splitCsvIds_(obj.source_order_ids)[0] || "";
    }
    if (!String(obj.source_order_id || "").trim() && Number(obj.auto_generated || 0)) {
      const linkedOrderId = findOrderIdByLinkedPurchaseId_(ss, obj.po_id, orderLookup);
      if (linkedOrderId) {
        obj.source_order_id = linkedOrderId;
        if (!obj.source_order_ids) obj.source_order_ids = linkedOrderId;
      }
    }

    out.push(expected.map(h => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, h) ? obj[h] : ""));
  }

  const maxRows = sh.getMaxRows();
  const maxCols = sh.getMaxColumns();
  if (maxRows < out.length) sh.insertRowsAfter(maxRows, out.length - maxRows);
  if (maxCols < expected.length) sh.insertColumnsAfter(maxCols, expected.length - maxCols);
  sh.clearContents();
  sh.getRange(1, 1, out.length, expected.length).setValues(out);
  if (sh.getMaxColumns() > expected.length) {
    sh.deleteColumns(expected.length + 1, sh.getMaxColumns() - expected.length);
  }
  return sh;
}

function orderHeaders_(){
  return ["order_id","name","phone","address","shipping_date","total","items","date","member_id","status","operator","source","stock_applied","linked_purchase_id","request_token","remark","created_at"];
}

function ensureOrdersSheet_(ss){
  const expected = orderHeaders_();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Orders", expected);
  const current = getHeaders_(sh);
  const normalizedCurrent = current.map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const hasDup = normalizedCurrent.some((h, i) => !!h && normalizedCurrent.indexOf(h) !== i);
  const sameOrder = expected.length === normalizedCurrent.length && expected.every((h, i) => normalizedCurrent[i] === h);
  if (sameOrder && !hasDup) return sh;

  const vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const currentHeaders = (vals[0] || []).map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const firstIdx = {};
  currentHeaders.forEach((h, i) => {
    if (h && firstIdx[h] === undefined) firstIdx[h] = i;
  });
  const aliases = {
    order_id: ["order_id", "orderId", "id"],
    name: ["name", "customer_name", "customer", "receiver"],
    phone: ["phone", "tel", "mobile"],
    address: ["address", "addr", "delivery_address", "shipping_address"],
    shipping_date: ["shipping_date", "ship_date", "delivery_date"],
    total: ["total", "amount"],
    items: ["items", "detail", "item_list"],
    date: ["date", "order_date", "created_date"],
    member_id: ["member_id", "memberId", "customer_id"],
    status: ["status"],
    operator: ["operator", "user"],
    source: ["source", "channel"],
    stock_applied: ["stock_applied", "stockApplied"],
    linked_purchase_id: ["linked_purchase_id", "linkedPurchaseId", "purchase_id"],
    request_token: ["request_token", "requestToken", "token"],
    remark: ["remark", "note", "memo"],
    created_at: ["created_at", "createdAt"]
  };

  const out = [expected.slice()];
  for (let r = 1; r < vals.length; r++) {
    const row = vals[r] || [];
    if (row.every(v => v === "" || v === null)) continue;
    const obj = {};
    expected.forEach((h) => {
      const names = aliases[h] || [h];
      let hitIdx = -1;
      for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
        const idx = firstIdx[names[i]];
        if (idx !== undefined) { hitIdx = idx; break; }
      }
      obj[h] = hitIdx >= 0 ? row[hitIdx] : "";
    });

    const existingDate = toISODateLocal_(obj.date || "");
    const existingShippingDate = toISODateLocal_(obj.shipping_date || "");
    obj.date = existingDate;
    obj.shipping_date = existingShippingDate || existingDate || "";
    obj.stock_applied = Number(obj.stock_applied || 0) ? 1 : 0;
    obj.order_id = String(obj.order_id || "").trim();
    obj.linked_purchase_id = String(obj.linked_purchase_id || "").trim();
    obj.request_token = String(obj.request_token || "").trim();
    obj.remark = String(obj.remark || "").trim();

    out.push(expected.map(h => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, h) ? obj[h] : ""));
  }

  const maxRows = sh.getMaxRows();
  const maxCols = sh.getMaxColumns();
  if (maxRows < out.length) sh.insertRowsAfter(maxRows, out.length - maxRows);
  if (maxCols < expected.length) sh.insertColumnsAfter(maxCols, expected.length - maxCols);
  sh.clearContents();
  sh.getRange(1, 1, out.length, expected.length).setValues(out);
  if (sh.getMaxColumns() > expected.length) {
    sh.deleteColumns(expected.length + 1, sh.getMaxColumns() - expected.length);
  }
  return sh;
}

function normalizeOrderRecordForResponse_(obj) {
  const x = Object.assign({}, obj || {});
  x.date = toISODateLocal_(x.date || "");
  const rawShippingDate = toISODateLocal_(x.shipping_date || "");
  const rawCreatedAt = x.created_at || x.createdAt || "";
  let shippingDate = rawShippingDate;

  // 舊資料修復：過去部份銷貨單尚未獨立 shipping_date，實際日期只留在 date。
  // 僅在 shipping_date 為空時補回，之後前端仍然只讀 shipping_date，不再混用新資料流。
  if (!shippingDate && x.date) {
    shippingDate = x.date;
    x.__legacy_shipping_date_backfilled = 1;
  }

  x.shipping_date = shippingDate;
  x.created_at = rawCreatedAt ? toLocalDateTime_(rawCreatedAt) : "";

  if (x.items && typeof x.items === "string") {
    try { x.items = JSON.parse(x.items); } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(x.items)) x.items = [];
  x.stock_applied = Number(x.stock_applied || 0) ? 1 : 0;
  x.source = String(x.source || "").trim();
  x.linked_purchase_id = String(x.linked_purchase_id || "").trim();
  return x;
}

function persistLegacyOrderShippingDates_(sheet, idx, list) {
  if (!sheet || !idx || idx.shipping_date === undefined) return;
  const updates = [];
  (Array.isArray(list) ? list : []).forEach(function(x) {
    const rowNo = Number(x && x.__rowNo || 0);
    if (!rowNo) return;
    if (!Number(x.__legacy_shipping_date_backfilled || 0)) return;
    const shippingDate = toISODateLocal_(x.shipping_date || "");
    if (!shippingDate) return;
    updates.push({ rowNo: rowNo, value: shippingDate });
  });
  updates.forEach(function(u) {
    try { sheet.getRange(u.rowNo, idx.shipping_date + 1).setValue(u.value); } catch (e) {}
  });
}

function getOrderDateById_(ss, orderId, lookup) {
  const target = String(orderId || "").trim();
  if (!target) return "";
  if (lookup && lookup.orderIdToDate) {
    return String(lookup.orderIdToDate[target] || "").trim();
  }
  const built = buildOrderLookupMaps_(ss);
  return String(built.orderIdToDate[target] || "").trim();
}

function getOrderMetaById_(ss, orderId, lookup) {
  const target = String(orderId || "").trim();
  if (!target) return { order_id: "", created_date: "", shipping_date: "" };
  if (lookup && lookup.orderIdToMeta && lookup.orderIdToMeta[target]) {
    return lookup.orderIdToMeta[target];
  }
  const built = buildOrderLookupMaps_(ss);
  return built.orderIdToMeta && built.orderIdToMeta[target]
    ? built.orderIdToMeta[target]
    : { order_id: target, created_date: "", shipping_date: "" };
}

function getPurchaseFormInfoByCategory_(category){
  const s = String(category || "").trim();
  if (/冷凍/.test(s)) return { form_no: "F-02-B-01-2", form_name: "冷凍食品類" };
  if (/(乾貨|素料)/.test(s)) return { form_no: "F-02-B-01-4", form_name: "乾貨素料類" };
  if (/(南北|雜貨|包材)/.test(s)) return { form_no: "F-02-B-01-3", form_name: "南北雜貨包材類" };
  return { form_no: "F-02-B-01-1", form_name: "生鮮蔬果類" };
}

function getPurchaseFormInfoBySkuPrefix_(sku){
  const prefix = String(sku || "").trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase();
  if (prefix === "A") return { form_no: "F-02-B-01-1", form_name: "生鮮蔬果類" };
  if (prefix === "B") return { form_no: "F-02-B-01-2", form_name: "冷凍食品類" };
  if (prefix === "C") return { form_no: "F-02-B-01-3", form_name: "南北雜貨包材類" };
  if (prefix === "D") return { form_no: "F-02-B-01-4", form_name: "乾貨素料類" };
  return null;
}

function purchaseItemSupplierSortKey_(item) {
  const supplierName = String(item && item.supplier_name || '').trim();
  const supplierId = String(item && item.supplier_id || '').trim();
  const productName = String(item && (item.product_name || item.name) || '').trim();
  const sku = String(item && item.sku || '').trim();
  return { supplierName: supplierName, supplierId: supplierId, productName: productName, sku: sku };
}

function sortPurchaseItemsBySupplier_(items) {
  const list = Array.isArray(items) ? items.map(function(it, idx){
    return { item: (it && typeof it === 'object') ? Object.assign({}, it) : it, index: idx };
  }) : [];
  list.sort(function(a, b){
    const ak = purchaseItemSupplierSortKey_(a.item);
    const bk = purchaseItemSupplierSortKey_(b.item);
    const supplierNameCmp = String(ak.supplierName || '未指定供應商').localeCompare(String(bk.supplierName || '未指定供應商'), 'zh-Hant');
    if (supplierNameCmp !== 0) return supplierNameCmp;
    const supplierIdCmp = String(ak.supplierId || '').localeCompare(String(bk.supplierId || ''), 'zh-Hant');
    if (supplierIdCmp !== 0) return supplierIdCmp;
    const productNameCmp = String(ak.productName || '').localeCompare(String(bk.productName || ''), 'zh-Hant');
    if (productNameCmp !== 0) return productNameCmp;
    const skuCmp = String(ak.sku || '').localeCompare(String(bk.sku || ''), 'zh-Hant');
    if (skuCmp !== 0) return skuCmp;
    return a.index - b.index;
  });
  return list.map(function(entry){ return entry.item; });
}

function buildPurchaseGroupsBySku_(items, defaultInfo){
  const groups = {};
  (Array.isArray(items) ? items : []).forEach(function(it){
    const mapped = getPurchaseFormInfoBySkuPrefix_(it && it.sku);
    const info = mapped || defaultInfo || { form_no: "F-02-B-01-1", form_name: "生鮮蔬果類" };
    const key = String(info.form_no || "F-02-B-01-1");
    if (!groups[key]) groups[key] = { form_no: info.form_no, form_name: info.form_name, items: [] };
    groups[key].items.push(it);
  });
  return Object.keys(groups).sort().map(function(key){
    return {
      form_no: groups[key].form_no,
      form_name: groups[key].form_name,
      items: sortPurchaseItemsBySupplier_(groups[key].items)
    };
  });
}

function findOrderByRequestToken_(orders, requestToken){
  const token = String(requestToken || "").trim();
  if (!token) return null;
  for (var i = 0; i < orders.length; i++) {
    if (String(orders[i].request_token || "").trim() === token) return orders[i];
  }
  return null;
}

function parseSupplierIdsFromProductRow_(row, pidx){
  const set = {};
  if (pidx.supplier_id !== undefined) {
    const v = String(row[pidx.supplier_id] || "").trim();
    if (v) set[v] = true;
  }
  if (pidx.supplier_ids !== undefined) {
    const raw = String(row[pidx.supplier_ids] || "").trim();
    if (raw) raw.split(",").map(s => String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(v => set[v] = true);
  }
  return Object.keys(set);
}

function buildSupplierNameMap_(ss){
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "suppliers", ["id","name","phone","email","address","note","created_at"]);
  const vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const h = vals[0].map(x => String(x || "").trim());
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(h);
  const map = {};
  for (let r = 1; r < vals.length; r++) {
    const id = String(vals[r][idx.id] || "").trim();
    const name = String(vals[r][idx.name] || "").trim();
    if (id) map[id] = name;
  }
  return map;
}

function setSheetRowByObject_(sheet, rowNumber, obj){
  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const current = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, headers.length).getValues()[0];
  const row = current.slice();
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    if (h && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, h)) row[i] = obj[h];
  });
  sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([row]);
}

function normalizePurchaseItemCostFields_(item){
  const rawText = String(item && item.cost_raw !== undefined && item.cost_raw !== null ? item.cost_raw : "").trim();
  let costText = rawText;
  if (!costText) {
    const fallbackText = String(item && item.cost !== undefined && item.cost !== null ? item.cost : "").trim();
    if (fallbackText && fallbackText !== "0" && fallbackText !== "0.0" && fallbackText !== "0.00") {
      costText = fallbackText;
    }
  }
  const hasCostInput = costText !== "";
  return {
    hasCostInput: hasCostInput,
    cost_raw: hasCostInput ? costText : "",
    cost: hasCostInput ? cleanDecimalInput_(costText, 6) : ""
  };
}

function buildProductLookupMaps_(prodVals, pidx) {
  const byId = {};
  const bySku = {};
  for (let r = 1; r < prodVals.length; r++) {
    const row = prodVals[r] || [];
    const id = pidx.id !== undefined ? String(row[pidx.id] || "").trim() : "";
    const sku = pidx.sku !== undefined ? String(row[pidx.sku] || "").trim() : "";
    const hit = { r: r, row: row, id: id, sku: sku };
    if (id) byId[id] = hit;
    if (sku) bySku[sku] = hit;
  }
  return { byId: byId, bySku: bySku };
}

function findProductHitBySkuOrId_(lookup, item) {
  const it = item || {};
  const skuCandidates = [it.sku, it.product_sku, it.SKU, it.item_sku, it.code, it.id, it.product_id, it.raw_id]
    .map(function(v){ return String(v || "").trim(); })
    .filter(Boolean);
  for (let i = 0; i < skuCandidates.length; i++) {
    const hit = lookup && lookup.bySku ? lookup.bySku[skuCandidates[i]] : null;
    if (hit) return hit;
  }
  const idCandidates = [it.product_id, it.raw_id, it.id]
    .map(function(v){ return String(v || "").trim(); })
    .filter(Boolean);
  for (let i = 0; i < idCandidates.length; i++) {
    const hit = lookup && lookup.byId ? lookup.byId[idCandidates[i]] : null;
    if (hit) return hit;
  }
  return null;
}

function productLookupKeyText_(item) {
  const it = item || {};
  return String(it.sku || it.product_sku || it.id || it.product_id || it.raw_id || "").trim();
}

function adjustAppliedPurchaseStockForUpdate_(ss, prodSh, po_id, oldItems, newItems, operator, arrivalDateValue){
  const ph = getHeaders_(prodSh);
  const pidx = headerIndexMap_(ph);
  const prodVals = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const productLookup = buildProductLookupMaps_(prodVals, pidx);
  const now = new Date();
  const nowText = toLocalDateTime_(now);
  const defaultArrivalDateIso = toISODateLocal_(arrivalDateValue || "");
  const saleMarkupPercent = getProductSaleMarkupPercent_(ss);
  const deltaMap = {};

  function addItemDelta_(item, sign){
    const it = item || {};
    const lookupKey = productLookupKeyText_(it);
    const qtyRaw = (it.qty_raw !== undefined && it.qty_raw !== null && String(it.qty_raw).trim() !== "") ? it.qty_raw : it.qty;
    const qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(qtyRaw);
    if (!lookupKey || !(qty > 0)) return;
    const hit = findProductHitBySkuOrId_(productLookup, it);
    if (!hit) throw new Error(`找不到商品 SKU/ID=${lookupKey}`);
    const rowNo = hit.r;
    const entry = deltaMap[rowNo] || {
      hit: hit,
      row: hit.row.slice(),
      delta: 0,
      newCostInfo: null,
      newItem: null
    };
    entry.delta = addDecimalInput_(entry.delta, sign * qty, 6);
    if (sign > 0) {
      const costInfo = normalizePurchaseItemCostFields_(it);
      const unitCost = costInfo.hasCostInput ? cleanDecimalInput_(costInfo.cost_raw, 6) : "";
      if (costInfo.hasCostInput && isFinite(unitCost) && unitCost > 0) {
        entry.newCostInfo = { costInfo: costInfo, unitCost: unitCost };
        entry.newItem = it;
      }
    }
    deltaMap[rowNo] = entry;
  }

  (Array.isArray(oldItems) ? oldItems : []).forEach(function(it){ addItemDelta_(it, -1); });
  (Array.isArray(newItems) ? newItems : []).forEach(function(it){ addItemDelta_(it, 1); });

  const changedRowMap = {};
  const ledgerRows = [];
  Object.keys(deltaMap).forEach(function(rowNoText){
    const rowNo = Number(rowNoText);
    const entry = deltaMap[rowNo];
    const delta = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(entry && entry.delta);
    if (!entry || !isFinite(delta) || delta === 0) return;
    const row = entry.row;
    const current = cleanDecimalInput_(row[pidx.stock] || 0, 6);
    const next = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(addDecimalInput_(current, delta, 6));
    row[pidx.stock] = next;

    if (entry.newCostInfo && entry.newCostInfo.unitCost > 0) {
      if (pidx.cost !== undefined) row[pidx.cost] = entry.newCostInfo.unitCost;
      if (pidx.price !== undefined) row[pidx.price] = calcRoundedSalePriceFromCost_(entry.newCostInfo.unitCost, saleMarkupPercent);
      if (pidx.date !== undefined) row[pidx.date] = nowText;
    }

    const newItem = entry.newItem || {};
    const itemArrivalDateIso = toISODateLocal_(newItem.receive_date || newItem.arrival_date || defaultArrivalDateIso || "");
    if (itemArrivalDateIso && pidx.last_purchase_date !== undefined) {
      const oldLast = toISODateLocal_(row[pidx.last_purchase_date] || "");
      row[pidx.last_purchase_date] = (!oldLast || oldLast < itemArrivalDateIso) ? itemArrivalDateIso : oldLast;
    }
    const itemExpiry = toISODateLocal_(newItem.expiry_date || newItem.expire_date || newItem.expiry || newItem.valid_date || newItem.valid_until || "");
    if (itemExpiry && pidx.expiry_date !== undefined) row[pidx.expiry_date] = itemExpiry;

    changedRowMap[rowNo] = row;
    entry.hit.row = row;
    const pid = String((pidx.sku !== undefined ? row[pidx.sku] : "") || entry.hit.id || productLookupKeyText_(newItem) || "").trim();
    ledgerRows.push({
      ledger_id: generateId_("LG"),
      product_id: pid,
      product_name: String(row[pidx.name] || newItem.product_name || ""),
      sku: (pidx.sku !== undefined) ? String(row[pidx.sku] || newItem.sku || "") : String(newItem.sku || ""),
      unit: (pidx.unit !== undefined) ? String(row[pidx.unit] || newItem.unit || "") : String(newItem.unit || ""),
      change: delta,
      direction: delta > 0 ? "IN" : "OUT",
      reason: "purchase_update",
      ref_id: String(po_id),
      operator: operator,
      date: nowText,
      cost: entry.newCostInfo ? entry.newCostInfo.unitCost : "",
      target: String(newItem.supplier_name || "")
    });
  });

  const rowNos = Object.keys(changedRowMap).map(function(v){ return Number(v); }).filter(function(v){ return v >= 1; }).sort(function(a, b){ return a - b; });
  for (let i = 0; i < rowNos.length; ) {
    const start = rowNos[i];
    const rows = [changedRowMap[start]];
    let j = i + 1;
    while (j < rowNos.length && rowNos[j] === rowNos[j - 1] + 1) {
      rows.push(changedRowMap[rowNos[j]]);
      j += 1;
    }
    prodSh.getRange(start + 1, 1, rows.length, ph.length).setValues(rows);
    i = j;
  }
  if (ledgerRows.length) appendObjectsBatch_(ensureLedgerSheet_(ss), ledgerRows);
}

function applyPurchaseStock_(ss, prodSh, po_id, items, operator, arrivalDateValue){
  const ph = getHeaders_(prodSh);
  const pidx = headerIndexMap_(ph);
  const prodVals = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const productLookup = buildProductLookupMaps_(prodVals, pidx);
  const now = new Date();
  const nowText = toLocalDateTime_(now);
  const defaultArrivalDateIso = toISODateLocal_(arrivalDateValue || "");
  const saleMarkupPercent = getProductSaleMarkupPercent_(ss);
  const changedRowMap = {};
  const ledgerRows = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
    const it = items[i] || {};
    const lookupKey = productLookupKeyText_(it);
    const qtyRaw = (it.qty_raw !== undefined && it.qty_raw !== null && String(it.qty_raw).trim() !== "") ? it.qty_raw : it.qty;
    const costInfo = normalizePurchaseItemCostFields_(it);
    const qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(qtyRaw);
    const unitCost = costInfo.hasCostInput ? cleanDecimalInput_(costInfo.cost_raw, 6) : "";
    if (!lookupKey) throw new Error("品項資料不完整，無法完成驗收入庫");
    if (!(qty > 0)) continue;
    const hit = findProductHitBySkuOrId_(productLookup, it);
    if (!hit) throw new Error(`找不到商品 SKU/ID=${lookupKey}`);
    const pid = String((pidx.sku !== undefined ? hit.row[pidx.sku] : "") || hit.id || lookupKey).trim();
    const rowNo = hit.r;
    const row = changedRowMap[rowNo] || hit.row.slice();
    const current = cleanDecimalInput_(row[pidx.stock] || 0, 6);
    const next = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(addDecimalInput_(current, qty, 6));
    row[pidx.stock] = next;
    if (costInfo.hasCostInput && isFinite(unitCost) && unitCost > 0) {
      // 採購驗收入庫時，商品主檔進價與售價必須同步：售價 = 進價 × (1 + 加價百分比)。
      // 預設加價百分比為 25%，可由 SystemSettings 的 product_sale_markup_percent / sale_markup_percent 等鍵覆蓋。
      if (pidx.cost !== undefined) row[pidx.cost] = unitCost;
      if (pidx.price !== undefined) row[pidx.price] = calcRoundedSalePriceFromCost_(unitCost, saleMarkupPercent);
      if (pidx.date !== undefined) row[pidx.date] = nowText;
    }
    const itemArrivalDateIso = toISODateLocal_(it.receive_date || it.arrival_date || defaultArrivalDateIso || "");
    if (itemArrivalDateIso && pidx.last_purchase_date !== undefined) {
      const oldLast = toISODateLocal_(row[pidx.last_purchase_date] || "");
      row[pidx.last_purchase_date] = (!oldLast || oldLast < itemArrivalDateIso) ? itemArrivalDateIso : oldLast;
    }
    const itemExpiry = toISODateLocal_(it.expiry_date || it.expire_date || it.expiry || it.valid_date || it.valid_until || "");
    if (itemExpiry && pidx.expiry_date !== undefined) {
      row[pidx.expiry_date] = itemExpiry;
      it.expiry_date = itemExpiry;
    }
    changedRowMap[rowNo] = row;
    hit.row = row;
    ledgerRows.push({
      ledger_id: generateId_("LG"),
      product_id: pid,
      product_name: String(row[pidx.name] || it.product_name || ""),
      sku: (pidx.sku !== undefined) ? String(row[pidx.sku] || it.sku || "") : String(it.sku || ""),
      unit: (pidx.unit !== undefined) ? String(row[pidx.unit] || it.unit || "") : String(it.unit || ""),
      change: qty,
      direction: "IN",
      reason: "purchase",
      ref_id: String(po_id),
      operator: operator,
      date: nowText,
      cost: (costInfo.hasCostInput && isFinite(unitCost) && unitCost > 0) ? unitCost : "",
      target: String(it.supplier_name || "")
    });
  }

  const rowNos = Object.keys(changedRowMap).map(function(v){ return Number(v); }).filter(function(v){ return v >= 1; }).sort(function(a, b){ return a - b; });
  for (let i = 0; i < rowNos.length; ) {
    const start = rowNos[i];
    const rows = [changedRowMap[start]];
    let j = i + 1;
    while (j < rowNos.length && rowNos[j] === rowNos[j - 1] + 1) {
      rows.push(changedRowMap[rowNos[j]]);
      j += 1;
    }
    prodSh.getRange(start + 1, 1, rows.length, ph.length).setValues(rows);
    i = j;
  }

  if (ledgerRows.length) {
    appendObjectsBatch_(ensureLedgerSheet_(ss), ledgerRows);
  }
}

function applyOrderShipment_(ss, prodSh, order_id, items, target, operator){
  const ph = getHeaders_(prodSh);
  const pidx = headerIndexMap_(ph);
  const prodVals = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const productLookup = buildProductLookupMaps_(prodVals, pidx);
  const now = new Date();
  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
    const it = items[i] || {};
    const lookupKey = productLookupKeyText_(it);
    const qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(it.qty || it.quantity || 0);
    if (!lookupKey || !(qty > 0)) throw new Error("訂單品項資料不完整");
    const hit = findProductHitBySkuOrId_(productLookup, it);
    if (!hit) throw new Error(`找不到商品 SKU/ID=${lookupKey}`);
    const pid = String((pidx.sku !== undefined ? hit.row[pidx.sku] : "") || hit.id || lookupKey).trim();
    const current = cleanDecimalInput_(hit.row[pidx.stock] || 0, 6);
    const next = addDecimalInput_(current, -qty, 6);
    if (next < 0) throw new Error(`庫存不足：${hit.row[pidx.name]} current=${current}, qty=${qty}`);
    prodSh.getRange(hit.r + 1, pidx.stock + 1).setValue(next);
    writeLedger_(ss, {
      ledger_id: generateId_("LG"),
      product_id: pid,
      product_name: String(hit.row[pidx.name] || it.name || ""),
      sku: (pidx.sku !== undefined) ? String(hit.row[pidx.sku] || "") : "",
      unit: (pidx.unit !== undefined) ? String(hit.row[pidx.unit] || "") : "",
      change: -qty,
      direction: "OUT",
      reason: "order",
      ref_id: String(order_id),
      operator: operator,
      date: toLocalDateTime_(now),
      cost: (pidx.cost !== undefined) ? (Number(hit.row[pidx.cost] || 0) || "") : "",
      target: target
    });
  }
}

function rowObjectFromHeaders_(headers, row) {
  const out = {};
  const list = Array.isArray(headers) ? headers : [];
  const vals = Array.isArray(row) ? row : [];
  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
    const key = String(list[i] || "").trim();
    if (!key) continue;
    out[key] = vals[i];
  }
  return out;
}

function findSheetRowNumberByColumnValue_(sheet, headerName, targetValue) {
  const target = String(targetValue || "").trim();
  if (!sheet || !target) return 0;
  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  const col = idx[String(headerName || "").trim()];
  if (col === undefined) return 0;
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return 0;
  const vals = sheet.getRange(2, col + 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
  for (let i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
    if (String(vals[i][0] || "").trim() === target) return i + 2;
  }
  return 0;
}

function purchaseNeedsOrderLookup_(x) {
  const sourceOrderId = String(x && x.source_order_id || "").trim();
  const sourceOrderIds = String(x && x.source_order_ids || "").trim();
  if (sourceOrderId || sourceOrderIds) return false;
  return Number(x && x.auto_generated || 0) ? 1 : 0;
}

function readPurchaseObjectByPoId_(sheet, poId) {
  const target = String(poId || "").trim();
  if (!target) return null;
  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const rowNumber = findSheetRowNumberByColumnValue_(sheet, "po_id", target);
  if (!rowNumber) return null;
  const row = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, headers.length).getValues()[0];
  return {
    rowNumber: rowNumber,
    rowIndex: rowNumber - 2,
    obj: rowObjectFromHeaders_(headers, row)
  };
}


function purchaseSummaryFields_() {
  return [
    "po_id", "date", "arrival_date", "form_no", "form_name",
    "supplier_id", "supplier_name", "total", "expiry_date", "status", "operator",
    "created_at", "source_order_id", "source_order_ids", "stock_applied",
    "completed_at", "auto_generated"
  ];
}

function isPurchaseSummaryRequest_(p) {
  return Number(p && (p.summary || p.summary_only || p.list_only) || 0) ? 1 : 0;
}

function readPurchaseSummaryObjects_(sheet) {
  if (!sheet) return [];
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow <= 1) return [];

  const headers = getHeaders_(sheet);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  const fields = purchaseSummaryFields_();
  const rowCount = lastRow - 1;
  const columnValues = {};

  fields.forEach(function(field) {
    const colIdx = idx[field];
    if (colIdx === undefined) return;
    columnValues[field] = sheet.getRange(2, colIdx + 1, rowCount, 1).getValues();
  });

  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    const obj = {};
    let hasAny = false;
    fields.forEach(function(field) {
      const vals = columnValues[field];
      const v = vals ? vals[i][0] : "";
      obj[field] = v;
      if (v !== "" && v !== null && v !== undefined) hasAny = true;
    });
    if (!hasAny) continue;
    out.push(obj);
  }
  return out;
}

function normalizePurchaseSummaryRowForApi_(x, i) {
  x = x || {};
  x.date = toISODateLocal_(x.date);
  x.arrival_date = toISODateLocal_(x.arrival_date || x.receive_date || "");
  x.created_at = toISODateLocal_(x.created_at);
  x.completed_at = toISODateLocal_(x.completed_at || "");
  x.po_id = x.po_id || x.purchase_id || x.purchaseId || x.id || x.ref_id || "";
  x.form_no = x.form_no || x.form_code || "";
  x.form_name = x.form_name || x.form_title || "";
  x.supplier_id = x.supplier_id || x.supplierId || x.supplier || "";
  x.supplier_name = x.supplier_name || x.supplierName || "";
  x.status = String(x.status || "待驗收").trim() || "待驗收";
  x.stock_applied = Number(x.stock_applied || 0) ? 1 : 0;
  x.source_order_ids = String(x.source_order_ids || "").trim();
  x.source_order_id = String(x.source_order_id || "").trim();
  if (!x.source_order_ids && x.source_order_id) x.source_order_ids = x.source_order_id;
  if (!x.source_order_id && x.source_order_ids) x.source_order_id = splitCsvIds_(x.source_order_ids)[0] || "";
  x.auto_generated = Number(x.auto_generated || 0) ? 1 : 0;

  if (!String(x.form_no || "").trim()) {
    const info = getPurchaseFormInfoByCategory_(String(x.form_name || "").trim());
    x.form_no = info.form_no;
    if (!String(x.form_name || "").trim()) x.form_name = info.form_name;
  }

  x.items = [];
  x.items_loaded = 0;
  x.item_count = Number(x.item_count || 0) || 0;

  if (!String(x.po_id || "").trim()) {
    const hasPurchaseLikeData = String(x.date || "").trim() || String(x.supplier_id || x.supplier_name || "").trim() || String(x.total || "").trim();
    if (hasPurchaseLikeData) x.po_id = `ROW${i + 2}`;
  }
  return x;
}

function normalizePurchaseRowForApi_(ss, x, i, includeItems, lookup) {
  x = x || {};
  x.date = toISODateLocal_(x.date);
  x.arrival_date = toISODateLocal_(x.arrival_date || x.receive_date || "");
  x.created_at = toISODateLocal_(x.created_at);
  x.completed_at = toISODateLocal_(x.completed_at || "");
  x.po_id = x.po_id || x.purchase_id || x.purchaseId || x.id || x.ref_id || "";
  x.form_no = x.form_no || x.form_code || "";
  x.form_name = x.form_name || x.form_title || "";
  x.supplier_id = x.supplier_id || x.supplierId || x.supplier || "";
  x.supplier_name = x.supplier_name || x.supplierName || "";
  x.status = String(x.status || "待驗收").trim() || "待驗收";
  x.stock_applied = Number(x.stock_applied || 0) ? 1 : 0;
  x.source_order_ids = String(x.source_order_ids || "").trim();
  x.source_order_id = String(x.source_order_id || "").trim();
  if (!x.source_order_ids && x.source_order_id) x.source_order_ids = x.source_order_id;
  if (!x.source_order_id && x.source_order_ids) x.source_order_id = splitCsvIds_(x.source_order_ids)[0] || "";
  x.auto_generated = Number(x.auto_generated || 0) ? 1 : 0;
  if (!x.source_order_id && lookup !== false) {
    const fallbackSourceOrderId = String(findOrderIdByLinkedPurchaseId_(ss, x.po_id, lookup) || "").trim();
    if (fallbackSourceOrderId) {
      x.source_order_id = fallbackSourceOrderId;
      if (!x.source_order_ids) x.source_order_ids = fallbackSourceOrderId;
      if (!x.auto_generated) x.auto_generated = 1;
    }
  }
  if (!String(x.form_no || "").trim()) {
    const info = getPurchaseFormInfoByCategory_(String(x.form_name || "").trim());
    x.form_no = info.form_no;
    if (!String(x.form_name || "").trim()) x.form_name = info.form_name;
  }
  const orderMeta = x.source_order_id ? getOrderMetaById_(ss, x.source_order_id, lookup) : { order_id: "", created_date: "", shipping_date: "" };
  if (!x.date && String(orderMeta.created_date || "").trim()) {
    x.date = String(orderMeta.created_date || "").trim();
  }
  if (!x.arrival_date && String(orderMeta.shipping_date || "").trim()) {
    x.arrival_date = String(orderMeta.shipping_date || "").trim();
  }

  const raw = x.items;
  if (includeItems) {
    if (raw && typeof raw === "string") {
      try { x.items = JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) { x.items = []; }
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(x.items)) x.items = [];
    x.items = x.items.map(function(it){
      var item = (it && typeof it === "object") ? Object.assign({}, it) : {};
      var costInfo = normalizePurchaseItemCostFields_(item);
      if (costInfo.hasCostInput) {
        item.cost_raw = costInfo.cost_raw;
        item.cost = costInfo.cost;
      } else {
        item.cost = "";
        item.cost_raw = "";
      }
      var itemReceiveDate = toISODateLocal_(item.receive_date || item.arrival_date || "");
      if (!itemReceiveDate && x.arrival_date) itemReceiveDate = toISODateLocal_(x.arrival_date || "");
      item.receive_date = itemReceiveDate || "";
      var qtyRaw = (item.qty_raw !== undefined && item.qty_raw !== null && String(item.qty_raw).trim() !== "") ? item.qty_raw : item.qty;
      var qtyRounded = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(qtyRaw);
      item.qty = qtyRounded;
      item.qty_raw = formatPurchaseQtyText_(qtyRounded);
      if (item.suggested_qty !== undefined && item.suggested_qty !== null && String(item.suggested_qty).trim() !== "") item.suggested_qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(item.suggested_qty);
      if (item.customer_order_qty !== undefined && item.customer_order_qty !== null && String(item.customer_order_qty).trim() !== "") item.customer_order_qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(item.customer_order_qty);
      return item;
    });
    x.items_loaded = 1;
    x.item_count = x.items.length;
  } else {
    x.items = [];
    x.items_loaded = 0;
    x.item_count = 0;
  }

  if (!String(x.po_id || "").trim()) {
    const hasPurchaseLikeData = String(x.date || "").trim() || String(x.supplier_id || x.supplier_name || "").trim() || String(x.total || "").trim() || String(raw || "").trim();
    if (hasPurchaseLikeData) x.po_id = `ROW${i + 2}`;
  }
  return x;
}

function apiPurchases_(p, respond) {
  if (typeof p === "function") {
    respond = p;
    p = {};
  }
  p = p || {};

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensurePurchasesSheet_(ss);
  const targetPoId = String(p.po_id || p.id || "").trim();
  const includeItems = targetPoId || Number(p.detail || p.include_items || 0) ? 1 : 0;
  const summaryOnly = !targetPoId && isPurchaseSummaryRequest_(p);

  if (targetPoId) {
    const hit = readPurchaseObjectByPoId_(sh, targetPoId);
    if (!hit || !hit.obj) return respond({ status: "ok", data: [], count: 0 });
    const lookup = purchaseNeedsOrderLookup_(hit.obj) ? buildOrderLookupMaps_(ss) : false;
    const row = normalizePurchaseRowForApi_(ss, hit.obj, hit.rowIndex, includeItems, lookup);
    if (!String(row.po_id || "").trim()) return respond({ status: "ok", data: [], count: 0 });
    return respond({ status: "ok", data: [row], count: 1, mode: "detail" });
  }

  if (summaryOnly) {
    const rawSummary = readPurchaseSummaryObjects_(sh);
    const list = rawSummary
      .map((x, i) => normalizePurchaseSummaryRowForApi_(x, i))
      .filter(x => String(x.po_id || "").trim() !== "");
    return respond({ status: "ok", data: list, count: list.length, mode: "summary" });
  }

  const rawList = readObjects_(sh);
  const needOrderLookup = rawList.some(purchaseNeedsOrderLookup_);
  const orderLookup = needOrderLookup ? buildOrderLookupMaps_(ss) : false;
  const list = rawList
    .map((x, i) => normalizePurchaseRowForApi_(ss, x, i, includeItems, orderLookup))
    .filter(x => String(x.po_id || "").trim() !== "");

  return respond({ status: "ok", data: list, count: list.length, mode: includeItems ? "full" : "legacy" });
}

function expandCompactPurchasePayload_(compact) {
  if (!compact || typeof compact !== "object") return compact;
  const items = Array.isArray(compact.it) ? compact.it : [];
  return {
    po_id: String(compact.po || compact.po_id || "").trim(),
    source_order_id: String(compact.so || compact.source_order_id || "").trim(),
    source_order_ids: String(compact.sos || compact.source_order_ids || compact.so || compact.source_order_id || "").trim(),
    auto_generated: Number(compact.ag || compact.auto_generated || 0) ? 1 : 0,
    date: compact.d || compact.date || "",
    arrival_date: compact.ad || compact.arrival_date || "",
    form_no: compact.fn || compact.form_no || "",
    form_name: compact.fm || compact.form_name || "",
    supplier_id: compact.sid || compact.supplier_id || "",
    supplier_name: compact.sn || compact.supplier_name || "",
    total: compact.t || compact.total || 0,
    operator: compact.op || compact.operator || "",
    status: compact.st || compact.status || "",
    apply_stock: !!(compact.as || compact.apply_stock),
    items: items.map(row => {
      if (Array.isArray(row)) {
        const cell = idx => (row[idx] === undefined || row[idx] === null) ? "" : row[idx];
        return {
          product_id: cell(0),
          qty_raw: cell(1),
          cost_raw: cell(2),
          supplier_id: cell(3),
          receive_date: cell(4),
          inspection_priority: cell(5),
          receipt_weight: cell(6),
          accept_weight: cell(7),
          acceptance_result: cell(8),
          pesticide_result: cell(9),
          note: cell(10),
          suggested_qty: cell(11),
          customer_order_qty: cell(12)
        };
      }
      return row || {};
    })
  };
}

function purchaseDedupeItemSignature_(items) {
  var list = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
  return JSON.stringify(list.map(function(it){
    return {
      product_id: String(it && (it.product_id || it.id) || "").trim(),
      product_name: String(it && it.product_name || "").trim(),
      qty: roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(it && (it.qty_raw !== undefined && it.qty_raw !== null && String(it.qty_raw).trim() !== "" ? it.qty_raw : it.qty) || 0),
      cost: cleanDecimalInput_(it && (it.cost_raw !== undefined && it.cost_raw !== null && String(it.cost_raw).trim() !== "" ? it.cost_raw : it.cost) || 0, 6),
      supplier_id: String(it && it.supplier_id || "").trim(),
      supplier_name: String(it && it.supplier_name || "").trim(),
      unit: String(it && it.unit || "").trim(),
      spec: String(it && it.spec || "").trim(),
      receive_date: String(it && it.receive_date || "").trim(),
      inspection_priority: String(it && it.inspection_priority || "").trim(),
      receipt_weight: String(it && it.receipt_weight || "").trim(),
      accept_weight: String(it && it.accept_weight || "").trim(),
      acceptance_result: String(it && it.acceptance_result || "").trim(),
      pesticide_result: String(it && it.pesticide_result || "").trim(),
      note: String(it && it.note || "").trim()
    };
  }));
}

function purchaseRowDateMetaFromObject_(ss, obj, orderLookup) {
  const sourceOrderIds = splitCsvIds_(obj && (obj.source_order_ids || obj.source_order_id) || "");
  const firstOrderId = sourceOrderIds[0] || "";
  const meta = firstOrderId ? getOrderMetaById_(ss, firstOrderId, orderLookup) : { order_id: "", created_date: "", shipping_date: "" };
  return {
    date: toISODateLocal_(obj && obj.date || "") || String(meta.created_date || "").trim() || "",
    arrival_date: toISODateLocal_(obj && (obj.arrival_date || obj.receive_date) || "") || String(meta.shipping_date || "").trim() || "",
    first_order_id: firstOrderId,
    source_order_ids: sourceOrderIds
  };
}

function purchaseItemMergeKey_(item) {
  const productId = String(item && (item.product_id || item.id) || "").trim();
  const supplierId = String(item && item.supplier_id || "").trim();
  const sku = String(item && item.sku || "").trim();
