  return [productId, supplierId, sku].join("||");
}

function mergePurchaseItemsForAutoAppend_(existingItems, incomingItems, defaultArrivalDate) {
  const base = Array.isArray(existingItems) ? existingItems.map(function(it){ return (it && typeof it === "object") ? Object.assign({}, it) : {}; }) : [];
  const indexMap = {};
  base.forEach(function(it, idx){
    const key = purchaseItemMergeKey_(it);
    if (key && indexMap[key] === undefined) indexMap[key] = idx;
    const receiveDate = toISODateLocal_(it.receive_date || it.arrival_date || "") || toISODateLocal_(defaultArrivalDate || "") || "";
    if (receiveDate) it.receive_date = receiveDate;
  });
  (Array.isArray(incomingItems) ? incomingItems : []).forEach(function(raw){
    const incoming = (raw && typeof raw === "object") ? Object.assign({}, raw) : {};
      incoming.qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(incoming.qty || incoming.qty_raw || 0);
      incoming.qty_raw = formatPurchaseQtyText_(incoming.qty);
      const incomingSuggestedRaw = (incoming.suggested_qty !== undefined && incoming.suggested_qty !== null) ? String(incoming.suggested_qty).trim() : "";
      if (incomingSuggestedRaw !== "") incoming.suggested_qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(incoming.suggested_qty);
      if (String(incoming.customer_order_qty || "").trim()) incoming.customer_order_qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(incoming.customer_order_qty);
    const receiveDate = toISODateLocal_(incoming.receive_date || incoming.arrival_date || "") || toISODateLocal_(defaultArrivalDate || "") || "";
    if (receiveDate) incoming.receive_date = receiveDate;
    const key = purchaseItemMergeKey_(incoming);
    const hitIdx = (key && indexMap[key] !== undefined) ? indexMap[key] : -1;
    if (hitIdx < 0) {
      base.push(incoming);
      if (key) indexMap[key] = base.length - 1;
      return;
    }
    const current = base[hitIdx];
    current.qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(addDecimalInput_(current.qty || current.qty_raw || 0, incoming.qty || incoming.qty_raw || 0, 6));
    current.qty_raw = formatPurchaseQtyText_(current.qty);
    const currentSuggested = (current.suggested_qty !== undefined && current.suggested_qty !== null) ? String(current.suggested_qty).trim() : "";
    const incomingSuggested = (incoming.suggested_qty !== undefined && incoming.suggested_qty !== null) ? String(incoming.suggested_qty).trim() : "";
    if (currentSuggested !== "" || incomingSuggested !== "") {
      current.suggested_qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(addDecimalInput_(currentSuggested || 0, incomingSuggested || 0, 6));
    }
    const currentCustomer = String(current.customer_order_qty || "").trim();
    const incomingCustomer = String(incoming.customer_order_qty || "").trim();
    if (currentCustomer || incomingCustomer) {
      current.customer_order_qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(addDecimalInput_(currentCustomer || 0, incomingCustomer || 0, 6));
    }
    if (!String(current.receive_date || "").trim() && receiveDate) current.receive_date = receiveDate;
    if (!String(current.cost_raw || "").trim() && String(incoming.cost_raw || "").trim()) current.cost_raw = incoming.cost_raw;
    if ((current.cost === "" || current.cost === undefined || current.cost === null) && incoming.cost !== "") current.cost = incoming.cost;
  });
  return sortPurchaseItemsBySupplier_(base);
}

function summarizePurchaseHeaderSuppliers_(items) {
  const ids = Array.from(new Set((Array.isArray(items) ? items : []).map(function(x){ return String(x && x.supplier_id || "").trim(); }).filter(Boolean)));
  const names = Array.from(new Set((Array.isArray(items) ? items : []).map(function(x){ return String(x && x.supplier_name || "").trim(); }).filter(Boolean)));
  return {
    supplier_id: ids.length === 1 ? ids[0] : (ids.length ? "MULTI" : ""),
    supplier_name: names.length === 1 ? names[0] : (names.length ? "多供應商" : "")
  };
}

function purchaseRowArrayFromObject_(headers, obj) {
  return headers.map(function(h){ return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj || {}, h) ? obj[h] : ""; });
}

function findMergeableGeneratedPurchaseRow_(ss, rows, headers, idx, criteria, orderLookup) {
  const targetFormNo = String(criteria && criteria.form_no || "").trim();
  const targetDate = toISODateLocal_(criteria && criteria.date || "");
  const targetArrivalDate = toISODateLocal_(criteria && criteria.arrival_date || "");
  if (!targetFormNo) return null;
  for (let r = rows.length - 1; r >= 1; r--) {
    const row = rows[r] || [];
    const obj = rowObjectFromHeaders_(headers, row);
    if (Number(obj.auto_generated || 0) !== 1) continue;
    if (Number(obj.stock_applied || 0)) continue;
    if (String(obj.status || "待驗收").trim() !== "待驗收") continue;
    if (String(obj.form_no || "").trim() !== targetFormNo) continue;
    const meta = purchaseRowDateMetaFromObject_(ss, obj, orderLookup);
    if (String(meta.date || "") !== targetDate) continue;
    if (String(meta.arrival_date || "") !== targetArrivalDate) continue;
    return {
      rowIndex: r,
      rowNumber: r + 1,
      obj: obj
    };
  }
  return null;
}

function findRecentDuplicatePurchasePoId_(rows, idx, payload) {
  var incomingPoId = String(payload && payload.po_id || "").trim();
  if (incomingPoId) return "";
  var targetItemsSig = purchaseDedupeItemSignature_(payload && payload.items);
  if (!targetItemsSig || targetItemsSig === "[]") return "";
  var targetSourceOrderId = String(payload && payload.source_order_id || "").trim();
  var targetOperator = String(payload && payload.operator || "").trim();
  var targetDate = String(payload && payload.date || "").trim();
  var targetArrivalDate = String(payload && payload.arrival_date || "").trim();
  var targetFormNo = String(payload && payload.form_no || "").trim();
  var targetSupplierId = String(payload && payload.supplier_id || "").trim();
  var now = Date.now();
  for (var r = rows.length - 1; r >= 1; r--) {
    var rowPoId = String(rows[r][idx.po_id] || "").trim();
    if (!rowPoId) continue;
    var createdAt = rows[r][idx.created_at];
    var createdMs = createdAt instanceof Date ? createdAt.getTime() : new Date(createdAt || "").getTime();
    if (isFinite(createdMs)) {
      var ageMs = now - createdMs;
      if (ageMs > 20000) break;
      if (ageMs < -5000) continue;
    }
    if (String(rows[r][idx.source_order_id] || "").trim() !== targetSourceOrderId) continue;
    if (String(rows[r][idx.operator] || "").trim() !== targetOperator) continue;
    if (String(rows[r][idx.date] || "").trim() !== targetDate) continue;
    if (String(rows[r][idx.arrival_date] || "").trim() !== targetArrivalDate) continue;
    if (String(rows[r][idx.form_no] || "").trim() !== targetFormNo) continue;
    if (String(rows[r][idx.supplier_id] || "").trim() !== targetSupplierId) continue;
    var rowItems = [];
    try {
      rowItems = JSON.parse(rows[r][idx.items] || "[]");
    } catch (err) {
      rowItems = [];
    }
    if (purchaseDedupeItemSignature_(rowItems) !== targetItemsSig) continue;
    return rowPoId;
  }
  return "";
}

function parsePurchasePayload_(p) {
  if (p && p.purchase_compact) return expandCompactPurchasePayload_(mustJson_(p.purchase_compact));
  return mustJson_(p && p.purchase);
}

function apiManagePurchase_(p, respond) {
  const action = String(p.action || "").trim();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const purchasesSh = ensurePurchasesSheet_(ss);
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);
  try {
    if (action === "add" || action === "update") {
      const payload = parsePurchasePayload_(p) || {};
      let po_id = String(payload.po_id || p.po_id || "").trim();
      if (!po_id) po_id = String(generateDailyDocNo_(purchasesSh, "po_id", new Date())).trim();
      const headers = getHeaders_(purchasesSh);
      const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
      let foundRow = 0;
      let existingRow = null;
      let oldStockApplied = 0;
      const form_no = String(payload.form_no || "F-02-B-01-1").trim();
      const form_name = String(payload.form_name || "").trim() || getPurchaseFormInfoByCategory_(form_no).form_name;
      const items = Array.isArray(payload.items) ? payload.items : [];
      if (!items.length) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 items" });
      const operator = String(payload.operator || p.operator || "").trim();
      let rows = null;

      if (po_id) {
        foundRow = findSheetRowNumberByColumnValue_(purchasesSh, "po_id", po_id);
        if (foundRow) {
          existingRow = purchasesSh.getRange(foundRow, 1, 1, headers.length).getValues()[0];
          oldStockApplied = Number(existingRow[idx.stock_applied] || 0) ? 1 : 0;
        }
      }
      if (action === "update" && po_id && !foundRow) {
        return respond({ status: "error", message: `找不到原採購驗收單：${po_id}，已停止本次寫入，請重新整理後再試` });
      }
      if (!foundRow && action === "add") {
        rows = purchasesSh.getDataRange().getValues();
      }

      const incomingSourceOrderIds = splitCsvIds_(payload.source_order_ids || payload.source_order_id || "");
      const existingSourceOrderIds = existingRow ? splitCsvIds_((idx.source_order_ids !== undefined ? existingRow[idx.source_order_ids] : "") || (idx.source_order_id !== undefined ? existingRow[idx.source_order_id] : "")) : [];
      const fallbackSourceOrderId = String(findOrderIdByLinkedPurchaseId_(ss, po_id) || "").trim();
      const linkedSourceOrderIds = incomingSourceOrderIds.length
        ? incomingSourceOrderIds
        : (existingSourceOrderIds.length ? existingSourceOrderIds : (fallbackSourceOrderId ? [fallbackSourceOrderId] : []));
      const linkedSourceOrderId = String(linkedSourceOrderIds[0] || "").trim();
      const linkedAutoGenerated = Number(payload.auto_generated || (existingRow ? existingRow[idx.auto_generated] : 0) || (linkedSourceOrderId && fallbackSourceOrderId && linkedSourceOrderId === fallbackSourceOrderId ? 1 : 0) || 0) ? 1 : 0;
      const linkedOrderMeta = linkedSourceOrderId ? getOrderMetaById_(ss, linkedSourceOrderId) : { order_id: "", created_date: "", shipping_date: "" };
      const defaultDateFromOrder = String(linkedOrderMeta.created_date || "").trim();
      const defaultArrivalDateFromOrder = String(linkedOrderMeta.shipping_date || "").trim();
      const hasDateField = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(payload, "date");
      const incomingDate = hasDateField ? toISODateLocal_(payload.date || "") : "";
      const existingDate = existingRow ? toISODateLocal_(existingRow[idx.date] || "") : "";
      // 採購日期：優先採用使用者輸入，其次保留既有值；建立/空白時自動帶入來源訂單建立日期。
      const date = hasDateField
        ? (incomingDate || existingDate || defaultDateFromOrder || "")
        : (existingDate || defaultDateFromOrder || "");
      const hasArrivalDateField = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(payload, "arrival_date") || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(payload, "receive_date");
      const incomingArrivalDate = hasArrivalDateField ? toISODateLocal_(payload.arrival_date || payload.receive_date || "") : "";
      const existingArrivalDate = existingRow ? toISODateLocal_(existingRow[idx.arrival_date] || "") : "";
      // 到貨日期：優先採用使用者輸入，其次保留既有值；建立/空白時自動帶入來源訂單出貨日期。
      const arrival_date = hasArrivalDateField
        ? (incomingArrivalDate || existingArrivalDate || defaultArrivalDateFromOrder || "")
        : (existingArrivalDate || defaultArrivalDateFromOrder || "");

      const supplierMap = buildSupplierNameMap_(ss);
      const prodVals = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
      const ph = prodVals[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
      const pidx = headerIndexMap_(ph);
      const productLookup = buildProductLookupMaps_(prodVals, pidx);

      // 採購驗收單總金額一律以「訂購數量 × 單價」逐列加總為準，
      // 不再信任前端或商品主檔可能帶入的其他價格來源。
      let exactTotal = 0;
      let existingItems = [];
      if (existingRow && idx.items !== undefined) {
        try { existingItems = JSON.parse(existingRow[idx.items] || "[]"); } catch (e) { existingItems = []; }
      }
      if (!Array.isArray(existingItems)) existingItems = [];
      const normalizedItems = items.map((it, itemIndex) => {
        const lookupKey = productLookupKeyText_(it);
        if (!lookupKey) throw new Error("品項資料不完整");
        const hit = findProductHitBySkuOrId_(productLookup, it);
        if (!hit) throw new Error(`找不到商品 SKU/ID=${lookupKey}`);
        const pid = String((pidx.sku !== undefined ? hit.row[pidx.sku] : "") || hit.id || lookupKey).trim();
        const productInternalId = String(hit.id || "").trim();
        const qtyRaw = (it.qty_raw !== undefined && it.qty_raw !== null && String(it.qty_raw).trim() !== "") ? it.qty_raw : it.qty;
        const costInfo = normalizePurchaseItemCostFields_(it);
        const hasCostInput = costInfo.hasCostInput;
        const costRaw = costInfo.cost_raw;
        const qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(qtyRaw);
        const cost = hasCostInput ? costInfo.cost : "";
        if (qty < 0) throw new Error("品項數量不可為負數");
        const supplierIds = parseSupplierIdsFromProductRow_(hit.row, pidx);
        let supplier_id = String(it.supplier_id || supplierIds[0] || "").trim();
        const supplier_name = String(it.supplier_name || supplierMap[supplier_id] || "").trim();
        if (hasCostInput) exactTotal = addDecimalInput_(exactTotal, mulDecimalInput_(qty, costRaw, 6), 6);
        const existingItem = (existingItems[itemIndex] && typeof existingItems[itemIndex] === "object") ? existingItems[itemIndex] : {};
        const existingReceiveDate = toISODateLocal_(existingItem.receive_date || existingItem.arrival_date || "");
        const incomingReceiveDate = toISODateLocal_(it.receive_date || it.arrival_date || "");
        const resolvedReceiveDate = incomingReceiveDate || existingReceiveDate || arrival_date || defaultArrivalDateFromOrder || "";
        return {
          product_id: pid,
          product_internal_id: productInternalId,
          product_name: String(it.product_name || hit.row[pidx.name] || ""),
          qty_raw: formatPurchaseQtyText_(qty),
          cost_raw: hasCostInput ? costRaw : "",
          qty: qty,
          suggested_qty: (it.suggested_qty !== undefined && it.suggested_qty !== null && String(it.suggested_qty).trim() !== "") ? roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(it.suggested_qty) : qty,
          customer_order_qty: (function(){
            var raw = String(it.customer_order_qty || "").trim();
            return raw === "" ? "" : roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(raw);
          })(),
          cost: hasCostInput ? cost : "",
          supplier_id: supplier_id,
          supplier_name: supplier_name,
          expiry_date: toISODateLocal_(it.expiry_date || ""),
          unit: (pidx.unit !== undefined) ? String(it.unit || hit.row[pidx.unit] || "") : String(it.unit || ""),
          sku: (pidx.sku !== undefined) ? String(it.sku || hit.row[pidx.sku] || "") : String(it.sku || ""),
          spec: (pidx.spec !== undefined) ? String(it.spec || hit.row[pidx.spec] || "") : String(it.spec || ""),
          receive_date: resolvedReceiveDate,
          inspection_priority: String(it.inspection_priority || "").trim(),
          receipt_weight: normalizePurchaseMeasurementText_(it.receipt_weight || "", (pidx.unit !== undefined) ? String(it.unit || hit.row[pidx.unit] || "") : String(it.unit || "")),
          accept_weight: normalizePurchaseMeasurementText_(it.accept_weight || "", (pidx.unit !== undefined) ? String(it.unit || hit.row[pidx.unit] || "") : String(it.unit || "")),
          acceptance_result: String(it.acceptance_result || "").trim(),
          pesticide_result: String(it.pesticide_result || "").trim(),
          note: String(it.note || "").trim()
        };
      });

      const sortedNormalizedItems = sortPurchaseItemsBySupplier_(normalizedItems);
      const uniqSupIds = Array.from(new Set(sortedNormalizedItems.map(it => String(it.supplier_id || "").trim()).filter(Boolean)));
      const uniqSupNames = Array.from(new Set(sortedNormalizedItems.map(it => String(it.supplier_name || "").trim()).filter(Boolean)));
      const headerSupplierId = String(payload.supplier_id || ((uniqSupIds.length === 1) ? uniqSupIds[0] : "MULTI")).trim();
      const headerSupplierName = String(payload.supplier_name || ((uniqSupNames.length === 1) ? uniqSupNames[0] : "多供應商")).trim();
      const duplicatePoId = (!foundRow && action === "add" && rows) ? String(findRecentDuplicatePurchasePoId_(rows, idx, {
        po_id: String(payload.po_id || p.po_id || "").trim(),
        source_order_id: linkedSourceOrderId,
        operator: operator,
        date: date,
        arrival_date: arrival_date,
        form_no: form_no,
        supplier_id: headerSupplierId,
        items: sortedNormalizedItems
      }) || "").trim() : "";
      if (duplicatePoId) {
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: `採購驗收單已儲存：${duplicatePoId}`, po_id: duplicatePoId, deduped: 1 });
      }
      const applyStockNow = !!payload.apply_stock && !oldStockApplied;
      const adjustStockForAppliedUpdate = !!(foundRow && oldStockApplied);
      const initialStatus = oldStockApplied ? "已入庫" : String(payload.status || "待驗收").trim() || "待驗收";
      const rowObj = {
        po_id: po_id,
        date: date,
        arrival_date: arrival_date,
        form_no: form_no,
        form_name: form_name,
        supplier_id: headerSupplierId,
        supplier_name: headerSupplierName,
        total: exactTotal,
        items: JSON.stringify(sortedNormalizedItems),
        expiry_date: "",
        status: initialStatus,
        operator: operator,
        created_at: existingRow ? (existingRow[idx.created_at] || new Date()) : new Date(),
        source_order_id: linkedSourceOrderId,
        source_order_ids: joinCsvIds_(linkedSourceOrderIds),
        stock_applied: oldStockApplied ? 1 : 0,
        completed_at: existingRow ? existingRow[idx.completed_at] : "",
        auto_generated: linkedAutoGenerated ? 1 : 0
      };

      if (foundRow) {
        setSheetRowByObject_(purchasesSh, foundRow, rowObj);
      } else {
        appendObject_(purchasesSh, rowObj);
      }
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();

      let savedHit = readPurchaseObjectByPoId_(purchasesSh, po_id);
      if (!savedHit || !savedHit.obj) {
        return respond({ status: "error", message: `採購驗收單寫入後讀回失敗：${po_id}` });
      }
      let savedItems = [];
      try { savedItems = JSON.parse(savedHit.obj.items || "[]"); } catch (e) { savedItems = []; }
      if (!Array.isArray(savedItems) || savedItems.length < sortedNormalizedItems.length) {
        return respond({ status: "error", message: `採購驗收單內容未完整寫入：${po_id}，已停止本次入庫` });
      }

      if (adjustStockForAppliedUpdate) {
        adjustAppliedPurchaseStockForUpdate_(ss, prodSh, po_id, existingItems, sortedNormalizedItems, operator, arrival_date);
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
      }

      if (applyStockNow) {
        applyPurchaseStock_(ss, prodSh, po_id, sortedNormalizedItems, operator, arrival_date);
        setSheetRowByObject_(purchasesSh, savedHit.rowNumber, {
          status: "已入庫",
          stock_applied: 1,
          completed_at: new Date(),
          operator: operator
        });
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
      }

      savedHit = readPurchaseObjectByPoId_(purchasesSh, po_id);
      if (!savedHit || !savedHit.obj) {
        return respond({ status: "error", message: `採購驗收單完成後讀回失敗：${po_id}` });
      }
      savedItems = [];
      try { savedItems = JSON.parse(savedHit.obj.items || "[]"); } catch (e) { savedItems = []; }
      if (!Array.isArray(savedItems) || savedItems.length < sortedNormalizedItems.length) {
        return respond({ status: "error", message: `採購驗收單完成後內容仍不完整：${po_id}` });
      }
      if (applyStockNow) {
        const applied = Number(savedHit.obj.stock_applied || 0) ? 1 : 0;
        const statusText = String(savedHit.obj.status || "").trim();
        if (!applied || statusText !== "已入庫") {
          return respond({ status: "error", message: `採購驗收單入庫狀態未正確寫入：${po_id}` });
        }
      }

      const responseRow = normalizePurchaseRowForApi_(ss, Object.assign({}, savedHit.obj), savedHit.rowIndex, 1, false);
      return respond({
        status: "ok",
        message: applyStockNow ? `採購驗收單已完成：${po_id}` : `採購驗收單已儲存：${po_id}`,
        po_id: po_id,
        data: [responseRow],
        count: 1,
        purchase: responseRow
      });
    }

    if (action === "delete") {
      const po_id = String(p.po_id || p.id || "").trim();
      if (!po_id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 po_id" });
      const rows = purchasesSh.getDataRange().getValues();
      const headers = rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
      const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
      for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
        if (String(rows[r][idx.po_id]).trim() === po_id) {
          purchasesSh.deleteRow(r + 1);
          return respond({ status: "ok", message: "採購驗收單已刪除（若已入庫，不會自動回滾庫存）" });
        }
      }
      return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到進貨單" });
    }

    return respond({ status: "error", message: "未知 action" });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
  }
}

// ----------------- Pickups (領貨 / 內部出庫) -----------------
function getPickupsSheet_(ss){
  // 兼容可能名稱（若存在，優先使用；最後仍會 ensure 成 pickups）
  return getSheetByAnyName_(ss, ["pickups","Pickups","pickup","Pickup","領貨","領貨單","領用","領用單"]);
}

function ensurePickupsSheet_(ss){
  const baseHeaders = ["pickup_id","date","department","receiver","total","items","note","operator","created_at"];
  let sh = getPickupsSheet_(ss);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "pickups", baseHeaders);
  } else {
    const n = String(sh.getName() || "");
    if (n.trim().toLowerCase() === "pickups" && n !== "pickups") {
      try { sh.setName("pickups"); } catch(e) {}
    }
  }

  // 確保 baseHeaders 都存在（缺的補在最後，不重排不覆蓋）
  const headers = getHeaders_(sh);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);
  let lastCol = sh.getLastColumn();
  baseHeaders.forEach(h => {
    if (idx[h] === undefined) {
      lastCol += 1;
      sh.getRange(1, lastCol).setValue(h);
    }
  });
  return sh;
}

/**
 * 領貨單號：yyyyMMdd + 4位流水（例：202602090001）
 * - 會同時掃描 pickups 上可能的單號欄位（pickup_id / id / doc_no / docNo / pickupId）
 * - 避免「舊資料單號在別欄位」造成序號重複
 */
function generatePickupDocNo_(pickupsSh, dateObj){
  const d = dateObj instanceof Date ? dateObj : new Date();
  const prefix = Utilities.formatDate(d, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyyMMdd");

  const headers = getHeaders_(pickupsSh);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  const candidates = ["pickup_id","id","doc_no","docNo","pickupId"];
  const cols = candidates.map(k => idx[k]).filter(v => v !== undefined);

  if (!cols.length) {
    // 若真的沒有任何欄位，就先確保 pickup_id 存在（已在 ensurePickupsSheet_ 保障）
    return generateDailyDocNo_(pickupsSh, "pickup_id", d);
  }

  const lastRow = pickupsSh.getLastRow();
  let maxSeq = 0;

  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    // 逐欄掃描（避免抓整表太大）
    for (let c = 0; c < cols.length; c++){
      const col = cols[c] + 1;
      const vals = pickupsSh.getRange(2, col, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
      for (let i = 0; i < vals.length; i++){
        const s = String(vals[i][0] || "").trim();
        if (!s) continue;
        if (s.length >= 12 && s.indexOf(prefix) === 0) {
          const suf = parseInt(s.slice(8), 10);
          if (!isNaN(suf)) maxSeq = Math.max(maxSeq, suf);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const next = maxSeq + 1;
  const suf4 = ("0000" + next).slice(-4);
  return prefix + suf4;
}

function apiPickups_(respond){
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensurePickupsSheet_(ss);
  const raw = readObjects_(sh);

  const list = raw.map(x => {
    // 兼容可能欄位命名
    const pickup_id = x.pickup_id || x.id || x.pickupId || x.doc_no || x.docNo || x.單號 || x["領貨單號"] || "";
    const date = toISODateLocal_(x.date || x.日期 || x.pickup_date || x.pickupDate || "");
    const department = x.department || x.dept || x.unit || x.單位 || x["領用單位"] || x.target || "";
    const receiver = x.receiver || x.receiver_name || x.領貨人 || x["領用人"] || "";
    const total = (x.total !== undefined && x.total !== "") ? Number(x.total) : Number(x.amount || 0);
    let items = x.items || x.detail || x.item || x["items_json"] || "";
    if (items && typeof items === "string") {
      try { items = JSON.parse(items); } catch(e) { items = []; }
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(items)) items = [];
    const note = x.note || x.remark || x.備註 || "";
    const operator = x.operator || x.user || x.操作人 || "";
    const created_at = toISODateLocal_(x.created_at || x.createdAt || x.建立時間 || "");

    return { pickup_id, date, department, receiver, total, items, note, operator, created_at };
  }).filter(x => String(x.pickup_id || "").trim() !== "");

  return respond({ status: "ok", data: list });
}

function apiManagePickup_(p, respond){
  const action = String(p.action || "").trim(); // add/delete
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  const pickupsSh = ensurePickupsSheet_(ss);
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(10000);

  try{
    if (action === "add"){
      const payload = mustJson_(p.pickup);
      const department = String(payload.department || payload.dept || "").trim();
      const receiver = String(payload.receiver || "").trim();
      const note = String(payload.note || "").trim();
      const items = Array.isArray(payload.items) ? payload.items : [];
      const operator = String(payload.operator || p.operator || "").trim();

      if (!department) return respond({ status:"error", message:"缺少領用單位/門市（department）" });
      if (!items.length) return respond({ status:"error", message:"缺少 items" });

      // 領貨單號：yyyyMMdd + 4位流水（例：202602090001）
      const pickup_id = String(generatePickupDocNo_(pickupsSh, new Date())).trim();

      // products map
      const prodVals = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
      const ph = prodVals[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
      const pidx = headerIndexMap_(ph);
      const productLookup = buildProductLookupMaps_(prodVals, pidx);

      const now = new Date();
      let total = 0;

      // 扣庫存 + 寫 ledger
      for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++){
        const it = items[i];
        const lookupKey = productLookupKeyText_(it);
        const qty = Number(it.qty || it.quantity || 0);
        const unitCost = (it.cost !== undefined && it.cost !== "") ? Number(it.cost) : Number(it.unit_cost || 0);

        if (!lookupKey || !qty || qty <= 0) return respond({ status:"error", message:"品項資料不完整（product_id/qty）" });

        const hit = findProductHitBySkuOrId_(productLookup, it);
        if (!hit) return respond({ status:"error", message:`找不到商品 SKU/ID=${lookupKey}` });
        const pid = String((pidx.sku !== undefined ? hit.row[pidx.sku] : "") || hit.id || lookupKey).trim();

        const current = Number(hit.row[pidx.stock] || 0);
        const next = current - qty;
        if (next < 0) return respond({ status:"error", message:`庫存不足：${hit.row[pidx.name]} current=${current}, qty=${qty}` });

        // update stock
        prodSh.getRange(hit.r + 1, pidx.stock + 1).setValue(next);

        const pname = String(hit.row[pidx.name] || "");
        const target = receiver ? `${department} / ${receiver}` : department;

        writeLedger_(ss, {
          ledger_id: generateId_("LG"),
          product_id: pid,
          product_name: pname,
                    sku: (pidx.sku !== undefined) ? String(hit.row[pidx.sku] || "") : "",
          unit: (pidx.unit !== undefined) ? String(hit.row[pidx.unit] || "") : "",
change: -qty,                // signed change: out = negative
          direction: "OUT",
          reason: "pickup",
          ref_id: pickup_id,
          operator: operator,
          date: toLocalDateTime_(now),
          cost: (pidx.cost !== undefined) ? (Number(hit.row[pidx.cost] || 0) || "") : "",
          target: target
        });

        // total cost (optional)
        if (!isNaN(unitCost) && unitCost > 0) total += unitCost * qty;
      }

      // 寫 pickups（items 存 JSON）
      // 同時寫入 pickup_id 與 id（若有）以兼容舊表頭（appendObject_ 只會寫入表頭存在的欄位）
      appendObject_(pickupsSh, {
        pickup_id: pickup_id,
        id: pickup_id,
        doc_no: pickup_id,
        date: payload.date || toROCDateString_(new Date()),
        department: department,
        receiver: receiver,
        total: total,
        items: JSON.stringify(items),
        note: note,
        operator: operator,
        created_at: now
      });

      return respond({ status:"ok", message:`領貨完成：${pickup_id}`, pickup_id: pickup_id });
    }

    if (action === "delete"){
      const pickup_id = String(p.pickup_id || p.id || "").trim();
      if (!pickup_id) return respond({ status:"error", message:"缺少 pickup_id" });

      const rows = pickupsSh.getDataRange().getValues();
      const headers = rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
      const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

      const candidates = ["pickup_id","id","doc_no","docNo","pickupId"];
      const cols = candidates.map(k => idx[k]).filter(v => v !== undefined);
      const col = cols.length ? cols[0] : 0;

      for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++){
        if (String(rows[r][col] || "").trim() === pickup_id){
          pickupsSh.deleteRow(r + 1);
          return respond({ status:"ok", message:"領貨單已刪除（未回滾庫存）" });
        }
      }
      return respond({ status:"error", message:"找不到領貨單" });
    }

    return respond({ status:"error", message:"未知 action" });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch(e2) {}
  }
}

// ----------------- Orders (list/manage) -----------------
function apiOrders_(respond) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureOrdersSheet_(ss);
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(getHeaders_(sh));
  const list = readObjectsWithRowNo_(sh).map(function(x) {
    return normalizeOrderRecordForResponse_(x);
  });
  persistLegacyOrderShippingDates_(sh, idx, list);
  list.forEach(function(x){ try { delete x.__rowNo; delete x.__legacy_shipping_date_backfilled; } catch(e) {} });
  return respond({ status: "ok", data: list });
}


function parseOrderPriceEditCompactPayload_(p) {
  const compact = p && p.payload_compact ? mustJson_(p.payload_compact) : null;
  if (!compact || typeof compact !== 'object') return null;
  const prices = Array.isArray(compact.pr) ? compact.pr : [];
  return {
    shipping_date: toISODateLocal_(compact.sd || compact.shipping_date || ""),
    prices: prices.map(function(v){ return Number(v || 0); })
  };
}

function apiManageOrder_(p, respond) {
  const action = String(p.action || "").trim();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureOrdersSheet_(ss);
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const headers = rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  const order_id = String(p.order_id || p.id || "").trim();
  if (!order_id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 order_id" });

  if (action === "update") {
    const status = String(p.status || "").trim();
    if (!status) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 status" });
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.order_id]).trim() === order_id) {
        const row = rows[r].slice();
        const source = String(rows[r][idx.source] || "").trim();
        const linkedPurchaseId = String(rows[r][idx.linked_purchase_id] || "").trim();
        const stockApplied = Number(rows[r][idx.stock_applied] || 0) ? 1 : 0;
        let items = rows[r][idx.items];
        if (typeof items === "string" && items) {
          try { items = JSON.parse(items); } catch (e) { items = []; }
        }
        if (!Array.isArray(items)) items = [];

        const incomingOperator = String(p.operator || "").trim();
        const existingOperator = String(rows[r][idx.operator] || "").trim();
        const shipmentOperator = incomingOperator || existingOperator;

        if (status === "已出貨" && !stockApplied) {
          applyOrderShipment_(ss, prodSh, order_id, items, String(rows[r][idx.name] || "").trim(), shipmentOperator);
          if (idx.stock_applied !== undefined) row[idx.stock_applied] = 1;
        }
        if (idx.status !== undefined) row[idx.status] = status;
        if (idx.operator !== undefined && incomingOperator) {
          if (status === "已出貨" || (!existingOperator && status === "已完成")) {
            row[idx.operator] = incomingOperator;
          }
        }
        sh.getRange(r + 1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([row.slice(0, headers.length)]);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "訂單已更新" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到訂單" });
  }

  if (action === "updateShippingDate") {
    const shippingDate = toISODateLocal_(p.shipping_date || "");
    if (!shippingDate) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少出貨日期" });
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.order_id]).trim() !== order_id) continue;
      if (idx.shipping_date !== undefined) {
        const row = rows[r].slice();
        row[idx.shipping_date] = shippingDate;
        sh.getRange(r + 1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([row.slice(0, headers.length)]);
      }
      return respond({ status: "ok", message: "出貨日期已更新", shipping_date: shippingDate });
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到訂單" });
  }

  if (action === "updatePrices") {
    const compactPayload = parseOrderPriceEditCompactPayload_(p);
    const payload = compactPayload ? null : (p.payload ? mustJson_(p.payload) : null);
    const incomingItems = Array.isArray(payload && payload.items) ? payload.items : [];
    const incomingPrices = Array.isArray(compactPayload && compactPayload.prices) ? compactPayload.prices : [];
    const incomingShippingDate = String((compactPayload && compactPayload.shipping_date) || toISODateLocal_((payload && payload.shipping_date) || p.shipping_date || "") || "").trim();
    if (!incomingItems.length && !incomingPrices.length) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 items" });

    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.order_id]).trim() !== order_id) continue;

      let existingItems = rows[r][idx.items];
      if (typeof existingItems === "string" && existingItems) {
        try { existingItems = JSON.parse(existingItems); } catch (e) { existingItems = []; }
      }
      if (!Array.isArray(existingItems)) existingItems = [];

      const normalizedItems = (incomingPrices.length ? existingItems : incomingItems).map((it, i) => {
        const base = existingItems[i] && typeof existingItems[i] === 'object' ? existingItems[i] : {};
        const merged = incomingPrices.length ? Object.assign({}, base) : Object.assign({}, base, it || {});
        const qty = Number(merged.qty != null ? merged.qty : (merged.Quantity != null ? merged.Quantity : (merged.quantity != null ? merged.quantity : 0)));
        const nextPrice = incomingPrices.length
          ? Number(incomingPrices[i] != null ? incomingPrices[i] : (merged.price != null ? merged.price : (merged.UnitPrice != null ? merged.UnitPrice : (merged.unit_price != null ? merged.unit_price : 0))))
          : Number(merged.price != null ? merged.price : (merged.UnitPrice != null ? merged.UnitPrice : (merged.unit_price != null ? merged.unit_price : 0)));
        const subtotal = Number(merged.subtotal != null ? merged.subtotal : (merged.Subtotal != null ? merged.Subtotal : (qty * nextPrice)));
        merged.qty = qty;
        merged.price = nextPrice;
        merged.subtotal = subtotal;
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(merged, 'Quantity')) merged.Quantity = qty;
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(merged, 'quantity')) merged.quantity = qty;
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(merged, 'UnitPrice')) merged.UnitPrice = nextPrice;
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(merged, 'unit_price')) merged.unit_price = nextPrice;
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(merged, 'Subtotal')) merged.Subtotal = subtotal;
        return merged;
      });

      const total = normalizedItems.reduce((sum, it) => {
        const qty = Number(it.qty != null ? it.qty : 0);
        const price = Number(it.price != null ? it.price : 0);
        return sum + Number(it.subtotal != null ? it.subtotal : (qty * price));
      }, 0);

      const row = rows[r].slice();
      if (idx.items !== undefined) row[idx.items] = JSON.stringify(normalizedItems);
      if (idx.total !== undefined) row[idx.total] = total;
      if (incomingShippingDate && idx.shipping_date !== undefined) row[idx.shipping_date] = incomingShippingDate;
      sh.getRange(r + 1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([row.slice(0, headers.length)]);

      const orderItemsSh = getSheetByAnyName_(ss, ["orderItems"]);
      if (orderItemsSh) {
        ensureSheet_(ss, orderItemsSh.getName(), ["OrderItemID","OrderID","ProductID","Quantity","UnitPrice","Subtotal"]);
        const itemRows = orderItemsSh.getDataRange().getValues();
        const itemIdx = headerIndexMap_(itemRows[0] || []);
        const matched = [];
        for (let rr = 1; rr < itemRows.length; rr++) {
          if (String(itemRows[rr][itemIdx.OrderID] || '').trim() === order_id) matched.push(rr + 1);
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < matched.length; i++) {
          const item = normalizedItems[i] || normalizedItems[normalizedItems.length - 1] || null;
          if (!item) continue;
          const rowNo = matched[i];
          const qty = Number(item.qty != null ? item.qty : 0);
          const price = Number(item.price != null ? item.price : 0);
          const subtotal = Number(item.subtotal != null ? item.subtotal : (qty * price));
          if (itemIdx.Quantity !== undefined) orderItemsSh.getRange(rowNo, itemIdx.Quantity + 1).setValue(qty);
          if (itemIdx.UnitPrice !== undefined) orderItemsSh.getRange(rowNo, itemIdx.UnitPrice + 1).setValue(price);
          if (itemIdx.Subtotal !== undefined) orderItemsSh.getRange(rowNo, itemIdx.Subtotal + 1).setValue(subtotal);
        }
      }

      return respond({ status: "ok", message: "銷貨單已更新", total: total, shipping_date: incomingShippingDate || String(rows[r][idx.shipping_date] || '').trim() });
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到訂單" });
  }

  if (action === "delete") {
    for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
      if (String(rows[r][idx.order_id]).trim() === order_id) {
        const status = String(rows[r][idx.status] || "").trim() || "待出貨";
        if (status === "已完成") return respond({ status: "error", message: "已完成的訂單不可刪除" });
        sh.deleteRow(r + 1);
        return respond({ status: "ok", message: "訂單已刪除（未回滾庫存）" });
      }
    }
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到訂單" });
  }

  return respond({ status: "error", message: "未知 action" });
}


// ----------------- LINE 推播：銷貨單建立通知 -----------------
function lineOrderPushHeaders_(){
  return ["log_id","created_at","order_id","source","target","status","response_code","message"];
}

function writeLineOrderPushLog_(ss, obj){
  try {
    const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "LineOrderPushLog", lineOrderPushHeaders_());
    appendObject_(sh, {
      log_id: generateId_("LN"),
      created_at: new Date(),
      order_id: String(obj && obj.order_id || ""),
      source: String(obj && obj.source || ""),
      target: String(obj && obj.target || ""),
      status: String(obj && obj.status || ""),
      response_code: String(obj && obj.response_code || ""),
      message: String(obj && obj.message || "")
    });
  } catch (e) {}
}

function hasSuccessfulLineOrderPushLog_(ss, orderId){
  const target = String(orderId || "").trim();
  if (!target) return false;
  try {
    const sh = getSheetByAnyName_(ss, ["LineOrderPushLog"]);
    if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return false;
    const rows = readObjects_(sh);
    for (let i = rows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const row = rows[i] || {};
      if (String(row.order_id || "").trim() !== target) continue;
      if (String(row.status || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "ok") return true;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}

function isLineOrderPushEnabled_(settings){
  const raw = String(settings && settings.line_order_push_enabled || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  return raw === "1" || raw === "true" || raw === "yes" || raw === "on" || raw === "啟用" || raw === "是";
}

function normalizeLineToken_(v){
  return String(v || "").trim().replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, "");
}

function orderLineQtyText_(v){
  try {
    if (typeof formatPurchaseQtyText_ === "function") return formatPurchaseQtyText_(v);
  } catch(e) {}
  const n = Number(v || 0);
  if (!isFinite(n)) return String(v || "");
  return (Math.round(n * 10) / 10).toString().replace(/\.0$/, "");
}

function buildLineOrderSummaryText_(order, items, options){
  order = order || {};
  options = options || {};
  const title = String(options.title || "電商訂單通知").trim() || "電商訂單通知";
  const shippingDate = toISODateLocal_(order.shipping_date || "") || toISODateLocal_(order.date || "");
  const customerName = String(order.name || "").trim();
  const phone = String(order.phone || "").trim();
  const address = String(order.address || "").trim();
  const list = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];

  const lines = [];
  lines.push(`【${title}】`);
  if (shippingDate) lines.push(`出貨日期：${shippingDate}`);
  if (customerName) lines.push(`客戶：${customerName}`);
  if (phone) lines.push(`電話：${phone}`);
  if (address) lines.push(`地址：${address}`);
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("商品明細：");
  if (!list.length) {
    lines.push("（無）");
  } else {
    list.forEach(function(it, i){
      const name = String(it && (it.name || it.product_name || it.product || "") || "未命名商品").trim();
      const qty = orderLineQtyText_(it && (it.qty !== undefined ? it.qty : (it.quantity !== undefined ? it.quantity : it.Quantity)));
      const unit = String(it && it.unit || "").trim();
      // LINE 商品明細只顯示品名與數量，不推播任何金額。
      lines.push(`${i + 1}. ${name} × ${qty}${unit ? unit : ""}`);
    });
  }
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function splitLinePushText_(text){
  const src = String(text || "");
  const maxLen = 4500;
  const out = [];
  let index = 0;
  while (index < src.length && out.length < 5) {
    let chunk = src.substring(index, index + maxLen);
    const lastNewline = chunk.lastIndexOf("\n");
    if (lastNewline > 500 && index + lastNewline < src.length) {
      chunk = src.substring(index, index + lastNewline);
      index += lastNewline + 1;
    } else {
      index += maxLen;
    }
    out.push(chunk);
  }
  return out.length ? out : [src];
}

function sendLineOrderCreatedPush_(ss, order, items, options){
  const settings = getSystemSettingsMap_(ss);
  const enabled = isLineOrderPushEnabled_(settings);
  const token = normalizeLineToken_(settings.line_order_channel_access_token || settings.line_channel_access_token || settings.line_token || "");
  const groupId = String(settings.line_order_group_id || settings.line_group_id || "").trim();
  const orderId = String(order && order.order_id || "").trim();
  const source = String(options && options.source_label || order && order.source || "").trim();
  const orderSource = String(order && order.source || "").trim().toUpperCase();

  // 只針對電商平台訂單推播；後台手動建立的 ADMIN 銷貨單不推播。
  if (orderSource && orderSource !== "EC") return { skipped: true, reason: "non_ec_order" };
  if (!enabled) return { skipped: true, reason: "disabled" };
  if (hasSuccessfulLineOrderPushLog_(ss, orderId)) return { skipped: true, reason: "already_pushed" };
  if (!token || !groupId) {
    writeLineOrderPushLog_(ss, { order_id: orderId, source: source, target: groupId, status: "skipped", message: "LINE token 或 group id 未設定" });
    return { skipped: true, reason: "missing_token_or_group_id" };
  }

  const title = String(settings.line_order_push_title || "電商訂單通知").trim() || "電商訂單通知";
  const text = buildLineOrderSummaryText_(order, items, { source_label: source, title: title });
  const chunks = splitLinePushText_(text).map(function(t){ return { type: "text", text: t }; });
  const payload = { to: groupId, messages: chunks.slice(0, 5) };

  try {
    const res = UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://api.line.me/v2/bot/message/push", {
      method: "post",
      contentType: "application/json",
      headers: { Authorization: "Bearer " + token },
      payload: JSON.stringify(payload),
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    });
    const code = res.getResponseCode();
    const body = String(res.getContentText() || "").slice(0, 500);
    const ok = code >= 200 && code < 300;
    writeLineOrderPushLog_(ss, { order_id: orderId, source: source, target: groupId, status: ok ? "ok" : "error", response_code: code, message: body });
    return { ok: ok, response_code: code, message: body };
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = String(e && e.message || e || "").slice(0, 500);
    writeLineOrderPushLog_(ss, { order_id: orderId, source: source, target: groupId, status: "error", message: msg });
    return { ok: false, message: msg };
  }
}

// ----------------- manageSale (後台銷貨/出庫) -----------------

function expandAdminSaleCompactPayload_(compact) {
  compact = compact || {};
  const rows = Array.isArray(compact.it) ? compact.it : [];
  return {
    shipping_date: compact.sd || compact.shipping_date || "",
    name: compact.n || compact.name || "",
    customer_id: compact.cid || compact.customer_id || "",
    phone: compact.ph || compact.phone || "",
    address: compact.ad || compact.address || "",
    remark: compact.rm || compact.remark || compact.note || "",
    total: compact.t || compact.total || 0,
    operator: compact.op || compact.operator || "",
    items: rows.map(function(row) {
      if (Array.isArray(row)) {
        return {
          product_id: row[0] || "",
          sku: row[0] || "",
          qty: row[1] || 0,
          price: row[2] || 0
        };
      }
      return row || {};
    })
  };
}

function parseAdminSalePayload_(p) {
  if (p && p.sale_compact) return expandAdminSaleCompactPayload_(mustJson_(p.sale_compact));
  return mustJson_(p && p.sale);
}

function normalizeAdminSaleItems_(items, prodSh) {
  const list = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
  const ph = getHeaders_(prodSh);
  const pidx = headerIndexMap_(ph);
  const prodVals = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const productLookup = buildProductLookupMaps_(prodVals, pidx);
  const out = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
    const it = list[i] || {};
    const lookupKey = productLookupKeyText_(it);
    const qty = cleanDecimalInput_(it.qty || it.quantity || 0, 6);
    const price = cleanDecimalInput_(it.price || it.unit_price || it.UnitPrice || 0, 6);
    if (!lookupKey || !(qty > 0)) throw new Error("銷貨品項資料不完整");
    const hit = findProductHitBySkuOrId_(productLookup, it);
    if (!hit) throw new Error(`找不到商品 SKU/ID=${lookupKey}`);

    const row = hit.row || [];
    const sku = pidx.sku !== undefined ? String(row[pidx.sku] || "").trim() : "";
    const rawId = pidx.id !== undefined ? String(row[pidx.id] || "").trim() : "";
    const productKey = sku || rawId || lookupKey;
    const name = pidx.name !== undefined ? String(row[pidx.name] || it.name || it.product_name || "").trim() : String(it.name || it.product_name || "").trim();
    const unit = pidx.unit !== undefined ? String(row[pidx.unit] || it.unit || "").trim() : String(it.unit || "").trim();
    const spec = pidx.spec !== undefined ? String(row[pidx.spec] || it.spec || "").trim() : String(it.spec || "").trim();
    const subtotal = mulDecimalInput_(qty, price, 6);

    out.push({
      product_id: productKey,
      sku: sku,
      raw_id: rawId,
      product_name: name,
      name: name,
      unit: unit,
      spec: spec,
      qty: qty,
      price: price,
      subtotal: subtotal
    });
  }
  return out;
}

function apiManageSale_(p, respond) {
  const action = String(p.action || "").trim();
  if (action !== "add") return respond({ status: "error", message: "未知 action" });

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const ordersSh = ensureOrdersSheet_(ss);
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(10000);

  try {
    const payload = parseAdminSalePayload_(p) || {};
    const name = String(payload.name || "").trim();
    const phone = String(payload.phone || "").trim();
    const address = String(payload.address || "").trim();
    const remark = String(payload.remark || payload.note || "").trim();
    const now = new Date();
    const orderDate = toISODateLocal_(now);
    const shippingDate = toISODateLocal_(payload.shipping_date || "") || orderDate;
    const items = normalizeAdminSaleItems_(payload.items, prodSh);
    const total = items.reduce(function(sum, it){ return addDecimalInput_(sum, Number(it.subtotal || (Number(it.qty || 0) * Number(it.price || 0))) || 0, 6); }, 0);
    const operator = String(payload.operator || "").trim();

    if (!items.length) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 items" });

    const order_id = String(generateDailyDocNo_(ordersSh, "order_id", now)).trim();

    const orderItemsSh = getSheetByAnyName_(ss, ["orderItems"]);
    if (orderItemsSh) {
      ensureSheet_(ss, orderItemsSh.getName(), ["OrderItemID","OrderID","ProductID","Quantity","UnitPrice","Subtotal"]);
      for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        const it = items[i];
        appendObject_(orderItemsSh, {
          OrderItemID: generateId_("OI"),
          OrderID: order_id,
          ProductID: it.product_id,
          Quantity: Number(it.qty),
          UnitPrice: Number(it.price || 0),
          Subtotal: Number(it.subtotal || (Number(it.qty || 0) * Number(it.price || 0)))
        });
      }
    }

    const orderObj = {
      order_id: order_id,
      name: name,
      phone: phone,
      address: address,
      shipping_date: shippingDate,
      total: total,
      items: JSON.stringify(items),
      date: orderDate,
      member_id: "",
      status: "待出貨",
      operator: operator,
      source: "ADMIN",
      stock_applied: 0,
      linked_purchase_id: "",
      remark: remark,
      created_at: now
    };

    appendObject_(ordersSh, orderObj);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    return respond({ status: "ok", message: `銷貨單已建立：${order_id}`, order_id: order_id });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
  }
}

// ----------------- Stock ledger & adjust -----------------
function apiStockLedger_(respond) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureLedgerSheet_(ss);
  return respond({ status: "ok", data: readObjects_(sh) });
}

function apiProductLedger_(p, respond) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureLedgerSheet_(ss);
  const pid = String(p.product_id || p.id || "").trim();
  const sku = String(p.sku || "").trim();
  if (!sku && !pid) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 sku 或 product_id" });

  const limit = Math.max(1, Math.min(1000, Number(p.limit || 300)));

  const from = String(p.from || "").trim(); // yyyy-MM-dd (optional)
  const to = String(p.to || "").trim();     // yyyy-MM-dd (optional)

  const list = readObjects_(sh).filter(x => {
    const xs = String(x.sku || "").trim();
    if (sku && xs && xs === sku) return true;
    return pid ? (String(x.product_id || x.id || "").trim() === pid) : false;
  });

  // 日期篩選（用 date，若無則不篩）
  let rows = list;
  if (from) {
    const ft = Date.parse(from + "T00:00:00");
    rows = rows.filter(x => {
      const t = Date.parse(String(x.date || "").replace(" ", "T"));
      return isNaN(t) ? true : (t >= ft);
    });
  }
  if (to) {
    const tt = Date.parse(to + "T23:59:59");
    rows = rows.filter(x => {
      const t = Date.parse(String(x.date || "").replace(" ", "T"));
      return isNaN(t) ? true : (t <= tt);
    });
  }

  // 依時間倒序（date）
  rows.sort((a,b) => {
    const ta = Date.parse(String(a.date || "").replace(" ", "T"));
    const tb = Date.parse(String(b.date || "").replace(" ", "T"));
    return (isNaN(tb)?0:tb) - (isNaN(ta)?0:ta);
  });

  return respond({ status: "ok", data: rows.slice(0, limit) });
}


function pickHistoryReferencePrice_(row) {
  const ref = parseLooseNumber_(row && row.reference_price);
  if (ref !== '' && ref !== null && ref !== undefined && !isNaN(Number(ref))) return Number(ref);
  const upper = parseLooseNumber_(row && row.upper_price);
  if (upper !== '' && upper !== null && upper !== undefined && !isNaN(Number(upper))) return Number(upper);
  const middle = parseLooseNumber_(row && row.middle_price);
  if (middle !== '' && middle !== null && middle !== undefined && !isNaN(Number(middle))) return Number(middle);
  const lower = parseLooseNumber_(row && row.lower_price);
  if (lower !== '' && lower !== null && lower !== undefined && !isNaN(Number(lower))) return Number(lower);
  return '';
}

function apiProductMarketHistory_(p, respond) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const histSh = ensureSheet_(ss, 'MarketPriceHistory', MARKET_PRICE_HISTORY_HEADERS_);
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, 'Products', ['id','name','sku','price','image','stock','category','date','cost','unit','safety_stock','supplier_ids','expiry_date','last_purchase_date','spec','reference_price','reference_price_date','reference_price_name','shop_enabled']);

  const pid = String(p.product_id || p.id || '').trim();
  const sku = String(p.sku || '').trim();
  if (!pid && !sku) return respond({ status: 'error', message: '缺少 sku 或 product_id' });

  const limit = Math.max(1, Math.min(2000, Number(p.limit || 1000)));
  const from = normalizeLooseDate_(String(p.from || '').trim());
  const to = normalizeLooseDate_(String(p.to || '').trim());

  const products = readObjects_(prodSh);
  let product = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < products.length; i++) {
    const row = products[i] || {};
    const rowId = String(row.id || '').trim();
    const rowSku = String(row.sku || row.part_no || row.code || '').trim();
    if (pid && rowId === pid) { product = row; break; }
    if (!product && sku && rowSku && rowSku === sku) product = row;
  }

  const productId = pid || String(product && product.id || '').trim();
  const productName = String(product && product.name || '').trim();
  const productSpec = String(product && product.spec || '').trim();

  const rows = readObjects_(histSh).map(r => ({
    history_id: String(r.history_id || '').trim(),
    batch_id: String(r.batch_id || '').trim(),
    sync_time: String(r.sync_time || '').trim(),
    sync_source: String(r.sync_source || '').trim(),
    product_id: String(r.product_id || '').trim(),
    product_name: String(r.product_name || '').trim(),
    product_spec: String(r.product_spec || '').trim(),
    reference_price: pickHistoryReferencePrice_(r),
    reference_price_date: normalizeLooseDate_(r.reference_price_date || ''),
    reference_price_name: String(r.reference_price_name || '').trim(),
    market: normalizeMarketLabel_(r.market || ''),
    market_label: String(r.market_label || '').trim() || marketDisplayLabel_(normalizeMarketLabel_(r.market || '')),
    market_date: normalizeLooseDate_(r.market_date || ''),
    raw_name: String(r.raw_name || '').trim(),
    raw_spec: String(r.raw_spec || '').trim(),
    upper_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.upper_price),
    middle_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.middle_price),
    lower_price: parseLooseNumber_(r.lower_price),
    matched: String(r.matched || '').trim()
  })).filter(r => {
    if (!r.market_date) return false;
    if (productId && r.product_id === productId) return true;
    if (!productId && productName && r.product_name === productName && (!productSpec || r.product_spec === productSpec)) return true;
    return false;
  }).filter(r => {
    if (from && r.market_date < from) return false;
    if (to && r.market_date > to) return false;
    return true;
  });

  rows.sort((a, b) => {
    const dateCmp = String(b.market_date || '').localeCompare(String(a.market_date || ''));
    if (dateCmp !== 0) return dateCmp;
    const syncCmp = String(b.sync_time || '').localeCompare(String(a.sync_time || ''));
    if (syncCmp !== 0) return syncCmp;
    return String(b.history_id || '').localeCompare(String(a.history_id || ''));
  });

  const deduped = [];
  const seenDate = {};
  rows.forEach(r => {
    const key = String(r.market_date || '').trim();
    if (!key) return;
    if (seenDate[key]) return;
    seenDate[key] = true;
    deduped.push(r);
  });

  return respond({
    status: 'ok',
    product_id: productId,
    product_name: productName,
    product_spec: productSpec,
    data: deduped.slice(0, limit)
  });
}

function apiStockAdjust_(p, respond) {
  const product_id = String(p.product_id || "").trim();
  const delta = Number(p.delta || 0);
  const reason = String(p.reason || "adjust").trim();
  const operator = String(p.operator || "").trim();
  if (!product_id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 product_id" });
  if (!delta || isNaN(delta)) return respond({ status: "error", message: "delta 必須是非 0 數字" });

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(10000);
  try {
    const vals = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = vals[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
    const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

    let row = -1;
    for (let r = 1; r < vals.length; r++) {
      if (String(vals[r][idx.id]).trim() === product_id) { row = r; break; }
    }
    if (row === -1) return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到商品" });

    const current = Number(vals[row][idx.stock] || 0);
    const next = current + delta;
    if (next < 0) return respond({ status: "error", message: `庫存不足 current=${current}, delta=${delta}` });

    prodSh.getRange(row + 1, idx.stock + 1).setValue(next);

    const pname = String(vals[row][idx.name] || "");
    const now = new Date();
    writeLedger_(ss, {
      ledger_id: generateId_("LG"),
      product_id: product_id,
      product_name: pname,
            sku: (idx.sku !== undefined) ? String(vals[row][idx.sku] || "") : "",
      unit: (idx.unit !== undefined) ? String(vals[row][idx.unit] || "") : "",
change: delta,                 // signed change：增加為正、減少為負
      direction: "ADJ",
      reason: "adjust",
      ref_id: "ADJ-" + Utilities.formatDate(now, "Asia/Taipei", "yyyyMMddHHmmss"),
      operator: operator,
      date: toLocalDateTime_(now),
      cost: (idx.cost !== undefined) ? (Number(vals[row][idx.cost] || 0) || "") : "",
      target: reason                 // 調整原因放在 target（便於前台顯示）
    });

    return respond({ status: "ok", message: "庫存已調整", before: current, after: next });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
  }
}

function systemSettingsHeaders_(){
  return ["key","value","updated_at"];
}

function getSystemSettingsMap_(ss){
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "SystemSettings", systemSettingsHeaders_());
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const map = {};
  for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const key = String(rows[r][0] || "").trim();
    if (!key) continue;
    map[key] = String(rows[r][1] || "");
  }
  return map;
}

function upsertSystemSetting_(sh, key, value){
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    if (String(rows[r][0] || "").trim() === key) {
      sh.getRange(r + 1, 2).setValue(value);
      sh.getRange(r + 1, 3).setValue(new Date());
      return;
    }
  }
  appendObject_(sh, { key: key, value: value, updated_at: new Date() });
}

function apiSystemSettings_(respond){
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const map = getSystemSettingsMap_(ss);
  return respond({
    status: "ok",
    settings: {
      driver_name: String(map.driver_name || ""),
      driver_phone: String(map.driver_phone || ""),
      sales_phone: String(map.sales_phone || ""),
      sales_name: String(map.sales_name || ""),
      line_order_push_enabled: String(map.line_order_push_enabled || "0"),
      line_order_channel_access_token: String(map.line_order_channel_access_token || ""),
      line_order_group_id: String(map.line_order_group_id || ""),
      line_order_push_title: String(map.line_order_push_title || "電商訂單通知")
    }
  });
}

function apiManageSystemSettings_(p, respond){
  const action = String(p.action || "save").trim();
  if (action !== "save") return respond({ status: "error", message: "未知 action" });
  const payload = p.settings ? mustJson_(p.settings) : p;
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "SystemSettings", systemSettingsHeaders_());
  upsertSystemSetting_(sh, "driver_name", String(payload.driver_name || p.driver_name || "").trim());
  upsertSystemSetting_(sh, "driver_phone", String(payload.driver_phone || p.driver_phone || "").trim());
  upsertSystemSetting_(sh, "sales_phone", String(payload.sales_phone || p.sales_phone || "").trim());
  upsertSystemSetting_(sh, "sales_name", String(payload.sales_name || p.sales_name || "").trim());
  upsertSystemSetting_(sh, "line_order_push_enabled", String(payload.line_order_push_enabled || p.line_order_push_enabled || "0").trim());
  upsertSystemSetting_(sh, "line_order_channel_access_token", normalizeLineToken_(payload.line_order_channel_access_token || p.line_order_channel_access_token || ""));
  upsertSystemSetting_(sh, "line_order_group_id", String(payload.line_order_group_id || p.line_order_group_id || "").trim());
  upsertSystemSetting_(sh, "line_order_push_title", String(payload.line_order_push_title || p.line_order_push_title || "電商訂單通知").trim());
  return respond({ status: "ok", message: "系統設定已儲存" });
}


// ----------------- lastUpdate -----------------
function apiLastUpdate_(respond) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const headers = rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const dateIndex = headers.indexOf("date");
  if (dateIndex === -1) return respond({ status: "error", message: "找不到 date 欄位" });

  let latest = "";
  for (let r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const d = rows[r][dateIndex];
    if (!d) continue;
    const ds = normalizeDate_(d);
    if (!latest || compareDate_(ds, latest) > 0) latest = ds;
  }
  return respond({ status: "ok", lastUpdate: latest });
}


/* =========================================================
 * ✅ 前台（商城）會員註冊 / 下單 / 查單 API
 * - type=register  : 會員註冊（寫入 Members）
 * - type=order     : 前台下單（寫入 Orders、扣庫存、寫入 stock_ledger、可寫 orderItems）
 * - type=myOrders  : 查詢會員自己的訂單列表
 * - type=getOrder  : 依 order_id 查詢訂單明細（items 會格式化成可直接顯示的字串）
 * ========================================================= */

function apiMemberRegister_(p, respond) {
  const name = String(p.name || "").trim();
  const username = String(p.username || "").trim();
  const password = String(p.password || "").trim();

  if (!name || !username || !password) {
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少註冊資料" });
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Members", ["id","name","username","password","created_at","role"]);
  const rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  const headers = rows[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
  const idx = headerIndexMap_(headers);

  // 帳號重複檢查
  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const u = String(rows[i][idx.username] || "").trim();
    if (u === username) {
      return respond({ status: "error", message: "帳號已存在" });
    }
  }

  // 產生新 id（取目前最大值 + 1）
  let newId = 1;
  if (rows.length > 1 && idx.id !== undefined) {
    let maxId = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
      const v = Number(String(rows[i][idx.id] || "").trim());
      if (!isNaN(v)) maxId = Math.max(maxId, v);
    }
    newId = maxId + 1;
  }

  appendObject_(sh, {
    id: newId,
    name: name,
    username: username,
    password: password,
    created_at: new Date(),
    role: "user"
  });

  return respond({ status: "ok", message: "註冊成功", id: newId });
}

function parseEcOrderCartPayload_(p) {
  p = p || {};
  if (p.cart_compact) {
    const text = String(p.cart_compact || '').trim();
    if (!text) return [];
    // compact format: SKU~qty~price;SKU~qty~price
    return text.split(';').map(function(part){
      const cells = String(part || '').split('~');
      const key = String(cells[0] || '').trim();
      const qty = Number(String(cells[1] || '').replace(/,/g, '').trim() || 0);
      const price = Number(String(cells[2] || '').replace(/,/g, '').trim() || 0);
      if (!key || !(qty > 0)) return null;
      return { id: key, sku: key, product_id: key, qty: qty, price: Number.isFinite(price) ? price : 0 };
    }).filter(function(x){ return !!x; });
  }
  if (p.order_compact) {
    const compact = mustJson_(p.order_compact) || {};
    const rows = Array.isArray(compact.it) ? compact.it : [];
    return rows.map(function(row){
      if (Array.isArray(row)) {
        const key = String(row[0] || '').trim();
        const qty = Number(row[1] || 0);
        const price = Number(row[2] || 0);
        return key && qty > 0 ? { id: key, sku: key, product_id: key, qty: qty, price: Number.isFinite(price) ? price : 0 } : null;
      }
      return row || null;
    }).filter(function(x){ return !!x; });
  }
  let cart = [];
  try {
    const raw = String(p.cart || "[]");
    const decoded = decodeURIComponent(raw);
    cart = JSON.parse(decoded);
  } catch (e) {
    try { cart = JSON.parse(String(p.cart || "[]")); } catch (e2) { cart = []; }
  }
  return Array.isArray(cart) ? cart : [];
}

function apiOrderCreate_(p, respond) {
  const name = String(p.name || "").trim();
  const phone = String(p.phone || "").trim();
  const address = String(p.address || "").trim();
  const member_id = String(p.member_id || "").trim();
  const shipping_date = toISODateLocal_(p.shipping_date || "");
  const request_token = String(p.request_token || "").trim();

  if (!name || !phone || !address) {
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少收件資訊" });
  }

  const cart = parseEcOrderCartPayload_(p);
  if (!Array.isArray(cart) || cart.length === 0) {
    return respond({ status: "error", message: "購物車為空" });
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const ordersSh = ensureOrdersSheet_(ss);
  const prodSh = ensureSheet_(ss, "Products", ["id","name","sku","price","image","stock","category","date","cost","unit","safety_stock","supplier_ids","expiry_date","last_purchase_date","spec","reference_price","reference_price_date","reference_price_name","shop_enabled"]);
  const purchasesSh = ensurePurchasesSheet_(ss);

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);

  try {
    const existingOrders = readObjects_(ordersSh);
    const existing = findOrderByRequestToken_(existingOrders, request_token);
    if (existing) {
      let existingItems = existing.items;
      if (typeof existingItems === "string" && existingItems) {
        try { existingItems = JSON.parse(existingItems); } catch (e) { existingItems = []; }
      }
      if (!Array.isArray(existingItems)) existingItems = [];
      try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (eUnlock) {}
      const linePushResult = sendLineOrderCreatedPush_(ss, existing, existingItems, { source_label: "電商訂單" });
      return respond({ status: "ok", message: "下單成功", order_id: existing.order_id, total: existing.total, linked_purchase_id: existing.linked_purchase_id || "", line_push: linePushResult });
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const orderDate = toISODateLocal_(now);
    const deliveryDate = shipping_date || orderDate;
    const order_id = String(generateDailyDocNo_(ordersSh, "order_id", now)).trim();

    const prodVals = prodSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    const ph = prodVals[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
    const pidx = headerIndexMap_(ph);
    const productLookup = buildProductLookupMaps_(prodVals, pidx);
    const supplierMap = buildSupplierNameMap_(ss);

    const items = [];
    const purchaseItems = [];
    let total = 0;
    let firstCategory = "";

    for (let i = 0; i < cart.length; i++) {
      const it = cart[i] || {};
      const lookupKey = productLookupKeyText_(it);
      const qty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(it.qty || it.quantity || 0);
      const price = cleanDecimalInput_(it.price || 0, 6);
      if (!lookupKey || !(qty > 0)) return respond({ status: "error", message: "購物車品項資料不完整" });
      const hit = findProductHitBySkuOrId_(productLookup, it);
      if (!hit) return respond({ status: "error", message: `找不到商品 SKU/ID=${lookupKey}` });
      const row = hit.row;
      const productInternalId = String(hit.id || "").trim();
      const productSku = String((pidx.sku !== undefined ? row[pidx.sku] : "") || lookupKey).trim();
      const pid = productSku || productInternalId || lookupKey;
      const pname = String(it.name || row[pidx.name] || "");
      const category = String(row[pidx.category] || "").trim();
      if (!firstCategory && category) firstCategory = category;
      const stock = cleanDecimalInput_(row[pidx.stock] || 0, 6);
      const safety = cleanDecimalInput_(row[pidx.safety_stock] || 0, 6);
      const suggestedQty = roundPurchaseQtyNumber_(Math.max(0, addDecimalInput_(qty, safety, 6) - stock));
      const supplierIds = parseSupplierIdsFromProductRow_(row, pidx);
      const supplier_id = String(supplierIds[0] || "").trim();
      const supplier_name = String(supplierMap[supplier_id] || "").trim();
      const sub = mulDecimalInput_(qty, price, 6);
      total = addDecimalInput_(total, sub, 6);

      const itemObj = {
        product_id: pid,
        product_internal_id: productInternalId,
        name: pname,
        qty: qty,
        price: price,
        subtotal: sub,
        spec: (pidx.spec !== undefined) ? String(row[pidx.spec] || "") : "",
        unit: (pidx.unit !== undefined) ? String(row[pidx.unit] || "") : "",
        sku: (pidx.sku !== undefined) ? String(row[pidx.sku] || "") : ""
      };
      items.push(itemObj);

      purchaseItems.push({
        product_id: pid,
        product_internal_id: productInternalId,
        product_name: pname,
        qty: suggestedQty,
        qty_raw: formatPurchaseQtyText_(suggestedQty),
        suggested_qty: suggestedQty,
        customer_order_qty: qty,
        cost: "",
        cost_raw: "",
        supplier_id: supplier_id,
        supplier_name: supplier_name,
        spec: itemObj.spec,
        unit: itemObj.unit,
        sku: itemObj.sku,
        receive_date: deliveryDate,
        inspection_priority: "",
        receipt_weight: "",
        accept_weight: "",
        acceptance_result: "",
        pesticide_result: "",
        note: suggestedQty > 0 ? "" : "目前還有庫存"
      });
    }

    const orderItemsSh = getSheetByAnyName_(ss, ["orderItems"]);
    if (orderItemsSh) {
      ensureSheet_(ss, orderItemsSh.getName(), ["OrderItemID","OrderID","ProductID","Quantity","UnitPrice","Subtotal"]);
      const orderItemRows = items.map(function(it){
        return {
          OrderItemID: generateId_("OI"),
          OrderID: order_id,
          ProductID: it.product_id,
          Quantity: Number(it.qty),
          UnitPrice: Number(it.price),
          Subtotal: Number(it.subtotal)
        };
      });
      appendObjectsBatch_(orderItemsSh, orderItemRows);
    }

    const defaultFormInfo = getPurchaseFormInfoByCategory_(firstCategory);
    const purchaseGroups = buildPurchaseGroupsBySku_(purchaseItems, defaultFormInfo);
    const createdPurchaseIds = [];
    const purchaseRows = [];
    const purchaseHeaders = getHeaders_(purchasesSh);
    const purchaseIdx = headerIndexMap_(purchaseHeaders);
    const purchaseValues = purchasesSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    const orderLookup = buildOrderLookupMaps_(ss);
    const rowUpdates = {};

    let nextPoSeq = nextDailySequence_(purchasesSh, "po_id", now);
    purchaseGroups.forEach(function(group){
      const mergeHit = findMergeableGeneratedPurchaseRow_(ss, purchaseValues, purchaseHeaders, purchaseIdx, {
        form_no: group.form_no,
        date: orderDate,
        arrival_date: deliveryDate
      }, orderLookup);

      if (mergeHit && mergeHit.obj) {
        let existingItems = [];
        try { existingItems = JSON.parse(mergeHit.obj.items || "[]"); } catch (e) { existingItems = []; }
        if (!Array.isArray(existingItems)) existingItems = [];
        const mergedItems = mergePurchaseItemsForAutoAppend_(existingItems, group.items, deliveryDate);
        const headerSuppliers = summarizePurchaseHeaderSuppliers_(mergedItems);
        const sourceOrderIds = joinCsvIds_([
          mergeHit.obj.source_order_ids || mergeHit.obj.source_order_id || "",
          order_id
        ]);
        const updatedObj = Object.assign({}, mergeHit.obj, {
          po_id: String(mergeHit.obj.po_id || "").trim(),
          date: purchaseRowDateMetaFromObject_(ss, mergeHit.obj, orderLookup).date || orderDate,
          arrival_date: purchaseRowDateMetaFromObject_(ss, mergeHit.obj, orderLookup).arrival_date || deliveryDate,
          form_no: group.form_no,
          form_name: group.form_name,
          supplier_id: headerSuppliers.supplier_id,
          supplier_name: headerSuppliers.supplier_name,
          items: JSON.stringify(mergedItems),
          total: Number(mergeHit.obj.total || 0) || 0,
          status: "待驗收",
          source_order_id: splitCsvIds_(sourceOrderIds)[0] || order_id,
          source_order_ids: sourceOrderIds,
          stock_applied: 0,
          auto_generated: 1
        });
        rowUpdates[mergeHit.rowNumber] = updatedObj;
        purchaseValues[mergeHit.rowIndex] = purchaseRowArrayFromObject_(purchaseHeaders, updatedObj);
        createdPurchaseIds.push(updatedObj.po_id);
        return;
      }

      const po_id = String(dailyDocNoFromSeq_(now, nextPoSeq++)).trim();
      createdPurchaseIds.push(po_id);
      purchaseRows.push({
        po_id: po_id,
        date: orderDate,
        arrival_date: deliveryDate,
        form_no: group.form_no,
        form_name: group.form_name,
        supplier_id: (function(){ const ids = Array.from(new Set(group.items.map(function(x){ return String(x.supplier_id || "").trim(); }).filter(Boolean))); return ids.length === 1 ? ids[0] : "MULTI"; })(),
        supplier_name: (function(){ const names = Array.from(new Set(group.items.map(function(x){ return String(x.supplier_name || "").trim(); }).filter(Boolean))); return names.length === 1 ? names[0] : "多供應商"; })(),
        total: 0,
        items: JSON.stringify(group.items),
        expiry_date: "",
        status: "待驗收",
        operator: "",
        created_at: now,
        source_order_id: order_id,
        source_order_ids: order_id,
        stock_applied: 0,
        completed_at: "",
        auto_generated: 1
      });
    });

    const linkedPurchaseIdText = joinCsvIds_(createdPurchaseIds);
    const orderObj = {
      order_id: order_id,
      name: name,
      phone: phone,
      address: address,
      shipping_date: deliveryDate,
      total: total,
      items: JSON.stringify(items),
      date: orderDate,
      member_id: member_id,
      status: "待出貨",
      operator: "",
      source: "EC",
      stock_applied: 0,
      linked_purchase_id: linkedPurchaseIdText,
      request_token: request_token,
      remark: "",
      created_at: now
    };

    appendObject_(ordersSh, orderObj);

    Object.keys(rowUpdates).map(function(v){ return Number(v); }).sort(function(a, b){ return a - b; }).forEach(function(rowNumber){
      setSheetRowByObject_(purchasesSh, rowNumber, rowUpdates[rowNumber]);
    });
    if (purchaseRows.length) appendObjectsBatch_(purchasesSh, purchaseRows);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (eUnlock) {}

    const linePushResult = sendLineOrderCreatedPush_(ss, orderObj, items, { source_label: "電商訂單" });
    return respond({ status: "ok", message: "下單成功", order_id: order_id, linked_purchase_id: linkedPurchaseIdText, total: total, line_push: linePushResult });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
  }
}

function apiOrderStatusByToken_(p, respond) {
  const request_token = String(p.request_token || "").trim();
  if (!request_token) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 request_token" });
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ensureOrdersSheet_(ss);
  const hit = findOrderByRequestToken_(readObjects_(sh), request_token);
  if (!hit) return respond({ status: "pending", message: "訂單建立中" });
  return respond({ status: "ok", order_id: String(hit.order_id || "").trim(), total: Number(hit.total || 0), linked_purchase_id: String(hit.linked_purchase_id || "").trim() });
}

function apiMyOrders_(p, respond) {
  const member_id = String(p.member_id || "").trim();
  if (!member_id) return respond({ status: "error", message: "缺少 member_id" });
